Kosakata baru (4): `straight` = lurus | `turn` = belok | `far` = jauh | `map` = peta
Tips praktis: Jelaskan rute kos-kampusmu dalam Inggris 4 kalimat.
Kuis:
1. ___ me, where is the pharmacy? | A) Sorry B) Excuse C) Hallo D) Hey | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Excuse me yang sopan untuk membuka pertanyaan arah.
2. I'd like artinya ___. | A) aku mau (sopan) B) aku ingin marah C) aku suka D) aku benci | Jawaban: A | Alasan: I'd like adalah bentuk sopan dari I want.
3. Go straight artinya ___. | A) belok kiri B) putar balik C) jalan lurus D) berhenti | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Straight berarti lurus.

### en45 · A2 9/16 · Seberapa Sering? Adverbs of Frequency & Manner
- Deskripsi: Taruh always/usually/quickly di posisi benar biar cerita rutinitasmu natural.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: Always, Usually, Sometimes di Tengah
Penjelasan: Aturannya: adverb di antara subjek dan kata kerja — I always pray, she usually cooks. Kalau ada to be, adverb setelahnya — I am always late. Jangan taruh di belakang (I pray always) kecuali penekanan khusus.

Urutan frekuensi: always 100%, usually 80%, often 50%, sometimes 30%, rarely 5%, never 0%. Hafalkan tangga ini, kamu bisa jawab how often dengan presisi.

Orang Indonesia sering every day always double — pilih satu. I always go every day itu redundant. I go every day ATAU I always go. Hemat kata, hemat napas.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I usually wake up at five for Subuh.` — Aku biasanya bangun jam 5 untuk Subuh. | `She is often late because of traffic.` — Dia sering telat karena macet. | `We never smoke, it's haram and pricey.` — Kami tidak pernah merokok, haram dan mahal.
Kosakata baru (4): `usually` = biasanya | `often` = sering | `rarely` = jarang | `never` = tidak pernah
Tips praktis: Tulis rutinitasmu 5 baris pakai 5 adverb beda.

#### Bagian 2: Cepat vs Dengan Cepat: Manner -ly
Penjelasan: Kata sifat + ly jadi keterangan cara: quick→quickly, slow→slowly, careful→carefully. He drives carefully, she speaks softly. Cara-nya di belakang kata kerja.

Jebakan: fast, hard, late tidak tambah ly (fastly itu salah!). He runs fast, study hard, wake up late. Hafalkan tiga bandel ini biar nggak kejebak.

Bedakan good (sifat) vs well (cara): She is good, she sings well. Good singer = sifat orangnya, sings well = cara nyanyinya. Satu pasang ini keluar terus di ujian.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Please speak slowly, I'm still learning.` — Tolong bicara pelan, aku masih belajar. | `He finished the task quickly and neatly.` — Dia selesaikan tugas cepat dan rapi. | `My mother cooks well, everyone loves it.` — Ibuku masak enak, semua suka.
Kosakata baru (4): `quickly` = dengan cepat | `slowly` = dengan pelan | `carefully` = hati-hati | `well` = dengan baik
Tips praktis: Ubah 5 sifat di notes-mu jadi -ly hari ini.

#### Bagian 3: Cerita Rutinitas 1 Menit
Penjelasan: Gabungkan: I always wake at 5, I usually jog, I sometimes skip breakfast (bad!). Tiga frekuensi, satu cerita jujur. Tambahkan manner: I eat quickly because class is early. Langsung hidup.

Kesalahan umum: menaruh adverb di depan semua (Always I go...) — boleh untuk penekanan sastra, tapi di lisan sehari-hari taruh tengah. Natural itu tengah, bukan depan.

Tutup dengan kejujuran: I'm trying to sleep early, but I rarely succeed. Trying + rarely = relatable. Penguji dan teman sama-sama senyum.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I always brush my teeth twice a day.` — Aku selalu gosok gigi dua kali sehari. | `Buses rarely arrive on time here.` — Bus jarang tepat waktu di sini. | `Study hard but rest well too.` — Belajar giat tapi istirahat baik juga.
Kosakata baru (4): `routine` = rutinitas | `succeed` = berhasil | `try` = mencoba | `early` = awal
Tips praktis: Ceritakan harimu kemarin 5 kalimat adverb beda.
Kuis:
1. She ___ cooks rendang on Fridays. | A) usually B) usually cooks rarely C) cooks usually D) usual | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Adverb di antara subjek dan kata kerja.
2. He drives ___. | A) careful B) carefully C) care D) carefuls | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Keterangan cara memakai -ly.
3. Pilih kalimat benar: ___. | A) He runs fastly B) He runs fast C) He runs good D) He run fast | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Fast tanpa -ly, good jadi well untuk cara.

### en46 · A2 10/16 · Berapa Banyak? Much/Many/A Lot + How Much/Many
- Deskripsi: Tanya harga, porsi, dan stok dengan much/many/a lot yang tidak tertukar.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: Many Buat Bisa Dihitung, Much Buat Tidak
Penjelasan: Many + countable jamak (many chilies, many friends), much + uncountable (much sugar, much traffic). A lot of/lots of bisa dua-duanya di positif. Simple kan?

Orang Indonesia sering many money, much people — ketukar. Trik: bisa taruh angka? many. Nggak bisa? much. Many cars vs much pollution. Tes angka nggak pernah gagal.

Di negatif/tanya, much/many oke: I don't have much time, Do you have many cousins? Di positif, a lot of lebih natural: I have a lot of work (bukan much work yang kaku formal).

Contoh (3 kalimat): `How many eggs do we need?` — Butuh berapa telur? | `We don't have much oil left.` — Minyak tersisa tidak banyak. | `There are a lot of mosquitoes tonight!` — Banyak nyamuk malam ini!
Kosakata baru (4): `many` = banyak (countable) | `much` = banyak (uncountable) | `mosquito` = nyamuk | `left` = tersisa
Tips praktis: Buka kulkasmu dan tulis 3 many + 3 much.

#### Bagian 2: How Much Harganya? How Many Jumlahnya?
Penjelasan: How much + harga/uncountable (How much is this? How much rice?), How many + countable (How many tickets?). How much harganya vs how many jumlahnya. Beda satu kata, beda jawaban.

Untuk harga, jawab: It's 15k / They are 20k each. Each = per biji, penting saat beli buah. Jangan jawab fifteen thousand saja tanpa it is — kurang lengkap.

Kesalahan umum: How much people? harus How many people. People itu countable (persons), walau nggak ada -s. Hafalkan people = many.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `How much are these rambutans?` — Berapa rambutan ini? | `How many kilos do you want?` — Mau berapa kilo? | `How much sugar do you take?` — Gulamu seberapa banyak?
Kosakata baru (4): `how much` = berapa (harga/uncountable) | `how many` = berapa (countable) | `each` = tiap/per | `kilo` = kilo
Tips praktis: Tanya harga 3 barang di pasar khayalan Inggris.

#### Bagian 3: A Few vs A Little, Sedikit Tapi Beda
Penjelasan: A few + countable (a few friends = lumayan ada teman), a little + uncountable (a little money = lumayan ada duit). Tanpa a (few/little) artinya hampir tidak ada — negatif! Few friends = kesepian, a few friends = cukup bersyukur.

Ini jebakan ujian favorit: Only a little vs little. I have little time (hampir tidak ada, sedih) vs I have a little time (ada dikit, masih bisa). Satu a beda nasib.

Latihan pasar: I need a few chilies and a little salt. Dua takaran, satu kalimat belanja. Praktis banget.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I have a few close friends here.` — Aku punya beberapa teman dekat di sini. | `Add a little salt, not too much.` — Tambahkan sedikit garam, jangan kebanyakan. | `We have little water left, buy more!` — Air tersisa sedikit banget, beli lagi!
Kosakata baru (4): `a few` = beberapa (countable) | `a little` = sedikit (uncountable) | `salt` = garam | `close` = dekat (hubungan)
Tips praktis: Tulis stok dapurmu 4 baris few/little.
Kuis:
1. How ___ sugar do we have? | A) many B) much C) a few D) many much | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Sugar uncountable memakai much.
2. I have ___ friends, I'm happy. | A) few B) little C) a few D) a little | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Countable positif sedikit memakai a few.
3. ___ money do you need? (uang = uncountable) | A) How many B) How much C) How few D) How little | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Uncountable memakai how much.

### en47 · A2 11/16 · Masuk, Keluar, Lewat: Prepositions of Movement
- Deskripsi: Ceritakan rute ojek dan mudik dengan go to, into, across, past yang akurat.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: Go To, Go Home, Go There Tanpa Bingung
Penjelasan: Go to + tempat (go to school, go to the market), go home tanpa to, go there/here tanpa to. Home/there/here itu adverb, nggak butuh to. Go to home itu salah, go home yang benar.

Orang Indonesia sering arrive to Jakarta — harus arrive in (kota/negara) atau arrive at (titik kecil). Arrive tidak pakai to sama sekali. Hafalkan arrive in/at.

Get juga beda: get to (tiba), get on/off (naik/turun bus), get in/out of (masuk/keluar mobil). Get itu serbaguna, pasangannya yang menentukan arti.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I go to campus by bus every day.` — Aku pergi kampus naik bus tiap hari. | `We arrived in Solo late last night.` — Kami tiba di Solo larut semalam. | `Get off at the second stop.` — Turun di halte kedua.
Kosakata baru (4): `arrive` = tiba | `get off` = turun (kendaraan) | `stop` = halte | `late` = terlambat
Tips praktis: Tulis rute kos-kampusmu 4 langkah movement.

#### Bagian 2: Into, Out Of, Through, Across
Penjelasan: Bayangin film: go into (masuk ke dalam), go out of (keluar), go through (lewat dalam terowongan), go across (menyeberang permukaan), go past (lewat tanpa berhenti), go over (lewat atas jembatan). Visual = hafal.

Jangan bilang cross across the road — cross sudah kata kerja (cross the road) ATAU go across the road. Pilih satu paket, jangan double. Cross across itu double, salah.

Orang Indonesia suka into/to ketukar: go into the room (masuk fisik ke dalam) vs go to the room (pergi ke arahnya). Into ada adegan masuknya, to cuma arah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The cat ran into the kitchen.` — Kucing lari masuk dapur. | `Walk across the bridge then past the mosque.` — Jalan seberangi jembatan lalu lewati masjid. | `The bus went through a dark tunnel.` — Bus lewat terowongan gelap.
Kosakata baru (4): `into` = masuk ke dalam | `across` = menyeberang | `through` = lewat dalam | `past` = melewati
Tips praktis: Ceritakan rute mudikmu pakai 3 preposisi gerak.

#### Bagian 3: Naik-Turun: On/Off, In/Out Of
Penjelasan: Kendaraan besar (bisa berdiri): get on/off the bus/train/plane. Kecil (jongkok masuk): get in/out of the car/taxi. On = naik panggung besar, in = masuk kotak kecil. Gampang ingat kan?

Untuk lantai: go up/down the stairs, take the elevator to the third floor. Up naik, down turun. Jangan go to up — to-nya buang kalau sudah ada up/down.

Latihan ojek: Get on near the gate, get off past the minimarket, pay 10k. Tiga perintah, driver langsung paham walau bule.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Get in the car, it's raining!` — Masuk mobil, hujan! | `She got out of the taxi in a hurry.` — Dia keluar taksi buru-buru. | `Go up the stairs to the second floor.` — Naik tangga ke lantai dua.
Kosakata baru (4): `get on` = naik (bus/kereta) | `get in` = masuk (mobil) | `stairs` = tangga | `hurry` = buru-buru
Tips praktis: Praktikkan get on/off/in/out besok saat naik kendaraan.
Kuis:
1. I go ___ home now. | A) to B) at C) - (tanpa to) D) into | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Go home tanpa to karena home adverb.
2. Walk ___ the street at the zebra cross. | A) across B) through C) into D) out of | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Menyeberang permukaan memakai across.
3. Get ___ the bus at the next shelter. | A) in B) on C) off D) out | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Turun bus memakai get off (naiknya get on).

### en48 · A2 12/16 · Dulu Begini: Used To for Past Habits
- Deskripsi: Ceritakan masa kecil dan kebiasaan lama yang sudah berubah dengan used to.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: Used To = Dulu Sering, Kini Tidak
Penjelasan: Rumusnya used to + kata kerja dasar: I used to swim daily (dulu tiap hari, kini tidak). Beda dengan past simple yang cuma bilang kejadian, used to tekankan sudah berubah/berhenti. Ada rasa nostalgia.

Orang Indonesia sering I am used to swim untuk masa lalu — salah besar. I am used to + V-ing artinya terbiasa (kini). I used to + V1 artinya dulu. Beda be + V-ing vs polos, beda arti jauh.

Ejaan: used (bukan use) di positif. I used to play — pakai d. Jangan I use to play. Kecil tapi dilirik penguji.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I used to play layangan every afternoon.` — Dulu aku main layangan tiap sore. | `She used to live in a village.` — Dulu dia tinggal di desa. | `We used to eat together every night.` — Dulu kami makan bersama tiap malam.
Kosakata baru (4): `used to` = dulu (kebiasaan lama) | `layangan` = layang-layang | `village` = desa | `together` = bersama
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 used to tentang masa kecilmu.

#### Bagian 2: Be Used To + V-ing = Terbiasa Kini
Penjelasan: Kalau sudah terbiasa kini: I am used to spicy food / I am used to waking early. Rumusnya be + used to + V-ing/benda. Artinya adaptasi sukses, bukan nostalgia.

Jebakan ujian: I am used to wake vs waking — wajib waking. To di sini preposisi, bukan infinitive. Jadi setelahnya -ing. Hafalkan: be used to + -ing selamanya.

Negatif/tanya: I'm not used to cold weather, Are you used to night shifts? Praktis buat anak rantau: I'm not used to the food yet. Jujur dan grammar benar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I'm used to traffic jams now.` — Aku sudah terbiasa macet kini. | `Are you used to sleeping late?` — Apakah kamu terbiasa begadang? | `He isn't used to eating with a spoon.` — Dia belum terbiasa makan pakai sendok.
Kosakata baru (4): `be used to` = terbiasa | `yet` = belum | `shift` = sif/giliran | `spoon` = sendok
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 hal yang sudah/ belum terbiasa versimu.

#### Bagian 3: Dulu vs Kini Biar Haru
Penjelasan: Gabungkan kontras: I used to walk to school, now I ride a motorbike. Dulu vs kini satu kalimat, cerita langsung ada arc-nya. Did you use to...? buat tanya masa lalu teman. Seru!

Untuk negatif dulu: I didn't use to like coffee (tanpa d di use karena ada did!). Did sudah bawa past, use kembali polos. Sama kayak does + like tanpa s. Satu masa lalu cukup.

Tanya: Did you use to play gobak sodor? Jawab: Yes, I did / No, I didn't. Nostalgia 5 menit, Inggris jalan terus.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Did you use to swim in the river?` — Apakah dulu kamu berenang di sungai? | `Life used to be slower and cheaper.` — Hidup dulu lebih pelan dan murah. | `Now I am used to city noise.` — Kini aku terbiasa bising kota.
Kosakata baru (4): `river` = sungai | `slower` = lebih pelan | `noise` = kebisingan | `now` = kini
Tips praktis: Wawancara orang tuamu: What did you use to do?
Kuis:
1. I ___ play congklak daily. (dulu) | A) used to B) am used to C) use to D) using to | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Kebiasaan lama memakai used to + V1.
2. I'm used to ___ early. | A) wake B) waking C) wakes D) waked | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Be used to memakai V-ing.
3. Did you ___ swim here? | A) used to B) use to C) using to D) uses to | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Setelah did, use tanpa d.

### en49 · A2 13/16 · Sambung Kata: And/But/Because/So
- Deskripsi: Gabung kalimat pendek jadi cerita mengalir dengan konjungsi paling kepakai.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: And, But, Or Buat Sejajar
Penjelasan: And nambah (rice and eggs), but kontras (cheap but tasty), or pilihan (tea or coffee), so akibat (rain, so stay). Empat ini saja sudah sambung 80% kalimat A2-mu.

Komanya: dua klausa penuh pakai koma sebelum and/but/so — I was hungry, so I ate. Kalau cuma dua kata (rice and eggs) tanpa koma. Koma sebelum konjungsi itu sinyal klausa baru.

Orang Indonesia suka but di awal tiap kalimat lisan — di tulis formal jangan. But di tengah lebih rapi. Lisan santai oke, tulis rapi tengah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I bought rice and eggs at the market.` — Aku beli beras dan telur di pasar. | `The room is small but comfortable.` — Kamarnya kecil tapi nyaman. | `It was late, so we took a taxi.` — Sudah larut, jadi kami naik taksi.
Kosakata baru (4): `and` = dan | `but` = tapi | `so` = jadi/maka | `comfortable` = nyaman
Tips praktis: Gabung 5 pasang kalimat pendekmu pakai and/but/so.

#### Bagian 2: Because vs So Jangan Dobel
Penjelasan: Because alasan (I stayed because rain), so akibat (Rain, so I stayed). Jangan pakai dua-duanya: Because rain so I stayed itu double, salah. Pilih satu sudut: sebab ATAU akibat.

Posisi because fleksibel: Because I was sick, I rested ATAU I rested because I was sick. Koma hanya kalau because di depan. Hafalkan pola koma ini.

Because of + benda (because of rain), because + kalimat (because it rained). Of butuh benda, tanpa of butuh subjek + verb. Ketukar ini favorit soal.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `We stayed home because it flooded.` — Kami di rumah karena banjir. | `Because of the flood, school was off.` — Karena banjir, sekolah libur. | `I was hungry, so I cooked noodles.` — Aku lapar, jadi masak mie.
Kosakata baru (4): `because` = karena | `flood` = banjir | `hungry` = lapar | `off` = libur/mati
Tips praktis: Ubah 3 kalimat so-mu jadi because dan sebaliknya.

#### Bagian 3: Cerita 5 Kalimat Mengalir
Penjelasan: Saatnya nulis mini story 5 kalimat: pagi (and), masalah (but), alasan (because), akibat (so), penutup. Yesterday I woke late and missed the bus. The ojek was pricey but fast. I hurried because exam at 8, so I ran. I made it! Runtut kan?

Orang Indonesia sering kalimatnya putus-putus semua pendek. Sambung dua-tiga jadi satu pakai konjungsi, tulisanmu langsung naik level tanpa vocab sulit.

Bacakan keras-keras: kalau napasmu habis, kepanjangan. Pecah dua. Kalau terlalu patah, sambung. Telinga adalah editor terbaik A2.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I wanted coffee and tea, but I chose tea.` — Aku mau kopi dan teh, tapi pilih teh. | `She studied hard because she wants a scholarship.` — Dia belajar giat karena mau beasiswa. | `It's cheap and tasty, so I buy it daily.` — Murah dan enak, jadi kubeli tiap hari.
Kosakata baru (4): `choose - chose` = memilih | `scholarship` = beasiswa | `daily` = harian | `story` = cerita
Tips praktis: Tulis diary 5 kalimat tersambung malam ini.
Kuis:
1. I stayed ___ it rained. | A) so B) because C) but D) or | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Alasan memakai because.
2. It rained, ___ we stayed. | A) because B) because of C) so D) but | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Akibat memakai so.
3. ___ the rain, we stayed home. | A) Because B) Because of C) So D) But | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Because of + kata benda (rain).

### en50 · A2 14/16 · Telepon & Minta Tolong: Can/Could/Would
- Deskripsi: Nelpon, minta tolong, dan janji ketemuan dengan bahasa sopan tanpa grogi.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Conversation | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: Telepon: This Is..., May I Speak To...?
Penjelasan: Buka telepon: Hello, this is Rina. May I speak to Budi? Jawab: Speaking! / Hold on, please. Tutup: Thanks for calling, see you! Paket 4 langkah ini cukup buat 90% telepon A2.

Bedakan Can I (bolehkah aku, kasar dikit) vs Could I / May I (lebih sopan). Ke dosen pakai Could/May, ke teman Can oke. Level sopan = level aman.

Kalau sinyal jelek: Sorry, you are breaking up. Can you repeat? I'll call back. Tiga kalimat penyelamat pulsa dan harga diri.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Hello, may I speak to Mr. Joko?` — Halo, boleh bicara dengan Pak Joko? | `Hold on a second, please.` — Tunggu sebentar ya. | `Sorry, I can't hear you well.` — Maaf, aku kurang dengar jelas.
Kosakata baru (4): `hold on` = tunggu | `repeat` = mengulangi | `call back` = telepon balik | `breaking up` = terputus-putus
Tips praktis: Simulasi telepon 1 menit dengan temanmu.

#### Bagian 2: Minta Tolong: Could You...? Would You Mind...?
Penjelasan: Tangga sopan: Help me! (bos) → Can you help? (teman) → Could you help? (sopan) → Would you mind helping? (super sopan). Mind + -ing, jangan mind to help. Would you mind waiting? Jawab: Not at all! (santai, artinya tidak keberatan).

Orang Indonesia sering Would you mind to ... — to-nya buang, ganti -ing. Would you mind opening? Bukan to open. Hafalkan mind = -ing selamanya.

Tambahkan please dan thanks biar lengkap: Could you please send the file? Thank you so much! Please + thanks = SIM sopan santun.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Could you please speak slowly?` — Bisakah bicara pelan? | `Would you mind closing the door?` — Keberatan tutup pintu? | `Not at all! I'll do it now.` — Tidak keberatan! Kukerjakan kini.
Kosakata baru (4): `could` = bisakah (sopan) | `mind` = keberatan | `file` = berkas | `send` = mengirim
Tips praktis: Minta 2 tolong hari ini pakai Could/Would.

#### Bagian 3: Janjian: Are You Free...? How About...?
Penjelasan: Ngajak ketemu: Are you free tomorrow? How about 3 p.m. at the library? Setuju: Sounds good! / Perfect! Tawar: How about 4 instead? Tolak halus: Sorry, I'm busy. How about Friday? Ada alternatif, nggak PHP.

Konfirmasi H-1: Are we still on for tomorrow? Just to confirm, 3 p.m. right? Still on = masih jadi kan? Confirm = kunci biar nggak ghosting.

Waspada on/in/at waktu ketemuan: on Friday, at 3, in the afternoon. Paket komplit: at 3 on Friday afternoon. Tiga preposisi, satu janji rapi.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Are you free on Saturday morning?` — Apakah luang Sabtu pagi? | `How about meeting at the station?` — Bagaimana jika ketemu di stasiun? | `Are we still on for today?` — Apakah jadi untuk hari ini?
Kosakata baru (4): `free` = luang | `instead` = sebagai ganti | `confirm` = konfirmasi | `still on` = masih jadi
Tips praktis: Buat janji beneran 1x minggu ini pakai pola ini.
Kuis:
1. ___ I speak to Anna? (paling sopan) | A) Can B) May C) Must D) Should | Jawaban: B | Alasan: May paling formal untuk telepon.
2. Would you mind ___ the window? | A) open B) to open C) opening D) opened | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Mind diikuti V-ing.
3. Are we still ___ for tomorrow? | A) on B) in C) at D) off | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Still on berarti masih jadi.

### en51 · A2 15/16 · Badan Sehat: Should/Shouldn't + Health
- Deskripsi: Kasih saran kesehatan (batuk, begadang, jajan) dengan should yang peduli bukan menggurui.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Conversation | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: You Should Rest, Bukan You Must Rest
Penjelasan: Should itu saran peduli: You should see a doctor. Must itu perintah: You must finish! Buat teman sakit, should jauh lebih empati. Must ke teman sakit kesannya nyuruh-nyuruh.

Rumusnya should + kata kerja dasar: You should drink water. Negatif shouldn't + dasar: You shouldn't stay up. Jangan should to drink — to-nya buang.

Orang Indonesia sering Should you rest? urutan terbalik — yang benar Should I rest? Should di depan subjek. Should + subjek + verb, bukan subjek + should di tanya.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `You look pale. You should lie down.` — Kamu pucat. Sebaiknya rebahan. | `You shouldn't drink iced coffee now.` — Sebaiknya tidak minum es kopi kini. | `Should I take medicine?` — Sebaiknya aku minum obat?
Kosakata baru (4): `pale` = pucat | `lie down` = berbaring | `cough` = batuk | `fever` = demam
Tips praktis: Kasih 1 saran should ke teman yang kecapekan.

#### Bagian 2: Keluhan: I Have a Headache
Penjelasan: Bilang sakit: I have a headache/stomachache/toothache/cough/cold. Have a + sakit, bukan am. I am headache itu salah, I have a headache yang benar. Kecuali sick/dizzy pakai am: I am sick.

Di klinik: What's the problem? I feel dizzy and nauseous. Since when? Since yesterday. Template what-since ini yang dipakai suster beneran.

Jawab saran dokter: Take this twice a day after meals, avoid spicy food, get enough sleep. Twice a day = dua kali sehari, avoid = hindari. Catat biar nggak salah dosis.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I have a terrible toothache.` — Aku sakit gigi parah. | `I feel dizzy since this morning.` — Aku pusing sejak pagi ini. | `Get well soon, take care!` — Cepat sembuh, jaga diri!
Kosakata baru (4): `headache` = sakit kepala | `stomachache` = sakit perut | `dizzy` = pusing | `nauseous` = mual
Tips praktis: Hafalkan 5 have a + sakit favorit musim hujan.

#### Bagian 3: Gaya Hidup: Sleep, Water, Less Sugar
Penjelasan: Saran gaya hidup: You should sleep 7 hours, drink 8 glasses, eat less sugar. Angka + should = saran konkret, bukan omdo. Tambah because biar nancep: because lack of sleep ruins focus.

Larangan halus: You shouldn't skip breakfast, you shouldn't stare at phones till 2 a.m. Shouldn't di sini nasihat, bukan larangan keras mustn't. Beda rasa, beda hubungan.

Tutup dengan ajakan sehat: Let's jog together tomorrow morning! Saran + ajakan = peduli plus aksi. Temanmu susah nolak.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `You should drink more water, it's hot.` — Sebaiknya minum lebih banyak, panas. | `Don't skip meals, you'll get sick.` — Jangan lewati makan, kamu bisa sakit. | `Let's walk instead of ojek today!` — Jalan saja bukan ojek hari ini!
Kosakata baru (4): `sleep` = tidur | `meal` = makan (waktu makan) | `avoid` = hindari | `enough` = cukup
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 komitmen sehatmu pakai should.
Kuis:
1. You ___ see a doctor, you look sick. | A) must to B) should C) should to D) musts | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Saran memakai should + kata kerja dasar.
2. I ___ a headache. | A) am B) have C) has D) is | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Sakit headache memakai have a.
3. ___ I take a day off? | A) Should B) Do C) Am D) Have | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Tanya saran memakai Should di depan subjek.

### en52 · A2 16/16 · Diriku Sendiri: Reflexive & Object Pronouns
- Deskripsi: Bedakan me/myself, him/himself biar nggak bilang I cut me saat potong rambut.
- Durasi: 30 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: A2

#### Bagian 1: Me, Him, Us Buat Objek
Penjelasan: Subjek (I/you/he) di depan, objek (me/you/him/her/us/them) di belakang kata kerja/preposisi: Help me, call him, go with us, for her. Jangan I di belakang (help I itu salah).

Orang Indonesia sering between you and I — harus between you and me. Between itu preposisi, butuh objek me. I hanya di depan sebagai pelaku. Preposisi + me selamanya.

Its vs it: I love its color (milik) vs I love it (objeknya). Satu s beda fungsi. Cek: butuh milik atau objek?

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Please help me with this bag.` — Tolong bantu aku dengan tas ini. | `I called them twice but no answer.` — Aku telepon mereka dua kali tapi tak jawab. | `This gift is for you, not for him.` — Hadiah ini untukmu, bukan untuknya.
Kosakata baru (4): `me` = aku (objek) | `him` = dia lk (objek) | `them` = mereka (objek) | `gift` = hadiah
Tips praktis: Ubah 5 kalimat I/you-mu jadi objek me/you.

#### Bagian 2: Myself, Yourself Buat Sendiri
Penjelasan: Kalau pelaku = korban, pakai -self: I cut myself (kepotong sendiri), wash yourself, enjoy yourselves! Jangan I cut me — me itu orang lain, myself itu diri sendiri.

Daftar: myself, yourself, himself, herself, itself, ourselves, yourselves, themselves. Yourselves/themselves buat jamak. Ejaannya -selves bukan -selfs.

By + -self = sendirian: I live by myself = aku tinggal sendiri. All by myself = sendirian total. Frasa galau favorit lagu, kini kamu tahu grammar-nya.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I taught myself to cook indomie.` — Aku belajar sendiri masak indomie. | `Be careful, don't hurt yourself!` — Hati-hati, jangan lukai dirimu! | `They built the stall themselves.` — Mereka bangun warung itu sendiri.
Kosakata baru (4): `myself` = diriku sendiri | `yourself` = dirimu sendiri | `themselves` = mereka sendiri | `hurt` = melukai
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 hal yang kamu lakukan sendiri pakai -self.

#### Bagian 3: Each Other Biar Nggak Berantem
Penjelasan: Untuk saling: love each other, help each other, text each other. Each other buat dua/kelompok kecil, one another buat kelompok besar (formal). A2 cukup each other dulu.

Bedakan themselves vs each other: They hurt themselves (luka sendiri-sendiri) vs They hurt each other (saling melukai, berantem!). Beda arti jauh, jangan ketukar saat lapor guru.

Latihan kos: We respect each other, we share food, we clean together. Tiga each other/together bikin aturan kos adem. Tempel di pintu!

Contoh (3 kalimat): `We help each other with homework.` — Kami saling bantu PR. | `They love each other very much.` — Mereka saling cinta. | `Call each other if lost, okay?` — Saling telepon jika tersesat ya?
Kosakata baru (4): `each other` = saling/satu sama lain | `respect` = menghormati | `share` = berbagi | `lost` = tersesat
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 aturan saling versi kosmu.
Kuis:
1. I cut ___ while cooking. | A) me B) myself C) I D) mine | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Pelaku = korban memakai reflexive myself.
2. Between you and ___, this is secret. | A) I B) me C) myself D) mine | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Setelah preposisi memakai objek me.
3. They love ___ . (saling) | A) themselves B) each other C) them D) theirs | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Aksi saling memakai each other.

### Level B1

### en17 · B1 1/12 · Sudah vs Kemarin: Present Perfect vs Past Simple
- Deskripsi: Kunci beda have gone dan went biar ceritamu nggak dicap campur aduk.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Ada Waktu Spesifik? Past Simple. Nggak Ada? Perfect.
Penjelasan: Ini pembeda sejuta umat: "I visited Bali in 2022" (past, ada tahunnya) vs "I have visited Bali" (perfect, pengalaman saja). Satu ada cap tanggal, satu nggak.

Orang Indonesia hobi bilang "I have visited Bali last year" — tabrakan. Last year itu spesifik, jadi buang have: I visited Bali last year. Perfect alergi sama yesterday, last ..., in 2019.

Tanya yang mancing: "Have you eaten?" (sampai sekarang) vs "Did you eat at noon?" (siang tadi). Beda jam, beda tense.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I lost my wallet yesterday.` — Aku kehilangan dompetku kemarin. | `I have lost my wallet, I can't pay now.` — Aku kehilangan dompetku, aku tidak bisa bayar sekarang. | `She finished her thesis in June.` — Dia menyelesaikan skripsinya bulan Juni.
Kosakata baru (4): `lose` = kehilangan | `wallet` = dompet | `thesis` = skripsi | `pay` = membayar
Tips praktis: Setiap nulis have, cek: ada keterangan lampau spesifik? Kalau ada, hapus have.

#### Bagian 2: For dan Since, Duo Durasi
Penjelasan: For + durasi (for three years), since + titik awal (since 2020). "I have lived here for five years / since 2020." Dua-duanya perfect karena masih berlangsung sampai sekarang.

Jebakan: "I live here since 2020" — kurang have. Since itu bendera perfect, wajib have/has di depannya. Tanpa have kalimatmu pincang.

Bedakan dengan ago yang temannya past simple: "I moved here five years ago". Ago = past, since/for = perfect. Jangan tukar pasangannya.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `We have known each other since high school.` — Kami sudah saling kenal sejak SMA. | `He has worked here for a decade.` — Dia sudah bekerja di sini selama satu dekade. | `They moved here two years ago.` — Mereka pindah ke sini dua tahun lalu.
Kosakata baru (4): `since` = sejak | `decade` = dekade | `move` = pindah | `each other` = satu sama lain
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 kalimat for dan 2 since tentang hidupmu.

#### Bagian 3: Berita dan Cerita Fresh
Penjelasan: Present perfect itu langganan berita: "There has been an accident". Kejadiannya baru, efeknya masih kerasa. Lalu detailnya pakai past simple: "It happened at 7".

Pola berita: pembuka perfect, isi past. "A fire has broken out in the market. Five stalls burned down." Sekali paham pola ini, baca berita Inggris jadi enteng.

Latihan: ubah kabar kosan jadi breaking news Inggris: "My motorbike has disappeared! I parked it outside last night." Dramatis dan grammar-nya benar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `There has been a flood in Semarang.` — Telah terjadi banjir di Semarang. | `The water damaged many houses.` — Air merusak banyak rumah. | `Residents have moved to shelters.` — Warga telah pindah ke pengungsian.
Kosakata baru (4): `flood` = banjir | `damage` = merusak | `resident` = warga | `shelter` = pengungsian
Tips praktis: Ubah satu kabar hari ini jadi mini berita 3 kalimat.
Kuis:
1. I ___ my keys last night. | A) have lost B) lost C) have lose D) lose | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Keterangan last night memakai past simple.
2. We have lived here ___ 2018. | A) for B) since C) ago D) at | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Titik awal tahun memakai since.
3. She has worked here ___ five years. | A) since B) ago C) for D) in | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Durasi memakai for.

### en18 · B1 2/12 · Kalau Hujan, Aku Bawa Payung: Conditional 1
- Deskripsi: Kuasai if + will buat janji bersyarat dan aturan main yang realistis.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: If Present, Will Future
Penjelasan: Conditional 1 itu rumus harapan realistis: If + present simple, will + kata kerja. "If it rains, I will stay home." Syaratnya sekarang, hasilnya nanti.

Kesalahan abadi orang Indonesia: "If it will rain" — will jangan masuk klausa if. If-nya present saja, will-nya di hasil. Satu will cukup, jangan kembar.

Koma juga ada aturannya: If di depan pakai koma, will di depan tanpa koma. "I will stay home if it rains." Kecil tapi dilirik penguji.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If you study hard, you will pass.` — Jika kamu belajar giat, kamu akan lulus. | `We will cancel if the storm comes.` — Kami akan batal jika badai datang. | `If she calls, tell her I'm busy.` — Jika dia menelepon, katakan aku sibuk.
Kosakata baru (4): `study` = belajar | `pass` = lulus | `cancel` = membatalkan | `storm` = badai
Tips praktis: Buat 3 janji bersyarat tentang minggu ujianmu.

#### Bagian 2: Unless, As Long As, Biar Nggak Monoton
Penjelasan: Unless = if not: "Unless you hurry, you'll be late". Lebih singkat dan keren. As long as = selama/selagi syarat dipenuhi: "You can borrow it as long as you return it".

Jangan gabung not dengan unless: "Unless you don't hurry" itu double negatif, salah. Unless sudah mengandung not di dalamnya.

Pakai imperative juga bisa: "Take an umbrella if it looks cloudy". Conditional nggak melulu will, bisa perintah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Unless you save money, you can't buy it.` — Kecuali kamu menabung, kamu tidak bisa membelinya. | `You can stay as long as you keep quiet.` — Kamu boleh tinggal selama kamu tenang. | `Drink water if you feel dizzy.` — Minum air jika kamu pusing.
Kosakata baru (4): `unless` = kecuali jika | `save` = menabung | `quiet` = tenang | `dizzy` = pusing
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 aturan rumahmu jadi kalimat unless.

#### Bagian 3: Negosiasi dan Aturan Main
Penjelasan: Conditional 1 itu bahasa negosiasi: "If you pay cash, I'll give a discount". Syarat jelas, untung jelas. Kepakai di kos, kantor, pasar.

Orang Indonesia sering pakai kalau untuk semua: "If I will have money..." — lagi-lagi will bocor ke if. Jaga gawang if-mu tetap present.

Tulis kontrak mini dengan teman: If you cook, I will wash. Tempel di kulkas. Grammar kepakai, kerjaan beres.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If you finish early, we will watch a movie.` — Jika kamu selesai cepat, kita akan nonton film. | `I will lend you my notes if you need them.` — Aku akan meminjamkan catatanku jika kamu butuh. | `If the WiFi dies again, I'll move rooms.` — Jika WiFi mati lagi, aku akan pindah kamar.
Kosakata baru (4): `finish` = selesai | `lend` = meminjamkan | `need` = butuh | `move` = pindah
Tips praktis: Tulis satu negosiasi kosanmu pakai if hari ini.
Kuis:
1. If it rains, we ___ at home. | A) stay B) will stay C) stayed D) staying | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Hasil conditional 1 memakai will + kata kerja dasar.
2. ___ you hurry, you'll miss the bus. | A) If B) Unless C) When D) While | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Unless berarti if not, cocok untuk peringatan.
3. If you ___ now, you will catch the train. | A) will leave B) leave C) left D) leaving | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Klausa if memakai present simple tanpa will.

### en19 · B1 3/12 · Dibangun, Dicuri, Diantar: Passive Voice Dasar
- Deskripsi: Ubah fokus dari pelaku ke korban/aksi dengan be + V3 yang rapi.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Be + V3, Pelaku Boleh Minggir
Penjelasan: Passive itu kalau objek lebih penting dari pelaku: "My phone was stolen" (yang penting HP-ku, bukan siapa malingnya). Rumusnya be + V3, be-nya ikut tense.

Orang Indonesia kebawa "di-" lalu lupa be: "My phone stolen yesterday" — harus was stolen. Di- saja nggak cukup, be itu wajib.

Pakai by hanya kalau pelakunya penting: "Built by Habibie". Kalau nggak penting, buang saja. "Rice is grown in Java" sudah jelas tanpa by.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `This temple was built in the 9th century.` — Candi ini dibangun abad ke-9. | `Nasi padang is sold everywhere.` — Nasi padang dijual di mana-mana. | `The road is being repaired now.` — Jalannya sedang diperbaiki sekarang.
Kosakata baru (4): `build` = membangun | `sell` = menjual | `repair` = memperbaiki | `everywhere` = di mana-mana
Tips praktis: Ubah 3 berita di- menjadi passive Inggris.

#### Bagian 2: Tense Ikut Be-nya
Penjelasan: V3-nya awet, be-nya yang ganti baju: is built (sekarang), was built (lampau), will be built (nanti), has been built (baru saja). Hafalkan pola be-nya, V3 tinggal tempel.

Jebakan: "It has built" vs "It has been built" — kurang been bikin artinya aktif (ia membangun) bukan pasif (ia dibangun). Been itu nyawa perfect passive.

Untuk continuous passive: is being repaired — being jangan hilang. Memang panjang, tapi itu harga formalitas.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The new bridge will be opened next month.` — Jembatan baru akan dibuka bulan depan. | `Two phones have been stolen today.` — Dua HP telah dicuri hari ini. | `The report was written by my senior.` — Laporan ditulis oleh seniorku.
Kosakata baru (4): `bridge` = jembatan | `open` = membuka | `report` = laporan | `senior` = senior
Tips praktis: Conjugate satu kata: is/was/will be/has been + built.

#### Bagian 3: Kapan Passive Lebih Keren dari Active?
Penjelasan: Pakai passive buat formalitas: laporan, berita, prosedur. "The samples were tested" kedengeran ilmiah, "We tested the samples" kedengeran curhat. Pilih sesuai panggung.

Tapi jangan passive melulu — tulisan jadi lemas. Aturan praktis: kalau pelakunya jelas dan penting (I, my team), active saja. Kalau misterius/umum, passive.

Latihan: ubah resep jadi prosedur passive: "The chilies are ground, the coconut is grated". Langsung kerasa kayak buku masak profesional.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `First, the rice is washed twice.` — Pertama, beras dicuci dua kali. | `Applicants are asked to bring ID.` — Pelamar diminta membawa KTP. | `He was born in Medan.` — Dia lahir di Medan.
Kosakata baru (4): `wash` = mencuci | `applicant` = pelamar | `bring` = membawa | `born` = lahir
Tips praktis: Ubah satu prosedur (resep/manual) jadi passive.
Kuis:
1. My bike ___ last week. | A) stole B) stolen C) was stolen D) is steal | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Past passive memakai was + V3.
2. The room ___ cleaned every day. | A) is cleaned B) cleans C) cleaned D) is clean | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Kebiasaan passive memakai is + V3.
3. A new mall will ___ next year. | A) be built B) built C) building D) been built | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Future passive memakai will be + V3.

### en20 · B1 4/12 · Katanya Sih: Reported Speech Dasar
- Deskripsi: Sampaikan ulang omongan orang dengan perubahan tense dan kata ganti yang benar.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Mundur Satu Tense Kalau Ngutip Lampau
Penjelasan: Prinsipnya kayak parkir mundur: present→past, past→past perfect, will→would. "I am busy" jadi "He said he was busy". Karena ngutipnya sudah lewat.

Orang Indonesia sering nggak mundurin: "He said he is busy" padahal ngomongnya kemarin. Kalau reporting verb-nya past (said), isi kutipannya wajib mundur.

Pengecualian: fakta abadi nggak mundur — "The earth is round" tetap is. Fakta nggak kenal waktu.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Direct: "I like coffee." Reported: He said he liked coffee.` — Langsung: Aku suka kopi. Tak langsung: Dia bilang dia suka kopi. | `Direct: "We will come." Reported: They said they would come.` — Langsung: Kami akan datang. Tak langsung: Mereka bilang akan datang. | `Direct: "I bought it." Reported: She said she had bought it.` — Langsung: Aku membelinya. Tak langsung: Dia bilang sudah membelinya.
Kosakata baru (4): `say` = berkata | `tell` = memberi tahu | `come` = datang | `buy` = membeli
Tips praktis: Ubah 3 chat temanmu jadi kalimat reported.

#### Bagian 2: Say vs Tell, Jangan Ketukar Pasangan
Penjelasan: Say + isi ("He said he was tired"), tell + orang + isi ("He told me he was tired"). Tell butuh korban, say nggak. Simpel tapi favorit soal ujian.

Kesalahan klasik: "He told that he was tired" — told tanpa orang itu janggal. Pakai said that atau told me that. Pilih satu paket yang utuh.

That boleh dibuang: "He said (that) he was sick". Nggak ngaruh arti, cuma gaya. Buang biar lisanmu natural.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `She told me the meeting was cancelled.` — Dia memberitahuku rapatnya dibatalkan. | `He said he would call later.` — Dia bilang akan menelepon nanti. | `They told us to wait outside.` — Mereka menyuruh kami menunggu di luar.
Kosakata baru (4): `meeting` = rapat | `cancel` = batal | `wait` = menunggu | `outside` = di luar
Tips praktis: Hafalkan paket: say something, tell somebody something.

#### Bagian 3: Perintah dan Tanya Versi Tak Langsung
Penjelasan: Perintah jadi to-infinitive: "Close the door!" → "He told me to close the door". Larangan tambah not: told me not to smoke. Rapi dan sopan.

Pertanyaan Yes/No jadi if/whether: "Are you ready?" → "He asked if I was ready". WH-question tetap pakai WH-nya: asked where I lived. Urutannya kembali normal (bukan kalimat tanya lagi).

Jangan pakai tanda tanya di reported question: "He asked where I lived." Titik, bukan tanya. Karena sudah jadi berita, bukan tanya beneran.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `She asked me to turn off the light.` — Dia memintaku mematikan lampu. | `He asked if I needed help.` — Dia bertanya apakah aku butuh bantuan. | `The officer asked where I was going.` — Petugas bertanya ke mana aku pergi.
Kosakata baru (4): `turn off` = mematikan | `light` = lampu | `officer` = petugas | `need` = butuh
Tips praktis: Ubah satu perintah bos/dosenmu jadi reported.
Kuis:
1. "I am sick." He said he ___ sick. | A) is B) was C) will be D) has been | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Present mundur menjadi past dalam reported speech.
2. She ___ me to sit down. | A) said B) told C) asked to D) say | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Tell memakai objek orang sebelum infinitive.
3. He asked ___ I lived in Surabaya. | A) what B) if C) where do D) that | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Pertanyaan Yes/No menjadi if dalam reported speech.

### en21 · B1 5/12 · Yang Mana? Relative Clause Biar Padat
- Deskripsi: Gabung dua kalimat dengan who/which/that biar tulisanmu nggak putus-putus.
- Durasi: 35 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Who untuk Orang, Which untuk Benda
Penjelasan: Daripada "I have a friend. She lives in Bali", gabung: "I have a friend who lives in Bali". Satu kalimat, info double. Who untuk orang, which/that untuk benda.

Orang Indonesia suka nambah pronoun ganda: "The book which I bought it" — it-nya buang. Which sudah jadi objeknya, jangan dobel.

That bisa gantikan who/which di defining clause: "The man that helped me". Serbaguna, tapi nggak bisa dipakai setelah koma non-defining.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The girl who sits next to me is smart.` — Gadis yang duduk di sebelahku pintar. | `I bought a motorbike which is very fuel-efficient.` — Aku membeli motor yang sangat irit. | `This is the house that I grew up in.` — Ini rumah tempat aku dibesarkan.
Kosakata baru (4): `smart` = pintar | `fuel-efficient` = irit BBM | `house` = rumah | `grow up` = dibesarkan
Tips praktis: Gabungkan 3 pasang kalimat di notes-mu pakai who/which.

#### Bagian 2: Koma yang Mengubah Nyawa
Penjelasan: Tanpa koma = defining (wajib buat identifikasi): "Students who cheat will fail". Pakai koma = non-defining (info bonus): "My brother, who lives in Medan, is a pilot". Beda koma, beda nasib.

Di non-defining nggak boleh pakai that, wajib who/which. "My mother, that cooks well" itu salah, harus who. Detail kecil yang dilirik IELTS.

Latihan: baca ulang tulisanmu, tanya: kalau klausa ini dibuang, masih tahu yang mana? Kalau tidak, jangan pakai koma.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `My laptop, which I bought in 2021, is slow now.` — Laptopku, yang kubeli 2021, kini lambat. | `Rina, who speaks three languages, got the job.` — Rina, yang bicara tiga bahasa, dapat pekerjaan itu. | `The train that we missed left on time.` — Kereta yang kami lewatkan berangkat tepat waktu.
Kosakata baru (4): `laptop` = laptop | `slow` = lambat | `language` = bahasa | `miss` = melewatkan
Tips praktis: Tambahkan info bonus koma ke 2 kalimat tentang keluargamu.

#### Bagian 3: Where, When, Whose Biar Lengkap
Penjelasan: Tempat pakai where, waktu pakai when, milik pakai whose: "The warung where we eat", "The day when we met", "A friend whose father is a dalang". Langsung spesifik.

Whose sering ditakuti padahal gampang: whose + benda miliknya. "Students whose phones ring must leave". Nggak perlu of which yang ribet di level ini.

Waspada whose vs who's: whose = milik siapa, who's = who is. Satu apostrof beda dunia. "Who's coming?" vs "Whose bag is this?" Jangan ketukar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `This is the stall where we always eat pecel.` — Ini warung tempat kami selalu makan pecel. | `I remember the day when we first met.` — Aku ingat hari saat kita pertama bertemu. | `She is a writer whose books sell well.` — Dia penulis yang bukunya laris.
Kosakata baru (4): `stall` = warung | `remember` = ingat | `writer` = penulis | `sell` = menjual
Tips praktis: Deskripsikan tempat favoritmu pakai where 2 kalimat.
Kuis:
1. The man ___ helped me is kind. | A) who B) which C) whose D) when | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Orang sebagai subjek memakai who.
2. My phone, ___ I bought yesterday, is lost. | A) that B) which C) who D) whose | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Non-defining setelah koma memakai which bukan that.
3. She is a girl ___ cat is cute. | A) who's B) whose C) which D) who | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Kepemilikan memakai whose.

### en22 · B1 6/12 · Phrasal Verb dan Linking Words Biar Nulis Nggak Kaku
- Deskripsi: Jinakkan put off, look after, however, moreover buat esai dan obrolan natural.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Vocab | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Phrasal Verb, Artinya Nggak Bisa Ditebak
Penjelasan: Put off itu menunda, bukan menaruh mati. Look after itu merawat, bukan melihat setelah. Phrasal verb memang nggak bisa diterjemahkan kata per kata, harus hafal paketnya.

Orang Indonesia suka artikan harfiah: "I put my jacket off" dikira naruh jaket mati. Padahal put off = tunda, take off = lepas landas/lepas baju. Konteks yang menentukan.

Fokus ke 20 paling sering: get up, turn on/off, look for/after, put off, give up, run out of. Kuasai ini, percakapanmu naik 50%.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Don't put off your homework.` — Jangan tunda PR-mu. | `Who looks after your cat when you travel?` — Siapa merawat kucingmu saat kamu bepergian? | `We ran out of rice yesterday.` — Kami kehabisan beras kemarin.
Kosakata baru (4): `put off` = menunda | `look after` = merawat | `run out of` = kehabisan | `give up` = menyerah
Tips praktis: Tempel 5 phrasal verb di pintu kamar dan pakai minggu ini.

#### Bagian 2: However, Moreover, Therefore Buat Esai
Penjelasan: Esai tanpa linking words itu kayak soto tanpa kerupuk — hambar. However (namun), moreover (selain itu), therefore (oleh karena itu) bikin argumenmu mengalir.

Posisinya fleksibel: di awal + koma atau di tengah + titik koma. "Motorbikes are cheap. However, they are dangerous." Kontras langsung kerasa.

Jangan spam moreover tiap kalimat. Satu paragraf satu-dua penghubung cukup. Kebanyakan malah bikin penguji curiga kamu hafalan template.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Online shopping is cheap. However, the quality varies.` — Belanja online murah. Namun, kualitasnya beragam. | `Moreover, delivery is often late.` — Selain itu, pengiriman sering terlambat. | `Therefore, buyers must be careful.` — Oleh karena itu, pembeli harus hati-hati.
Kosakata baru (4): `however` = namun | `moreover` = selain itu | `therefore` = oleh karena itu | `quality` = kualitas
Tips praktis: Tulis ulang satu paragrafmu dengan 2 linking words.

#### Bagian 3: Paragraf B1 yang Rapi
Penjelasan: Struktur aman: topic sentence, 2-3 kalimat pendukung + contoh, closing mini. "Public transport should be improved. First, ... Moreover, ... Therefore, ..." Penguji langsung paham alurmu.

Kesalahan umum Indonesia: satu paragraf satu kalimat raksasa penuh koma. Pecah jadi 4-5 kalimat pendek. Pendek tapi jelas lebih baik dari panjang tapi ngos-ngosan.

Akhiri dengan contoh lokal biar beda: "For example, in Surabaya the bus is often full". Contoh konkret = nilai tambah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Firstly, buses reduce traffic jams.` — Pertama, bus mengurangi macet. | `For example, TransJakarta carries millions daily.` — Contohnya, TransJakarta mengangkut jutaan tiap hari. | `In conclusion, cities need better buses.` — Kesimpulannya, kota butuh bus lebih baik.
Kosakata baru (4): `reduce` = mengurangi | `traffic jam` = macet | `daily` = harian | `conclusion` = kesimpulan
Tips praktis: Tulis paragraf 5 kalimat tentang kotamu pakai firstly + for example.
Kuis:
1. Please ___ the lights when you leave. | A) turn off B) put off C) look after D) run out | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Turn off berarti mematikan lampu.
2. Motorbikes are fast. ___, they are risky. | A) Moreover B) Therefore C) However D) Firstly | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Kontras memakai however.
3. We ___ petrol on the way to Dieng. | A) ran out of B) looked after C) put off D) gave up | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Ran out of berarti kehabisan.

### en53 · B1 7/12 · Sudah Lama Berlangsung: Present Perfect Continuous
- Deskripsi: Ceritakan aksi sejak tadi/pagi dengan have been + V-ing biar durasinya kerasa.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Have Been Doing, Fokus Durasi
Penjelasan: Rumusnya have/has + been + V-ing: I have been waiting for an hour. Beda dengan present perfect biasa (I have waited = sudah selesai) — continuous tekankan masih jalan/baru berhenti dan ada bekasnya (capek, basah, ngos-ngosan).

Orang Indonesia sering I am waiting since morning — am + since itu tabrakan. Since/for itu bendera perfect, wajib have been. Since + titik (since 7), for + durasi (for 2 hours). Pasangan abadi.

Sinyal lelahnya: all day, all morning, lately, recently. Ketemu ini, hampir pasti perfect continuous. I have been scrolling lately, my eyes hurt. Relate kan?

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I have been queuing since seven this morning.` — Aku sudah antre sejak jam 7 pagi ini. | `She has been cooking rendang for three hours.` — Dia sudah memasak rendang tiga jam. | `My eyes hurt because I have been staring at my phone.` — Mataku sakit karena menatap HP terus.
Kosakata baru (4): `queue` = antre | `since` = sejak | `lately` = belakangan ini | `stare` = menatap
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 have been tentang harimu ini.

#### Bagian 2: Perfect Continuous vs Perfect Biasa
Penjelasan: Pilih continuous kalau aksinya panjang/berulang kelihatan capek: I have been running (napas ngos-ngosan). Pilih perfect biasa kalau hasilnya tuntas: I have run 5 km (jaraknya kelar). Satu capeknya, satu hasilnya. Pilih sesuai mau pamer yang mana.

Jebakan stative verb: know, love, have, be tidak pakai -ing. I have known him for years (bukan have been knowing). Stative itu keadaan, bukan aksi. Jangan dipaksa continuous.

Orang Indonesia suka I have been live here — live + been? Harusnya living. Been wajib diikuti -ing. Been live itu setengah matang.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I have lived here for five years.` — Aku sudah tinggal di sini lima tahun (stative, tanpa -ing). | `I have been learning guitar for a month.` — Aku sudah belajar gitar sebulan (aksi, pakai -ing). | `It has been raining all day.` — Hujan sudah seharian.
Kosakata baru (4): `statis` = stative (keadaan) | `result` = hasil | `duration` = durasi | `guitar` = gitar
Tips praktis: Ubah 3 have done-mu jadi have been doing jika aksinya panjang.

#### Bagian 3: Komplain Capek yang Elegan
Penjelasan: Perfect continuous itu bahasa komplain elegan: I've been waiting! I've been calling you! Kedengeran capek tapi grammar rapi. Tambahkan so + akibat: I've been standing, so my legs ache. Komplain + bukti.

Di interview, pakai buat pamer konsistensi: I have been teaching kids for two years. Durasi = dedikasi. Penguji langsung catat kamu tekun.

Akhiri dengan jeda: I need a break, I've been working nonstop. Need a break itu penutup manusiawi. Kerja keras boleh, istirahat wajib.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Sorry I'm sweaty, I have been jogging.` — Maaf keringatan, aku habis jogging. | `We have been saving to buy a laptop.` — Kami sudah menabung untuk beli laptop. | `He has been talking for an hour nonstop!` — Dia sudah ngomong sejam nonstop!
Kosakata baru (4): `sweaty` = berkeringat | `nonstop` = tanpa henti | `ache` = nyeri | `break` = istirahat
Tips praktis: Komplain 1x hari ini pakai I've been ...!
Kuis:
1. I ___ for you since noon. | A) am waiting B) have been waiting C) wait D) waited | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Sejak + masih berlangsung memakai have been + V-ing.
2. I have ___ him for years. (know = stative) | A) been knowing B) known C) knowing D) know | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Stative verb tidak memakai continuous.
3. She has been cooking ___ two hours. | A) since B) for C) ago D) at | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Durasi memakai for, titik awal memakai since.

### en54 · B1 8/12 · Sudah Terjadi Sebelum Itu: Past Perfect
- Deskripsi: Urutkan dua kejadian lampau dengan had + V3 biar alur ceritamu jelas.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Had Done, Kejadian yang Lebih Dulu
Penjelasan: Past perfect itu had + V3 buat kejadian yang lebih dulu dari kejadian lampau lain: When I arrived, the train had left. Keretanya pergi duluan, aku datang belakangan. Dua lampau, satu lebih lampau.

Orang Indonesia sering dua-duanya past simple: When I came, train left — ambigu siapa duluan. Had menandai senioritas waktu. Had left = sudah pergi sebelum aku datang. Jelas kan?

Sinyalnya: when, before, after, by the time, already, just. Ketemu after + had? After I had eaten, I left. Before + past simple? I left before he came. Pasangannya beda, hafalkan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `When I got home, my sister had finished the cake.` — Saat aku pulang, kakak sudah menghabiskan kue. | `The movie had started before we arrived.` — Film sudah mulai sebelum kami tiba. | `I had never seen snow before 2023.` — Aku belum pernah lihat salju sebelum 2023.
Kosakata baru (4): `before` = sebelum | `after` = setelah | `already` = sudah | `arrive` = tiba
Tips praktis: Ceritakan telatmu kemarin pakai had + V3.

#### Bagian 2: Reported + Alasan Telat Pakai Had
Penjelasan: Past perfect itu wajib di reported lampau: I lost → He said he had lost. Juga buat alasan: I was hungry because I had skipped lunch. Sebabnya lebih dulu, jadi had. Logis kan?

Jebakan: had had! I had had breakfast (aku sudah sarapan sebelum sesuatu). Had pertama aux, had kedua V3 dari have. Memang aneh dilihat, tapi benar. Jangan takut double had.

Bedakan had to (harus) vs had + V3 (telah): I had to go (wajib pergi) vs I had gone (telah pergi). Spasi + to vs V3 beda dunia.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `She was tired because she had worked all night.` — Dia lelah karena telah kerja semalaman. | `He said he had lost his wallet the day before.` — Dia bilang telah kehilangan dompet sehari sebelumnya. | `I had had coffee, so I wasn't sleepy.` — Aku sudah ngopi, jadi tidak ngantuk.
Kosakata baru (4): `skip` = melewatkan | `tired` = lelah | `wallet` = dompet | `sleepy` = mengantuk
Tips praktis: Jelaskan 1 alasan telatmu pakai because + had.

#### Bagian 3: Plot Twist Storytelling
Penjelasan: Past perfect bikin cerita ada twist: I rushed to the station, but the bus had gone. I saved for months, but the price had risen. Had gone/risen itu pukulan telak kedua. Drama dapet, grammar dapet.

Struktur 3 kalimat: setting past (I woke late), earlier event had (the rain had flooded the road), result past (so I failed). Setting-had-result. Hafalkan alur ini buat story test.

Latihan: tulis apesmu 2024 4 kalimat had. Dijamin pembaca ikut nyesek. Nyesek + grammar = engagement.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `By the time we came, tickets had sold out.` — Saat kami datang, tiket sudah ludes. | `I recognized her because I had seen her photo.` — Aku mengenalinya karena telah lihat fotonya. | `The road had been repaired, so it was smooth.` — Jalan telah diperbaiki, jadi mulus.
Kosakata baru (4): `sold out` = ludes | `recognize` = mengenali | `smooth` = mulus | `by the time` = saat (batas waktu)
Tips praktis: Tulis satu apes antre version had hari ini.
Kuis:
1. When I came, the bus ___. | A) had left B) has left C) leaves D) leave | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Kejadian lebih dulu memakai had + V3.
2. She said she ___ her key. | A) has lost B) had lost C) loses D) lose | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Reported lampau memakai past perfect.
3. After I ___ eaten, I paid. | A) have B) had C) has D) having | Jawaban: B | Alasan: After + kejadian duluan memakai had + V3.

### en55 · B1 9/12 · Suka Lari vs Mau Lari: Gerund vs Infinitive
- Deskripsi: Bedakan enjoy cooking vs want to cook biar nggak ketukar -ing dan to-.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Daftar Wajib -ing: Enjoy, Avoid, Finish
Penjelasan: Hafalkan geng -ing: enjoy, avoid, finish, mind, keep, suggest, dislike, practise. Enjoy cooking, avoid smoking, finish homework (finish + benda/-ing, bukan to!). Keluar terus di ujian.

Orang Indonesia sering enjoy to swim — to-nya buang. Enjoy + -ing selamanya. Suggest juga: suggest going, bukan suggest to go. Dua ini paling sering salah.

Mind + -ing sudah dibahas di telepon: Would you mind waiting? Mind itu ketua geng -ing. Ketemu mind, otomatis -ing.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I enjoy riding motorbikes at night.` — Aku suka naik motor malam hari. | `Avoid eating too much fried food.` — Hindari makan gorengan kebanyakan. | `Have you finished washing the dishes?` — Sudah selesai cuci piring?
Kosakata baru (4): `enjoy` = menikmati | `avoid` = menghindari | `finish` = selesai | `suggest` = menyarankan
Tips praktis: Tempel 8 geng -ing di cermin kamar.

#### Bagian 2: Daftar Wajib To: Want, Need, Decide, Plan
Penjelasan: Geng to-infinitive: want, need, decide, plan, hope, promise, agree, refuse. I want to sleep, she decided to move. To + kata kerja dasar, bukan -ing.

Want + orang + to juga bisa: I want you to come. Want me to call? Pola ini sopan buat minta. Jangan I want you coming — coming-nya ganti to come.

Kesalahan abadi: decide going harus decide to go. Plan holiday harus plan to go holiday? Plan + to + verb. To di sini jembatan wajib, bukan hiasan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I need to charge my phone now.` — Aku perlu cas HP kini. | `We decided to stay in Jogja longer.` — Kami memutuskan tinggal Jogja lebih lama. | `She promised to call back tonight.` — Dia janji telepon balik malam ini.
Kosakata baru (4): `want` = mau | `decide` = memutuskan | `promise` = berjanji | `refuse` = menolak
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 rencanamu pakai decided/planned to ....

#### Bagian 3: Yang Bisa Dua-duanya Tapi Beda Rasa
Penjelasan: Like/love/hate/prefer bisa -ing (umum) dan to (spesifik sekali): I like swimming (hobi umum) vs I'd like to swim now (mau kini spesifik). 'd like selalu + to, bukan -ing. I'd like to drink, bukan I'd like drinking.

Stop juga beda: stop smoking (berhenti kebiasaan) vs stop to smoke (berhenti jalan buat ngerokok). Satu berhenti total, satu berhenti sejenak buat aksi lain. Ujian suka jebak ini.

Remember/forget: remember to pay (jangan lupa bayar, belum kejadian) vs remember paying (ingat pernah bayar, sudah kejadian). To = masa depan, -ing = masa lalu. Waktu yang menentukan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I'd like to order soto, please.` — Saya mau pesan soto. | `Stop scrolling, let's study!` — Berhenti scroll, ayo belajar! | `Remember to lock the door!` — Ingat kunci pintu (jangan lupa)!
Kosakata baru (4): `prefer` = lebih suka | `stop` = berhenti | `remember` = ingat | `lock` = mengunci
Tips praktis: Bedakan 2 like -ing vs would like to versimu.
Kuis:
1. I enjoy ___ in the rain. | A) walk B) to walk C) walking D) walks | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Enjoy memakai V-ing.
2. She decided ___ a new phone. | A) buying B) to buy C) buy D) bought | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Decide memakai to-infinitive.
3. Stop ___! The baby sleeps. | A) to shout B) shouting C) shout D) shouted | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Stop kebiasaan memakai V-ing.

### en56 · B1 10/12 · Fakta Abadi: Zero Conditional
- Deskripsi: Jelaskan fakta sains dan aturan kos dengan if + present dua sisi yang logis.
- Durasi: 35 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: If Present, Present Buat Fakta
Penjelasan: Zero conditional itu if + present, hasil present: If you heat ice, it melts. Syarat dan hasil sama-sama fakta, bukan khayalan. Berlaku selamanya kayak hukum fisika.

Bedakan dengan conditional 1: 1 itu masa depan (If it rains, I'll stay), 0 itu fakta umum (If it rains, roads get slippery). Will vs present di hasil beda dunia. Cek: ramalan atau fakta?

Orang Indonesia sering If you will heat... — will bocor lagi. Zero tidak ada will sama sekali. Dua sisi present polos. Steril dari will.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If you mix red and white, you get pink.` — Jika campur merah putih, dapat pink. | `If it rains, the yard floods quickly.` — Jika hujan, halaman cepat banjir. | `Plants die if you don't water them.` — Tanaman mati jika tidak disiram.
Kosakata baru (4): `mix` = mencampur | `melt` = meleleh | `plant` = tanaman | `slippery` = licin
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 fakta dapur/kosanmu pakai zero.

#### Bagian 2: When/Unless + Imperative Fakta
Penjelasan: When bisa ganti if untuk fakta pasti: When I stay up, I get dizzy. If untuk syarat, when untuk kepastian waktu. Unless = if not: Unless you water, plants die. Tiga kata, satu logika fakta.

Imperative juga oke: Heat it if it's cold, call me if lost. Conditional nggak melulu will — perintah juga hasil yang sah. Praktis buat manual.

Komanya sama kayak cond 1: If di depan pakai koma, hasil di depan tanpa koma. Plants die if you ignore them. Kecil tapi rapi.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Unless you charge it, the phone dies.` — Kecuali dicas, HP mati. | `Call me if you get lost!` — Telepon aku jika tersesat! | `When oil is hot, it splashes.` — Saat minyak panas, ia memercik.
Kosakata baru (4): `unless` = kecuali jika | `charge` = mengisi daya | `splash` = memercik | `ignore` = mengabaikan
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 aturan kosmu jadi unless + present.

#### Bagian 3: Manual & Aturan Main 4 Baris
Penjelasan: Tulis manual: If the WiFi dies, restart the router. If it still fails, call the owner. Unless you pay, speed drops. Do not reset without asking! Manual 4 baris gini kepakai di kos, kantor, lab.

Kesalahan umum: mencampur zero dan first seenaknya — If ice melts, it will flow? Pilih fakta (melts, flows) ATAU ramalan (If ice melts tomorrow, we'll mop). Konsisten satu logika per kalimat.

Akhiri dengan peringatan fakta: If you stare long, your eyes hurt. Sains + peduli. Conditional zero yang bermanfaat.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If the gas runs out, we order food.` — Jika gas habis, kami pesan makan. | `If you press this, the light turns on.` — Jika tekan ini, lampu menyala. | `Leaves fall if the dry season comes.` — Daun gugur jika kemarau tiba.
Kosakata baru (4): `gas` = gas | `press` = menekan | `leaf - leaves` = daun | `dry season` = kemarau
Tips praktis: Tulis manual magic com/mesin cuci 3 baris zero.
Kuis:
1. If you heat water, it ___. | A) boils B) will boil C) boiled D) boiling | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Fakta umum memakai present dua sisi.
2. ___ you water plants, they die. | A) If B) Unless C) When will D) Will | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Unless = if not untuk fakta.
3. Call me if you ___ lost! | A) will get B) get C) got D) getting | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Klausa if zero memakai present tanpa will.

### en57 · B1 11/12 · Bosen vs Membosenkan: -ed vs -ing Adjectives
- Deskripsi: Bedakan I am bored vs the class is boring biar curhatmu tepat sasaran.
- Durasi: 35 menit | Tag: Vocab | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: -ed Buat yang Merasa, -ing Buat Penyebab
Penjelasan: Rumus abadi: -ed = perasaan orang (I am bored, tired, excited), -ing = sifat penyebab (the movie is boring, tiring, exciting). Aku yang bored, filmnya yang boring. Jangan ketukar pelakunya.

Orang Indonesia sering I am boring saat maksud bosan — artinya aku membosankan (kasihan!). Mau bilang bosan? I am bored. Mau bilang membosankan? It is boring. Satu huruf beda harga diri.

Daftar wajib: interested/interesting, tired/tiring, excited/exciting, embarrassed/embarrassing, confused/confusing. Lima pasang ini keluar melulu.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I am excited about the concert tonight!` — Aku antusias soal konser malam ini! | `The queue was tiring but worth it.` — Antreannya melelahkan tapi sepadan. | `Are you interested in volunteering?` — Apakah kamu tertarik relawan?
Kosakata baru (4): `bored` = bosan (merasa) | `boring` = membosankan | `excited` = antusias | `tiring` = melelahkan
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 -ed perasaanmu + 3 -ing penyebabnya.

#### Bagian 2: Sangat? Very vs So...That
Penjelasan: Perkuat dengan very (The game was very exciting) atau so...that (So boring that I slept!). So...that ada akibatnya, very cuma level. Mau drama? So...that.

Jebakan: very boring boleh, very bored boleh, tapi too bored = kelewat bosan sampai negatif. Too lagi-lagi keluhan. I was too tired to walk = kecapekan sampai nggak bisa jalan. Ada to + verb setelahnya.

Bedakan dengan enough: old enough, big enough. Too + sifat + to (kelewat, negatif), sifat + enough + to (cukup, positif). Too young to marry vs old enough to vote. Umur yang menentukan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The lecture was so confusing that half slept.` — Kuliahnya sangat membingungkan sampai separuh tidur. | `I'm too exhausted to cook tonight.` — Aku terlalu lelah untuk masak malam ini. | `Are you old enough to ride a motorbike?` — Apakah cukup umur naik motor?
Kosakata baru (4): `confusing` = membingungkan | `exhausted` = sangat lelah | `enough` = cukup | `lecture` = kuliah/ceramah
Tips praktis: Review film kemarin pakai 2 -ing + 1 so...that.

#### Bagian 3: Curhat Ujian Tanpa Drama Berlebih
Penjelasan: Curhat elegan: I was nervous before, confused during, relieved after. Tiga -ed, satu alur ujian. Tambah penyebab: The instructions were confusing, the room was noisy. Curhat + analisis.

Hindari spam very di esai — ganti exhausted (very tired), starving (very hungry), furious (very angry). Satu kata kuat = vocab score naik. Ini jembatan ke C1.

Tutup dengan syukur hedged: Overall it was tiring but satisfying. Overall + but = dewasa. Nggak cuma ngeluh, ada hikmah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I felt embarrassed when I mispronounced it.` — Aku malu saat salah melafalkan. | `The trip was exhausting yet fun.` — Perjalanannya melelahkan tapi seru. | `Overall, I am satisfied with my score.` — Secara umum, aku puas dengan nilaiku.
Kosakata baru (4): `nervous` = gugup | `relieved` = lega | `satisfied` = puas | `overall` = secara umum
Tips praktis: Ceritakan ujian terakhirmu 4 kalimat -ed/-ing.
Kuis:
1. I am ___ (merasa bosan). | A) boring B) bored C) bore D) boredom | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Perasaan memakai -ed.
2. The lesson was so ___ that I slept. | A) bored B) boring C) bore D) boredom | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Penyebab memakai -ing.
3. I'm too tired ___ dinner. | A) to cook B) cooking C) cook D) cooked | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Too + sifat + to-infinitive.

### en58 · B1 12/12 · Bener Kan? Question Tags
- Deskripsi: Akhiri kalimat dengan ..., isn't it? biar obrolan hidup dan konfirmasi dapet.
- Durasi: 35 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B1

#### Bagian 1: Positif-Negatif Berpasangan
Penjelasan: Aturannya: positif + tag negatif (You are Rina, aren't you?), negatif + tag positif (You aren't busy, are you?). Aux + pronoun, jangan nama. Rina is here, isn't she? She, bukan Rina lagi.

Aux-nya ngikut tense: will→won't, can→can't, have→haven't, do→don't/doesn't, be→isn't/aren't. You like sambal, don't you? She works here, doesn't she? Aux cerminan kalimatnya.

Orang Indonesia sering isn't it untuk semua — You are tired, isn't it? Harusnya aren't you. It hanya untuk benda/hewan/cuaca. Orangnya you, tag-nya you.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `You're from Medan, aren't you?` — Kamu dari Medan, kan? | `She can't swim, can she?` — Dia tidak bisa berenang, kan? | `Nice weather, isn't it?` — Cuaca bagus, ya?
Kosakata baru (4): `tag` = ekor tanya | `aren't you` = kan (kamu) | `doesn't she` = kan (dia) | `weather` = cuaca
Tips praktis: Tambahkan tag ke 3 kalimat chat-mu hari ini.

#### Bagian 2: Jawab Tag Tanpa Panik
Penjelasan: Tag positif tanya konfirmasi: You're coming, aren't you? Jawab Yes, I am / No, I'm not. Tag negatif juga sama polanya, jawab sesuai fakta bukan sesuai tag. You aren't sick, are you? Sakit? Yes, I am (ya, sakit). Jangan terkecoh negatifnya.

Intonasi naik (naik = tanya beneran, butuh jawab), turun (turun = yakin, cuma basa-basi). Hot today, isn't it? (turun) = setuju dong. You haven't paid, have you? (naik) = jawab jujur!

Jebakan let's: Let's go, shall we? Shall khusus untuk let's, bukan will. Let's eat, shall we? Satu-satunya tag shall yang wajib hafal.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Let's rest a bit, shall we?` — Istirahat bentar yuk, mau kan? | `You haven't eaten, have you?` — Kamu belum makan, kan? | `I'm late, aren't I?` — Aku telat, ya? (I am → aren't I, spesial!)
Kosakata baru (4): `shall we` = mau kan (untuk let's) | `confirm` = konfirmasi | `intonation` = intonasi | `late` = terlambat
Tips praktis: Latih 5 tag dengan intonasi naik vs turun.

#### Bagian 3: Tag Buat Bass-basi dan Rapat
Penjelasan: Di rapat, tag itu pelunak: The report is ready, isn't it? (yakin tapi sopan). This will work, won't it? Bandingkan tanpa tag yang kaku memerintah. Tag = sopan + interaktif.

Untuk opini sensitif, tambah think: I think it's pricey, isn't it? Think melembutkan, tag ngajak setuju. Kombo aman buat kritik harga kos.

Hindari tag ganda: Isn't it right, isn't it? Cukup satu. Satu tag per kalimat, itu aturan mainnya.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The WiFi is slow today, isn't it?` — WiFi lambat hari ini, ya? | `You will come tomorrow, won't you?` — Kamu datang besok, kan? | `We should leave now, shouldn't we?` — Kita harus pergi kini, kan?
Kosakata baru (4): `report` = laporan | `slow` = lambat | `leave` = pergi | `ready` = siap
Tips praktis: Buka 2 obrolan hari ini dengan tag cuaca/kerja.
Kuis:
1. You're tired, ___? | A) isn't it B) aren't you C) don't you D) isn't you | Jawaban: B | Alasan: You + be memakai aren't you.
2. Let's go home, ___? | A) will we B) shall we C) don't we D) are we | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Let's memakai shall we.
3. She works here, ___? | A) doesn't she B) don't she C) isn't she D) won't she | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Present simple she memakai doesn't she.

### Level B2

### en23 · B2 1/12 · Andai Saja: Conditional 2 untuk Mimpi
- Deskripsi: Hayalkan situasi tak nyata sekarang dengan if + past dan would yang halus.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: If Past, Would Kata Kuncinya
Penjelasan: Conditional 2 itu mesin pengandaian: If + past simple, would + kata kerja. "If I had more money, I would travel". Faktanya? Nggak punya duitnya sekarang. Makanya past di sini bukan lampau, tapi penanda khayalan.

Orang Indonesia sering tulis "If I will have money, I would..." — will bocor lagi. Di conditional 2, if-nya past polos, nggak ada will/would di dalamnya.

Were untuk semua orang di kalimat formal: "If I were you". Was boleh di lisan, tapi were lebih aman buat ujian tulis.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If I spoke Japanese, I would work in Tokyo.` — Jika aku bisa bahasa Jepang, aku akan kerja di Tokyo. | `Would you help if I asked?` — Maukah kamu membantu jika aku meminta? | `If I were rich, I would build a library.` — Jika aku kaya, aku akan membangun perpustakaan.
Kosakata baru (4): `speak` = berbicara | `rich` = kaya | `build` = membangun | `library` = perpustakaan
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 khayalanmu diawali If I had ....

#### Bagian 2: Mimpi, Saran, dan Keluhan Halus
Penjelasan: Conditional 2 kepakai buat tiga hal: mimpi ("If I lived near the beach..."), saran sopan ("If I were you, I'd rest"), dan keluhan halus ("If they paid more, I wouldn't complain"). Satu pola, banyak rasa.

Bedakan dengan conditional 1: 1 itu mungkin terjadi, 2 itu hampir mustahil sekarang. "If it rains, I'll stay" (mungkin) vs "If I had wings, I'd fly" (mustahil). Pilih sesuai realita.

Tambahkan second conditional tanpa if: "Were I rich..." versi formal. Jarang dipakai, tapi muncul di bacaan B2. Kenali saja dulu.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If I were you, I'd accept the offer.` — Jika aku jadi kamu, aku akan terima tawaran itu. | `Life would be easier if the train stopped here.` — Hidup akan lebih mudah jika kereta berhenti di sini. | `If we left now, we would still be late.` — Jika kita berangkat sekarang, kita tetap akan terlambat.
Kosakata baru (4): `accept` = menerima | `offer` = tawaran | `easier` = lebih mudah | `complain` = mengeluh
Tips praktis: Kasih saran ke temanmu diawali If I were you ....

#### Bagian 3: Diskusi What If Biar Lancar Ngomong
Penjelasan: Topik what if itu umpan speaking terbaik: What would you do if you won 1M? Jawab 3 kalimat conditional 2, langsung dinilai lancar sama penguji.

Kesalahan umum: jawab pakai will ("I will buy...") — harus would biar konsisten khayal. Will bikin khayalanmu kedengeran janji beneran.

Rekam jawabanmu 1 menit, cek: ada if + past? Ada would? Kalau dua-duanya ada, kamu aman.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If I won the lottery, I would open a coffee shop.` — Jika aku menang lotre, aku akan buka kedai kopi. | `I would hire my friends first.` — Aku akan mempekerjakan teman-temanku dulu. | `We would serve kopi tubruk and croissants.` — Kami akan menyajikan kopi tubruk dan croissant.
Kosakata baru (4): `win` = menang | `lottery` = lotre | `hire` = mempekerjakan | `serve` = menyajikan
Tips praktis: Jawab satu what if hari ini dengan voice note Inggris.
Kuis:
1. If I ___ more time, I would learn Korean. | A) have B) had C) will have D) have had | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Conditional 2 memakai past simple di klausa if.
2. If I ___ you, I would apologize. | A) am B) was C) were D) will be | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Bentuk formal conditional 2 memakai were untuk semua subjek.
3. What would you do if you ___ a ghost? | A) see B) saw C) will see D) seen | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Khayalan memakai past simple saw.

### en24 · B2 2/12 · Menyesali Kemarin: Conditional 3
- Deskripsi: Bahas penyesalan masa lalu dengan if + past perfect dan would have yang tepat.
- Durasi: 45 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: If Had Done, Would Have Happened
Penjelasan: Conditional 3 itu mesin waktu penyesalan: If + past perfect, would have + V3. "If I had studied, I would have passed". Faktanya? Nggak belajar, nggak lulus. Nyesek tapi grammar-nya rapi.

Orang Indonesia sering singkat jadi "If I studied, I would pass" untuk masa lalu — itu conditional 2, beda waktu. Kalau sudah kejadian dan gagal, wajib had + V3 dan would have.

Hafalkan had contraction: I'd studied = I had studied, bukan I would. Konteks if menandakan had. Jangan ketukar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If we had left earlier, we would have caught the train.` — Jika kami berangkat lebih awal, kami akan keburu kereta. | `She would have won if she hadn't fallen.` — Dia akan menang jika tidak jatuh. | `If you had saved, you would have bought it.` — Jika kamu menabung, kamu akan membelinya.
Kosakata baru (4): `earlier` = lebih awal | `catch` = mengejar/mendapat | `fall` = jatuh | `save` = menabung
Tips praktis: Tulis satu penyesalanmu 2024 pakai pola ini.

#### Bagian 2: Criticism Halus Pakai Should Have
Penjelasan: Should have + V3 itu cara ngomel elegan: "You should have told me". Artinya kamu nggak bilang, dan aku kecewa. Lebih sopan dari "Why didn't you tell me?!" tapi pesannya sampai.

Bedakan should have (penyesalan/nasihat lampau) vs must have (dugaan lampau, dibahas di modul deduction). "You should have come" (kamu nggak datang, sayang) vs "You must have come" (aku yakin kamu datang). Beda jauh.

Negatifnya shouldn't have: "I shouldn't have eaten that third gorengan". Semua orang Indonesia relate sama kalimat ini.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `You should have worn a jacket, it's cold.` — Seharusnya kamu pakai jaket, ini dingin. | `I shouldn't have stayed up late.` — Seharusnya aku tidak begadang. | `They should have booked earlier.` — Seharusnya mereka memesan lebih awal.
Kosakata baru (4): `wear` = memakai | `stay up` = begadang | `book` = memesan | `late` = terlambat
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 should have tentang minggu lalumu.

#### Bagian 3: Cerita Kegagalan Jadi Pelajaran
Penjelasan: Gabungkan conditional 3 dalam storytelling: "I missed the scholarship. If I had prepared the essay, I would have passed. Next time, I will start earlier." Ada sesal, ada rencana. Penguji suka arc kayak gini.

Jangan terjebak ngarang would have dobel di if: "If I would have known" itu salah. If-nya had, would-nya di hasil. Sekali lagi: satu kubu satu senjata.

Tutup dengan pelajaran: "The lesson I learned was...". Satu kalimat refleksi bikin esaimu dewasa, bukan sekadar curhat gagal.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If I had brought cash, I wouldn't have panicked.` — Jika aku bawa tunai, aku tidak akan panik. | `The lesson I learned was to check twice.` — Pelajaran yang kupetik adalah cek dua kali. | `I would have called you if my phone hadn't died.` — Aku akan meneleponmu jika HP-ku tidak mati.
Kosakata baru (4): `panic` = panik | `lesson` = pelajaran | `check` = memeriksa | `die` = mati (baterai)
Tips praktis: Ceritakan satu kegagalan kecilmu 4 kalimat conditional 3.
Kuis:
1. If she had run faster, she ___ won. | A) will have B) would have C) would D) will | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Hasil conditional 3 memakai would have + V3.
2. You should ___ called me. | A) have B) has C) had D) having | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Pola nasihat lampau adalah should have + V3.
3. If they ___ earlier, they would have met her. | A) come B) came C) had come D) would come | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Klausa if conditional 3 memakai past perfect.

### en25 · B2 3/12 · Pasti Dia Lelah: Modal of Deduction
- Deskripsi: Tebak masa kini dan lampau dengan must/can't/might yang penuh percaya diri.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Must, Can't, Might untuk Sekarang
Penjelasan: Lihat sepatu basah? "He must be tired" (yakin ya), "He can't be lazy" (yakin tidak), "He might be sick" (mungkin). Tiga level keyakinan, satu kejadian.

Orang Indonesia suka pakai must untuk kewajiban saja, lupa fungsi dugaan. Must be + kata sifat itu dugaan kuat, bukan perintah. "You must be hungry" itu simpati, bukan suruhan makan.

Can't vs mustn't: can't be = yakin tidak ("He can't be 50, he looks 30"), mustn't be = larangan. Jangan tukar, bisa bikin salah paham serius.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The lights are on. She must be home.` — Lampunya menyala. Dia pasti di rumah. | `He can't be the thief, he was with me.` — Dia tidak mungkin pencurinya, dia bersamaku. | `They might be stuck in traffic.` — Mereka mungkin terjebak macet.
Kosakata baru (4): `tired` = lelah | `thief` = pencuri | `stuck` = terjebak | `traffic` = lalu lintas
Tips praktis: Tebak 3 hal di sekitarmu pakai must/can't/might hari ini.

#### Bagian 2: Must Have Done untuk Masa Lalu
Penjelasan: Tambah have + V3 buat nebak yang sudah lewat: "He must have forgotten" (pasti lupa), "She can't have said that" (nggak mungkin ngomong gitu), "They might have missed the bus" (mungkin ketinggalan). Mesin detektif grammar.

Kesalahan umum: "He must forgot" — lupa have. Dugaan lampau wajib have + V3, tanpa kecuali. Must forgot itu setengah matang.

Bedakan must have (yakin) vs should have (seharusnya): "You must have been tired" (kamu pasti lelah) vs "You should have rested" (seharusnya istirahat). Satu dugaan, satu penyesalan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The road is wet. It must have rained.` — Jalannya basah. Pasti telah hujan. | `She can't have eaten all that, she hates spicy!` — Dia tidak mungkin makan semua itu, dia benci pedas! | `He might have lost his wallet.` — Dia mungkin kehilangan dompetnya.
Kosakata baru (4): `wet` = basah | `hate` = benci | `lose` = kehilangan | `wallet` = dompet
Tips praktis: Jadi detektif kosan: tebak siapa habiskan mie pakai must have.

#### Bagian 3: Main Tebak-tebakan Biar Nempel
Penjelasan: Cara tercepat hafal: lihat foto orang asing dan tebak: "She must be a teacher. She can't be a student. She might have just finished class." Tiga kalimat, tiga modal, langsung praktik.
