Di ujian, sinyalnya jelas: must untuk bukti kuat, might/could/may untuk kemungkinan, can't untuk mustahil. Baca buktinya dulu, baru pilih modalnya.

Jangan pakai mustn't untuk dugaan negatif — pakai can't. "He mustn't be home" itu larangan (dia dilarang di rumah), bukan "dia pasti nggak di rumah". Fatal kalau ketukar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `He looks pale. He might be sick.` — Dia pucat. Dia mungkin sakit. | `They left an hour ago. They must have arrived.` — Mereka pergi sejam lalu. Mereka pasti sudah tiba. | `This can't be your final answer.` — Ini tidak mungkin jawaban finalmu.
Kosakata baru (4): `pale` = pucat | `arrive` = tiba | `final` = final | `answer` = jawaban
Tips praktis: Tebak plot film yang kamu tonton pakai 3 modal berbeda.
Kuis:
1. She ___ be at home, the car is outside. | A) must B) mustn't C) shouldn't D) won't | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Bukti kuat memakai must be.
2. He ___ have stolen it, he was abroad. | A) must B) should C) can't D) mightn't | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Kemustahilan lampau memakai can't have + V3.
3. They ___ have forgotten the meeting, call them. | A) must B) might C) can't D) mustn't | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Kemungkinan memakai might have.

### en26 · B2 4/12 · Passive Lanjutan dan Reported Speech Lanjutan
- Deskripsi: Taklukkan passive berlapis dan kutipan kompleks biar tulisan formalmu mulus.
- Durasi: 45 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Passive dengan Dua Objek dan Infinitive
Penjelasan: Kalau ada dua objek, pilih satu jadi subjek: "They gave me a prize" → "I was given a prize" atau "A prize was given to me". Dua-duanya benar, pilih yang mau ditonjolkan.

Passive + to-infinitive juga sering: "He is believed to be rich", "She is said to work hard". Pola ini langganan berita gosip dan laporan resmi. Hafalkan paketnya.

Orang Indonesia sering "It is said that..." padahal bisa singkat "He is said to...". Versi pendek lebih disukai penutur asli karena padat.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I was offered a new position.` — Aku ditawari posisi baru. | `He is thought to live abroad.` — Dia diperkirakan tinggal di luar negeri. | `The suspect is said to have fled.` — Tersangka dikatakan telah kabur.
Kosakata baru (4): `offer` = menawarkan | `position` = posisi | `suspect` = tersangka | `flee` = kabur
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 kalimat aktif berobjek ganda jadi passive.

#### Bagian 2: Causative: Have Something Done
Penjelasan: Kalau motong rambut, kamu nggak motong sendiri: "I had my hair cut". Polanya have + benda + V3. Artinya nyuruh orang lain ngerjain. Penting banget buat kehidupan nyata.

Bedakan have done (aku yang ngerjain) vs have something done (orang lain yang ngerjain). "I cut my hair" (potong sendiri, serem) vs "I had my hair cut" (ke barber, aman). Beda nyawa.

Get juga bisa: "I got my phone fixed". Have lebih formal, get lebih santai. Pilih sesuai suasana.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I had my motorbike serviced yesterday.` — Aku menyervis motorku kemarin (oleh bengkel). | `She got her photos printed.` — Dia mencetak fotonya (di tukang cetak). | `We need to have the AC repaired.` — Kami perlu memperbaiki AC (oleh teknisi).
Kosakata baru (4): `service` = servis | `print` = mencetak | `repair` = memperbaiki | `yesterday` = kemarin
Tips praktis: Daftar 3 jasa yang kamu pakai dan tulis have something done.

#### Bagian 3: Reported Speech Campuran Waktu
Penjelasan: Di B2 kutipannya nggak tunggal: "I have finished and I will submit tomorrow" → "He said he had finished and would submit the next day". Dua tense mundur bareng. Kuncinya: mundurkan satu-satu, jangan panik.

Kata waktu juga geser: now→then, today→that day, tomorrow→the next day, yesterday→the day before. "I'll call tomorrow" jadi "He'd call the next day". Geser kompak sama tense-nya.

Latihan: ambil chat grup 3 baris dan jadikan satu paragraf reported. Dijamin otakmu keringetan tapi naik level.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `She said she was tired and would sleep early.` — Dia bilang dia lelah dan akan tidur cepat. | `He said he had lost his key the day before.` — Dia bilang kehilangan kuncinya sehari sebelumnya. | `They said they were leaving the next morning.` — Mereka bilang akan pergi keesokan paginya.
Kosakata baru (4): `tired` = lelah | `early` = cepat/awal | `key` = kunci | `leave` = pergi
Tips praktis: Ubah satu voice note temanmu jadi reported 2 kalimat.
Kuis:
1. I ___ my laptop repaired last week. | A) had B) have C) did D) am | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Causative lampau memakai had + objek + V3.
2. He is ___ to be the winner. | A) believed B) believe C) believing D) belief | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Pola passive reporting memakai be + V3 + to-infinitive.
3. "I'll come tomorrow." She said she would come ___. | A) tomorrow B) yesterday C) the next day D) today | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Tomorrow bergeser menjadi the next day.

### en27 · B2 5/12 · Nulis Opini yang Berbobot: Esai B2
- Deskripsi: Susun esai 4 paragraf dengan thesis, argumen, dan contoh yang meyakinkan.
- Durasi: 45 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Thesis Statement yang Tajam
Penjelasan: Thesis itu janji esaimu dalam satu kalimat di akhir intro: "This essay argues that public transport should be free". Jelas, berani, bisa diperdebatkan. Tanpa ini esaimu ngambang.

Orang Indonesia suka thesis malu-malu: "I will talk about transport". Itu bukan thesis, itu daftar isi. Thesis harus ada pendirian: setuju/tidak, perlu/tidak.

Rumus intro 3 kalimat: hook (fakta/pertanyaan), background (konteks Indonesia), thesis (pendirianmu). Pendek tapi mematikan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Traffic jams cost Jakarta billions every year.` — Macet merugikan Jakarta miliaran tiap tahun. | `Many suggest free buses as a solution.` — Banyak yang menyarankan bus gratis sebagai solusi. | `This essay agrees that free transport would help greatly.` — Esai ini setuju transportasi gratis akan sangat membantu.
Kosakata baru (4): `traffic jam` = macet | `billion` = miliar | `solution` = solusi | `agree` = setuju
Tips praktis: Tulis thesis 1 kalimat tentang HP di sekolah.

#### Bagian 2: Paragraf Argumen PEEL
Penjelasan: PEEL itu Point, Evidence, Example, Link: poin ("Free buses reduce poverty"), bukti ("data..."), contoh ("in Tallinn..."), kait balik ke thesis. Empat langkah, paragrafmu kokoh.

Kesalahan umum: poin tanpa contoh — argumenmu kayak gorengan tanpa sambal, kering. Selalu tambah "For instance, in my city...". Contoh lokal bikin esaimu beda dari template.

Satu paragraf satu ide. Jangan campur hemat + lingkungan + macet dalam satu tempat. Pisah biar penguji bisa kasih nilai per ide.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Firstly, free transport helps the poor.` — Pertama, transportasi gratis membantu orang miskin. | `For instance, students in my town spend 30% on ojek.` — Contohnya, pelajar di kotaku habiskan 30% untuk ojek. | `Thus, free buses would ease their burden.` — Jadi, bus gratis akan meringankan beban mereka.
Kosakata baru (4): `poor` = miskin | `spend` = menghabiskan | `ease` = meringankan | `burden` = beban
Tips praktis: Tulis satu paragraf PEEL tentang kantin sekolahmu.

#### Bagian 3: Kesimpulan yang Bukan Copy-Paste Intro
Penjelasan: Kesimpulan itu restate + rekomendasi, bukan copas intro. Parafrase thesis-mu: "In conclusion, free transport is worth trying" lalu tambah saran: "The government should pilot it in one city". Ada penutup, ada aksi.

Hindari frasa basi "In my opinion I think". Pilih satu: In my view atau I believe. Double opinion itu redundant dan nurunin skor.

Terakhir, cek linking words: however, moreover, consequently, in contrast. Tabur secukupnya, jangan sekilo. Esai B2 yang bagus itu mengalir, bukan penuh hiasan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `In conclusion, the benefits outweigh the costs.` — Kesimpulannya, manfaat lebih besar dari biaya. | `Consequently, cities should trial free routes.` — Akibatnya, kota harus uji coba rute gratis. | `If done well, everyone will benefit.` — Jika dilakukan baik, semua akan diuntungkan.
Kosakata baru (4): `benefit` = manfaat | `cost` = biaya | `trial` = uji coba | `route` = rute
Tips praktis: Tulis kesimpulan 3 kalimat untuk esaimu dan parafrasekan thesis.
Kuis:
1. Thesis statement yang baik berisi ___. | A) daftar isi B) pendirian jelas C) pertanyaan saja D) biodata | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Thesis harus memuat posisi penulis yang bisa diperdebatkan.
2. PEEL singkatan dari ___. | A) Point Evidence Example Link B) People Eat Every Lunch C) Prepare Essay Easy Long D) Point End Example Leave | Jawaban: A | Alasan: PEEL adalah struktur paragraf argumen.
3. Kata penghubung untuk akibat adalah ___. | A) however B) moreover C) consequently D) firstly | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Consequently menandakan akibat.

### en28 · B2 6/12 · Dengerin Berita dan Debat Kecil
- Deskripsi: Tangkap ide pokok berita cepat dan sampaikan pendapat pro-kontra dengan sopan.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Listening | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Listening Berita: Tangkap 5W1H
Penjelasan: Berita itu padat, jangan kejar tiap kata. Tangkap who, what, where, when saja dulu. "Flood ... Semarang ... yesterday ... 100 houses" sudah cukup buat jawab soal ide pokok.

Orang Indonesia sering freeze saat dengar angka dan nama asing. Tulis apa adanya, ejaan belakangan. Yang penting angkanya ketangkap: 100, 15%, $2M.

Latih dengan BBC 1 menit, dengar 3 kali: pertama ide umum, kedua detail, ketiga cek jawaban. Tiga putaran itu strategi ujian, bukan tanda lemah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Floods hit Semarang last night.` — Banjir melanda Semarang semalam. | `Officials say 200 people evacuated.` — Pejabat bilang 200 orang mengungsi. | `Aid is on the way this morning.` — Bantuan dalam perjalanan pagi ini.
Kosakata baru (4): `hit` = melanda | `official` = pejabat | `evacuate` = mengungsi | `aid` = bantuan
Tips praktis: Dengar satu berita 1 menit dan tulis 4 info 5W1H.

#### Bagian 2: Setuju-Tidak Setuju Tanpa Berantem
Penjelasan: Debat sopan itu skil: "I see your point, however...", "That's true, but...", "I partly agree because...". Nggak langsung "You are wrong!" yang bikin diskusi bubar.

Bedakan fakta vs opini: "The price rose 10%" (fakta) vs "It's too expensive" (opini). Di debat, serang opininya, akui faktanya. Kamu kelihatan dewasa.

Siapkan amunisi: 3 frasa setuju, 3 tidak setuju, 2 netral. Dengan 8 frasa itu kamu bisa survive debat apa pun dari ojek online sampai BBM.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I see your point, but buses are safer.` — Aku paham maksudmu, tapi bus lebih aman. | `I partly agree, yet the cost is high.` — Aku sebagian setuju, tapi biayanya tinggi. | `That's a good point about comfort.` — Itu poin bagus soal kenyamanan.
Kosakata baru (4): `point` = poin/pendapat | `safe` = aman | `comfort` = kenyamanan | `partly` = sebagian
Tips praktis: Debat 5 menit sama teman tentang motor vs bus.

#### Bagian 3: Diskusi Mini 3 Menit
Penjelasan: Format aman buat speaking test: buka ("In my view..."), kasih 2 alasan ("Firstly... Secondly..."), tutup ("So, I believe..."). 3 menit, 3 bagian, kelar. Nggak perlu pidato kenegaraan.

Jebakan: ngomong muter tanpa contoh. Satu contoh lokal ("in my kampung...") bikin jawabanmu konkret dan beda dari peserta lain.

Kalau blank, beli waktu: "That's an interesting question. Let me think..." Dua detik mikir itu legal, asal jangan 20 detik diam.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `In my view, online learning is helpful.` — Menurutku, belajar online membantu. | `Firstly, it saves time. Secondly, it is cheaper.` — Pertama, hemat waktu. Kedua, lebih murah. | `So, schools should mix both methods.` — Jadi, sekolah harus campur kedua metode.
Kosakata baru (4): `view` = pandangan | `save` = menghemat | `cheaper` = lebih murah | `mix` = mencampur
Tips praktis: Rekam opini 1 menit tentang AI dan dengarkan ulang.
Kuis:
1. Ide pokok berita paling cepat ditangkap lewat ___. | A) tiap kata B) 5W1H C) ejaan nama D) musik latar | Jawaban: B | Alasan: 5W1H menangkap inti tanpa memahami semua kata.
2. Respons debat paling sopan: ___. | A) You are wrong! B) I see your point, however... C) Shut up! D) No way! | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Mengakui lawan lalu kontras adalah strategi sopan.
3. Frasa untuk membeli waktu berpikir: ___. | A) Diam 20 detik B) That's an interesting question... C) I don't know, bye D) Ulangi soal keras-keras | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Frasa pengulur sopan memberi waktu berpikir.

### en59 · B2 7/12 · Sudah Selesai Nanti: Future Perfect & Continuous
- Deskripsi: Proyeksikan deadline dan janji dengan will have done dan will be doing yang presisi.
- Durasi: 45 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Will Have Done Buat Target Kelar
Penjelasan: Rumusnya will have + V3 buat aksi kelar sebelum titik masa depan: By December, I will have finished my thesis. Desembernya belum datang, skripsinya (diproyeksi) sudah kelar. Ada deadline, ada janji kelar.

Orang Indonesia sering By tomorrow I will finish — kurang have. By/until itu bendera perfect: by Friday, by 2026, by the time. Ketemu by masa depan, otomatis will have.

Bedakan will have (aktif) vs will have been (pasif/durasi): I will have written (aku nulis) vs It will have been written (ditulis orang). Been lagi-lagi penanda pasif/durasi.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `By next month, I will have saved 2 million.` — Bulan depan, aku sudah menabung 2 juta. | `The bridge will have been built by 2027.` — Jembatan sudah dibangun 2027. | `Will you have eaten by noon?` — Apakah sudah makan sebelum siang?
Kosakata baru (4): `by` = sebelum/sebatas (batas waktu) | `thesis` = skripsi | `deadline` = tenggat | `save` = menabung
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 targetmu diawali By ... I will have ....

#### Bagian 2: Will Be Doing Buat Lagi Jalan Nanti
Penjelasan: Will be + V-ing buat aksi lagi berlangsung di titik masa depan: At 8 tonight, I'll be cooking. Jam 8 nanti, adegannya lagi masak. Background masa depan.

Pakai buat janji sopan: Will you be using this motorbike tonight? (pinjam halus) vs Will you lend it? (nembak). Continuous bikin pinjamam kedengeran sementara, nggak ngambil alih.

Jangan campur dengan present continuous fix: I am meeting him tomorrow (sudah janjian) vs I'll be meeting (kebetulan lagi di sana). Satu fix, satu latar. Beda niat.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `This time tomorrow, I'll be surfing in Bali!` — Besok jam segini, aku lagi surfing di Bali! | `Don't call at 7, we'll be praying.` — Jangan telepon jam 7, kami lagi salat. | `Will she be working late again?` — Apakah dia lembur lagi nanti?
Kosakata baru (4): `surf` = berselancar | `pray` = berdoa/salat | `late` = lembur/telat | `tonight` = malam ini
Tips praktis: Tolak telepon halus 1x pakai I'll be ....

#### Bagian 3: Timeline 2026-2027 Versimu
Penjelasan: Gabungkan: In June I'll be interning, by December I'll have finished, by 2027 I'll have worked a year. Interning (latar), have finished (kelar), have worked (durasi). Tiga future, satu timeline keren.

Di CV/interview, future perfect itu senjata: By July I will have completed the course. Kelar + tanggal = dipercaya. Perekrut suka kepastian.

Waspada won't have vs haven't: I won't have finished (nanti belum kelar, ramalan) vs I haven't finished (kini belum kelar, fakta). Won't = masa depan, haven't = kini. Jangan ketukar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `By 2027, I will have graduated and worked a year.` — 2027, aku sudah lulus dan kerja setahun. | `At 9 tomorrow, I'll be presenting my proposal.` — Besok jam 9, aku lagi presentasi proposal. | `We won't have paid off debt by June, sadly.` — Kami belum lunas utang Juni, sedihnya.
Kosakata baru (4): `graduate` = lulus | `intern` = magang | `proposal` = proposal | `debt` = utang
Tips praktis: Gambar timeline setahunmu 3 titik future.
Kuis:
1. By Friday, I ___ my report. | A) will have finished B) will finish always C) have finished yesterday D) finish | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Batas by masa depan memakai will have + V3.
2. At 8 tonight, I ___ dinner. | A) will have eaten B) will be cooking C) cook yesterday D) have cooked | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Latar berlangsung memakai will be + V-ing.
3. The road ___ by 2026. (pasif) | A) will have built B) will have been built C) will build D) builds | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Future perfect pasif memakai will have been + V3.

### en60 · B2 8/12 · Seandainya Saja: Wish + If Only
- Deskripsi: Sampaikan sesal kini, lampau, dan komplain masa depan dengan wish yang tepat tense.
- Durasi: 45 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Wish Kini Pakai Past
Penjelasan: I wish + past simple buat kini tak nyata: I wish I had a car (faktanya tidak punya kini). Past di sini penanda khayal, bukan lampau. Sama kayak conditional 2.

Orang Indonesia sering I wish I have... — have-nya harus had. Wish memaksa mundur satu tense. Wish kini = past, wish lampau = past perfect, wish future = would. Mundur kompak.

Bedakan hope (mungkin terjadi) vs wish (mustahil/kecil): I hope it rains (mungkin) vs I wish it rained now (kini tidak hujan, mustahil ubah). Hope = optimis, wish = ngarep.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I wish I spoke English fluently now.` — Andai aku lancar Inggris kini. | `I wish you were here with me.` — Andai kamu di sini bersamaku. | `She wishes she didn't work weekends.` — Dia berharap tidak kerja akhir pekan.
Kosakata baru (4): `wish` = berharap (khayal) | `fluently` = lancar | `weekend` = akhir pekan | `hope` = berharap (mungkin)
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 wish kini tentang skill/kotamu.

#### Bagian 2: Wish Lampau Pakai Had + V3
Penjelasan: I wish + past perfect buat sesal lampau: I wish I had studied (faktanya tidak belajar, gagal). Sama kayak conditional 3 tapi tanpa if. Sesal tok, tanpa syarat ditulis.

If only = wish versi dramatis: If only I had saved! If only he hadn't left! Tanda seru + had = nyesek maksimal. Dipakai di lagu dan diary, jarang di rapat.

Jangan wish + would untuk diri sendiri kini: I wish I would... salah. Would untuk orang lain/masa depan: I wish you would stop smoking (komplain, tolong berhenti!). Diri pakai past, orang pakai would.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I wish I hadn't skipped that interview.` — Andai tidak melewatkan interview itu. | `If only we had left earlier!` — Andai berangkat lebih awal! | `I wish I had bought that land in 2019.` — Andai membeli tanah itu 2019.
Kosakata baru (4): `skip` = melewatkan | `interview` = wawancara | `land` = tanah | `earlier` = lebih awal
Tips praktis: Tulis 1 sesal 2024 pakai wish + had.

#### Bagian 3: Wish Future: Would Buat Komplain
Penjelasan: I wish + would buat komplain/permintaan masa depan: I wish it would stop raining, I wish you would reply faster. Kesel tapi sopan. Would di sini harapan berubah, bukan khayalan mustahil.

Pakai di kos: I wish they'd turn down music, I wish the owner would fix the leak. They + would = tegur halus tanpa nunjuk muka. Tempel di grup WA kos, aman.

Tutup dengan aksi: But wishes don't work, so I'll... Wish + so + plan. Ngarep boleh, gerak wajib. I wish I were fit, so I'll jog thrice weekly. Arc dewasa.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I wish you would turn down the music.` — Andai kamu kecilkan musik. | `I wish the bus would come sooner.` — Andai bus datang lebih cepat. | `If only it would stop leaking!` — Andai berhenti bocor!
Kosakata baru (4): `turn down` = mengecilkan | `leak` = bocor | `reply` = membalas | `thrice` = tiga kali
Tips praktis: Komplain halus 1x pakai I wish ... would ....
Kuis:
1. I wish I ___ a car now. | A) have B) had C) will have D) have had | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Wish kini memakai past simple.
2. I wish I ___ harder last year. | A) studied B) had studied C) study D) will study | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Wish lampau memakai past perfect.
3. I wish you ___ smoking! (komplain) | A) would stop B) stopped now C) stop D) had stopped now | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Komplain masa depan memakai wish + would.

### en61 · B2 9/12 · Terlalu vs Cukup: So/Such/Too/Enough
- Deskripsi: Taklukkan so tired, such a day, too dark, enough money biar intensitasmu presisi.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: So + Sifat vs Such + Benda
Penjelasan: So + sifat/keterangan (so fast, so well), such + (a) + sifat + benda (such a fast car, such nice people). So pegang sifat, such pegang paket benda. Jangan so a car atau such fast tanpa benda.

So...that ada akibat: So hot that we stayed in. Such...that juga: Such heat that we hid. That + akibat bikin dramamu lengkap. Ujian suka minta lengkapi that-nya.

Orang Indonesia sering so very — pilih satu. So beautiful ATAU very beautiful, bukan so very beautiful. Double penguat kayak double helm, satu cukup.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `It was so crowded we couldn't move.` — Sangat ramai sampai tak bisa gerak. | `It was such a long queue!` — Antrean yang sangat panjang! | `She speaks so politely everyone likes her.` — Dia bicara sangat sopan semua suka.
Kosakata baru (4): `so` = sangat (sifat) | `such` = sangat (paket benda) | `crowded` = ramai | `politely` = sopan
Tips praktis: Ubah 3 very-mu jadi so/such hari ini.

#### Bagian 2: Too Negatif, Enough Positif
Penjelasan: Too + sifat (+ to) = kelewat, keluhan (too salty to eat). Sifat + enough (+ to) = cukup, pujian (salty enough? jarang, brave enough to try). Too di depan, enough di belakang. Posisi mutlak.

Pola: too ... to (tidak bisa), ... enough to (bisa). I was too sick to come (nggak datang) vs I was well enough to come (datang!). Satu to, beda nasib hadir.

Enough + benda juga bisa di depan: enough money, enough time. Cukup duit/waktu. Jangan too money — too tidak pegang benda langsung, butuh much/many: too much noise, too many people.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The soup is too salty to drink.` — Sopnya keasinan tak bisa diminum. | `Are you brave enough to eat ulat sagu?` — Beranikah makan ulat sagu? | `We have enough cash for two bowls.` — Duit cukup untuk dua mangkuk.
Kosakata baru (4): `too` = terlalu | `enough` = cukup | `brave` = berani | `cash` = tunai
Tips praktis: Komplain 1 too + syukuri 1 enough hari ini.

#### Bagian 3: Review Kuliner Pedas Level B2
Penjelasan: Gabungkan review: The sambal was so hot I cried, such a red color! It was too spicy for my friend, but spicy enough for me. So/such/too/enough satu paragraf, review-mu Michelin level kos.

Hindari very...very... — variasi so/such + kata kuat (scorching, freezing, starving). Very hot → scorching. Satu kata ekstrem kalahkan dua kata lemah.

Tutup rekomendasi: If you love heat, it's perfect. If not, order the mild one. If + saran = review membantu, bukan sekadar pamer tahan pedas.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `It was such a tasty martabak we ordered twice!` — Martabak yang sangat enak sampai pesan dua kali! | `Too oily for me, but my brother loves it.` — Terlalu berminyak bagiku, tapi kakak suka. | `Just spicy enough, not too mild.` — Pedasnya pas, tidak terlalu ringan.
Kosakata baru (4): `oily` = berminyak | `mild` = ringan (rasa) | `tasty` = lezat | `scorching` = menyengat
Tips praktis: Review 1 makanan pakai 3 pola intensitas.
Kuis:
1. It was ___ beautiful day! | A) so B) such a C) too D) enough | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Paket benda memakai such a.
2. I'm too tired ___ . | A) to walk B) walking C) walk D) walked | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Too + sifat + to-infinitive.
3. Are you tall ___ to ride it? | A) too B) so C) enough D) such | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Cukup memakai sifat + enough + to.

### en62 · B2 10/12 · Butuh Dibenahi: Need V-ing + Causative Lanjutan
- Deskripsi: Minta servis AC dan motor dengan need repairing dan get/have something done yang natural.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Need Repairing = Perlu Diperbaiki
Penjelasan: Need + V-ing itu pasif terselubung: The AC needs fixing (= needs to be fixed). My hair needs cutting. Praktis buat lapor kos: The roof needs painting, the drain needs cleaning. Singkat dan sopan.

Orang Indonesia sering The AC need to fix — subjeknya AC, bukan kamu. AC butuh diperbaiki (pasif), bukan memperbaiki (aktif). Need fixing / needs to be fixed, bukan need to fix.

Bedakan need to + V1 (kamu yang aksi: I need to fix it) vs need + V-ing (bendanya yang diperlakukan: It needs fixing). Pelakunya siapa? Kamu vs tukang. Beda subjek, beda pola.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `My motorbike needs servicing soon.` — Motorku perlu diservis segera. | `The bathroom needs cleaning, it stinks!` — Kamar mandi perlu dibersihkan, bau! | `Your shoes need polishing for the interview.` — Sepatumu perlu disemir buat interview.
Kosakata baru (4): `need` = perlu | `service` = servis | `stink` = bau | `polish` = semir
Tips praktis: Lapor 2 need -ing kerusakan kosmu.

#### Bagian 2: Get It Done vs Have It Done
Penjelasan: Have + objek + V3 formal (I had my laptop repaired), get + objek + V3 santai (I got my hair dyed). Artinya sama: orang lain yang kerjakan. Pilih have ke atasan, get ke teman.

Tense ikut have/get: I have it cleaned weekly (rutin), I'm having it cleaned now (lagi jalan), I had it cleaned yesterday (lampau). Have/get yang ganti baju, V3 awet.

Kesalahan umum: I cut my hair yesterday untuk ke barber — kesannya potong sendiri pakai gunting dapur. Tambahkan had/got biar jelas ke barber: I had it cut. Selamat rambutmu dari salah paham.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I got my phone screen replaced.` — Layar HP-ku diganti (tukang). | `We're having the house painted green.` — Kami sedang mengecat rumah hijau (tukang). | `She had her documents printed.` — Dokumennya dicetak (tukang cetak).
Kosakata baru (4): `replace` = mengganti | `paint` = mengecat | `document` = dokumen | `screen` = layar
Tips praktis: Daftar 3 jasa langgananmu versi have/get.

#### Bagian 3: Komplain Kos 3 Langkah
Penjelasan: Template komplain: problem need -ing (The fan needs fixing), request causative (Could we have it repaired?), deadline (by Friday?). Problem-request-deadline. Tiga langkah, owner susah nolak.

Sopan santun: Could you please...? We'd appreciate it if... Appreciate + -ing/V3 itu bahasa rapat. We'd appreciate having it fixed soon. Halus tapi nancep.

Tutup follow-up: Please let me know when it's done. Let me know = kabari aku. Tanpa follow-up, komplainmu menguap kayak janji diet.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The WiFi needs upgrading, it's so slow.` — WiFi perlu di-upgrade, lambat banget. | `Could we have the leak fixed this week?` — Bisakah bocornya diperbaiki minggu ini? | `Please let me know the cost first.` — Tolong kabari biayanya dulu.
Kosakata baru (4): `upgrade` = meningkatkan | `leak` = bocor | `cost` = biaya | `appreciate` = menghargai
Tips praktis: Tulis 1 komplain kos 3 langkah Inggris.
Kuis:
1. The AC needs ___. | A) fix B) fixing C) to fix it D) fixed by me | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Bendanya diperlakukan memakai need + V-ing.
2. I had my hair ___. | A) cut B) cutting C) to cut D) cuts | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Causative memakai have + objek + V3.
3. We're ___ the roof painted. | A) have B) having C) has D) had yesterday now | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Lagi berlangsung memakai be + having + objek + V3.

### en63 · B2 11/12 · Nyambung Ngobrol: Discourse Markers Speaking
- Deskripsi: Beli waktu mikir dan pindah topik dengan well, anyway, actually yang natural.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Conversation | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Well, Actually, You Know Buat Pembuka
Penjelasan: Well buat jeda mikir (Well, I think...), actually buat koreksi halus (Actually, it was Tuesday), you know buat ajak setuju (It's pricey, you know?). Tiga ini bikin speaking-mu tidak robotik.

Orang Indonesia sering silent 5 detik saat blank — ganti dengan Well, that's interesting... / Let me think... Dua detik bersuara lebih baik dari lima detik mati. Penguji menilai fluency dari sini.

Hindari eee panjang — ganti well/so. Eee 10 detik nurunin skor, well 2 detik naikin kesan. Sama-sama ngulur, beda kelas.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Well, I have never thought about it.` — Hmm, belum pernah mikir itu. | `Actually, I prefer buses to bikes.` — Sebenarnya, aku lebih suka bus. | `It's far, you know? So I leave early.` — Jauh, kamu tahu kan? Jadi berangkat awal.
Kosakata baru (4): `well` = hmm (pembuka) | `actually` = sebenarnya | `you know` = kamu tahu kan | `prefer` = lebih suka
Tips praktis: Rekam 1 menit opini diawali Well,....

#### Bagian 2: Anyway, So, Right Buat Pindah & Tutup
Penjelasan: Anyway buat balik ke topik (Anyway, about the price...), so buat simpulkan (So, we agree?), right? buat cek paham (You're from Solo, right?). Tiga penutup/pindah yang wajib di debat dan test.

By the way buat selipan (By the way, nice shirt!), after all buat alasan akhir (I stayed, after all it was raining). After all = toh juga. Kecil tapi bikin argumenmu berlapis.

Kesalahan umum: anyway diartikan bagaimanapun formal — di speaking anyway = ngomong-ngomong/balik lagi. Anyway guys, let's order! Santai, bukan esai.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Anyway, let's get back to the plan.` — Ngomong-ngomong, balik ke rencana. | `So, shall we split the bill?` — Jadi, patungan ya? | `You're joining tomorrow, right?` — Kamu ikut besok, kan?
Kosakata baru (4): `anyway` = ngomong-ngomong | `so` = jadi (simpulan) | `right` = kan (cek) | `split` = patungan
Tips praktis: Tutup 2 chat-mu hari ini pakai so/right.

#### Bagian 3: Setuju-Tidak Setuju Versi B2
Penjelasan: Naik dari A2: I see where you're coming from, but... / That's true to some extent, however... / I'm afraid I disagree because... Ada acknowledgment + kontras + alasan. Dewasa dan sopan.

Tambahkan as I said, in other words buat tegaskan: As I said, buses save money. In other words, bikes cost more. Parafrase = pamer vocab + perjelas. Penguji suka.

Kalau mentok, minta klarifikasi: Sorry, do you mean...? Could you clarify...? Minta dijelasin itu strategi, bukan nyerah. Lebih baik dari ngarang ngalor-ngidul.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I see your point, but safety matters more.` — Paham maksudmu, tapi keselamatan lebih penting. | `In other words, cheap isn't always safe.` — Dengan kata lain, murah tak selalu aman. | `Sorry, could you clarify the last point?` — Maaf, bisa jelaskan poin terakhir?
Kosakata baru (4): `clarify` = memperjelas | `extent` = batas | `matter` = penting | `afraid` = khawatir (sopan)
Tips praktis: Debat 3 menit pakai 4 markers beda.
Kuis:
1. ___ , I haven't decided yet. (beli waktu) | A) Well B) Anyway bye C) Silent 10s D) Eeee long | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Well membeli waktu secara natural.
2. ___ , back to the topic. | A) Actually always B) Anyway C) So long D) Right now dog | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Anyway untuk kembali ke topik.
3. You're from Jogja, ___? | A) isn't it B) right C) anyway D) actually | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Right untuk cek pemahaman santai.

### en64 · B2 12/12 · Phrasal Verb Pisah vs Nemplek
- Deskripsi: Bedakan turn off the light vs look after mom (separable vs inseparable) biar nggak salah selip objek.
- Durasi: 40 menit | Tag: Vocab | Level: B2

#### Bagian 1: Separable: Objek Bisa Nyelip
Penjelasan: Separable boleh turn off the TV ATAU turn the TV off. Objek (the TV/it) bisa di tengah atau belakang. Tapi kalau pronoun (it/them), wajib tengah: turn it off (bukan turn off it!). Pronoun nyelip wajib.

Daftar separable wajib: turn on/off, put on/off, pick up, drop off, fill in, throw away, bring back. Tujuh ini langganan TOEIC + hidup sehari-hari.

Orang Indonesia sering turn off it — it di belakang itu fatal. It/them itu manja, maunya di tengah dipeluk dua kata. Turn it off, pick me up. Hafalkan!

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Please turn off the lights before you go.` — Tolong matikan lampu sebelum pergi. | `Turn it off, it's too loud!` — Matikan, terlalu keras! | `I'll pick you up at seven.` — Aku jemput kamu jam 7.
Kosakata baru (4): `turn off` = mematikan | `pick up` = menjemput/mengambil | `drop off` = mengantar turun | `throw away` = membuang
Tips praktis: Ubah 5 benda di kamarmu jadi turn it off versi.

#### Bagian 2: Inseparable: Jangan Diselipi
Penjelasan: Inseparable tidak bisa diselip: look after mom (bukan look mom after), run into Budi (bukan run Budi into), get over flu. Preposisi nempel ke verb, objek di belakang. Bongkar = salah.

Daftar inseparable: look after, get over, run into, look forward to, take care of, come across, go through. To/after/of nempel, jangan digeser.

Look forward to + -ing! I look forward to meeting you (bukan to meet). To di sini preposisi, bukan infinitive. Sama kayak be used to. To preposisi = -ing.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Who looks after your cat at home?` — Siapa merawat kucingmu di rumah? | `I ran into an old teacher at the market.` — Aku papasan guru lama di pasar. | `I look forward to hearing from you.` — Kutunggu kabarmu (formal).
Kosakata baru (4): `look after` = merawat | `run into` = papasan | `get over` = pulih dari | `come across` = menemukan tak sengaja
Tips praktis: Tulis 3 inseparable tentang minggu lalumu.

#### Bagian 3: 3 Kata: Put Up With, Get Rid Of
Penjelasan: Three-word tidak bisa diutak-atik: put up with (tahan), get rid of (buang), look forward to (nantikan), come up with (hasilkan ide). Urutan mati, objek di belakang semua. I can't put up with noise. Buang? Get rid of it (it di belakang, bukan tengah!).

Bedakan put off (tunda, separable: put it off) vs put up with (tahan, inseparable total). Put + off vs put + up + with. Satu kata beda nasib. Ujian suka sandingkan keduanya.

Latihan email: I look forward to working with you. Formal + -ing + with. Satu kalimat, tiga aturan kepakai. Nilai plus.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I can't put up with the smoke here.` — Aku tak tahan asap di sini. | `Let's get rid of old newspapers.` — Buang koran lama yuk. | `She came up with a brilliant idea!` — Dia hasilkan ide cemerlang!
Kosakata baru (4): `put up with` = mentolerir | `get rid of` = membuang | `come up with` = menghasilkan | `brilliant` = cemerlang
Tips praktis: Keluhkan 1 hal pakai can't put up with ....
Kuis:
1. Turn ___! (matikan itu) | A) off it B) it off C) offing it D) it offing | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Pronoun wajib di tengah separable.
2. She looks ___ her grandmother. | A) after B) her after C) up after D) after her after | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Look after inseparable, objek di belakang.
3. I look forward ___ you. | A) to meet B) to meeting C) meeting to D) meet | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Look forward to + V-ing.

### Level C1

### en29 · C1 1/8 · Gaya Panggung: Inversion Biar Nendang
- Deskripsi: Balik struktur kalimat dengan never, rarely, not only buat penekanan tingkat esai band 7+.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: Never Have I Seen yang Bikin Penguji Nooleh
Penjelasan: Inversion itu efek panggung: "Never have I seen such traffic" jauh lebih nendang dari "I have never seen...". Rumusnya: kata negatif di depan + aux + subjek + kata kerja. Kebalik dari biasa, makanya nempel di kepala pembaca.

Orang Indonesia sering lupa aux-nya: "Never I have seen" — salah, aux harus sebelum subjek: Never have I. Anggap never itu magnet yang narik aux ke depan.

Paket favorit: Never, Rarely, Seldom, Not only, No sooner, Hardly. Hafalkan enam, variasikan di esai. Satu inversion per esai cukup, kebanyakan malah norak.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Never have I seen such a long queue.` — Belum pernah aku melihat antrean sepanjang itu. | `Rarely does the bus arrive on time.` — Jarang bus tiba tepat waktu. | `Not only is it cheap, but it is also fast.` — Tidak hanya murah, tapi juga cepat.
Kosakata baru (4): `queue` = antrean | `rarely` = jarang | `seldom` = jarang | `nendang` = striking (slang: impactful)
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 kalimat never-mu jadi inversion hari ini.

#### Bagian 2: Not Only ... But Also yang Elegan
Penjelasan: Pasangan ini langganan skor tinggi: "Not only does it save money, but it also saves time". Dua manfaat sekali pukul. Inversion-nya di klausa pertama saja, klausa but-nya normal.

Jebakan paralelisme: setelah not only dan but also harus sejenis. "Not only saving money but also time" vs "Not only does it save money but it also saves time". Jangan campur aduk jenis katanya.

Di speaking, pakai versi santai tanpa inversion juga oke. Tapi di writing Task 2, versi inversion bikin kamu kelihatan C1 beneran.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Not only did he miss the flight, but he also lost his bag.` — Tidak hanya ketinggalan pesawat, dia juga kehilangan tasnya. | `Not only is Jakarta crowded, but it is also expensive.` — Jakarta tidak hanya padat, tapi juga mahal. | `Not only should you sleep early, but you should also eat well.` — Tidak hanya harus tidur cepat, kamu juga harus makan baik.
Kosakata baru (4): `crowded` = padat | `expensive` = mahal | `miss` = melewatkan | `flight` = penerbangan
Tips praktis: Tulis satu kalimat not only tentang kotamu versi inversion.

#### Bagian 3: No Sooner, Hardly, Scarcely Buat Cerita Dramatis
Penjelasan: Tiga frasa ini buat adegan film: "No sooner had I sat down than it rained", "Hardly had we left when the phone rang". Artinya: baru saja ... eh ... Kejadian nempel ketat.

Rumusnya: No sooner/hardly/scarcely + had + subjek + V3 + than/when. Than untuk no sooner, when untuk hardly/scarcely. Ketukar than/when langsung salah.

Orang Indonesia suka pakai when semua — hafalkan pasangannya: no sooner-than (sama-sama ada o dan t-nya biar gampang ingat). Kecil tapi nyelametin.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `No sooner had I paid than the price dropped.` — Baru saja aku membayar, harganya turun. | `Hardly had we started when the power died.` — Baru saja kami mulai, listrik mati. | `Scarcely had she slept when the baby cried.` — Baru saja dia tidur, bayinya menangis.
Kosakata baru (4): `drop` = turun | `power` = listrik | `cry` = menangis | `pay` = membayar
Tips praktis: Ceritakan satu apesmu kemarin pakai no sooner/hardly.
Kuis:
1. ___ have I tasted such good soto. | A) Never B) Always C) Often D) Usually | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Inversion memakai kata negatif di depan + aux + subjek.
2. Not only ___ cheap, but it is also durable. | A) it is B) is it C) it D) is | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Not only di depan memicu inversion is it.
3. No sooner had I left ___ it rained. | A) when B) than C) then D) that | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Pasangan tetap no sooner adalah than.

### en30 · C1 2/8 · Sorotan Panggung: Cleft Sentence
- Deskripsi: Tonjolkan pelaku, waktu, atau alasan dengan it-cleft dan what-cleft yang presisi.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: It Is ... That/ Who Buat Sorotan
Penjelasan: Kalau mau nyorot satu unsur, belah kalimatnya: "My mother cooked rendang" → "It was my mother who cooked rendang" (yang masak ibuku, bukan orang lain). Fokusnya pindah total ke mother.

Orang Indonesia sering "It is my mother who cook" — lupa tense-nya ngikut aslinya. Kalau kejadiannya lampau, was + cooked. Cleft nggak mengubah waktu, cuma mengubah lampu sorot.

Pakai that untuk benda/waktu/alasan, who untuk orang. "It was yesterday that we met", "It was because of rain that we failed". Fleksibel banget.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `It is discipline that makes athletes win.` — Disiplinlah yang membuat atlet menang. | `It was in 2020 that I started coding.` — Pada 2020-lah aku mulai ngoding. | `It was Rani who lent me the money.` — Rani-lah yang meminjamkan uang padaku.
Kosakata baru (4): `discipline` = disiplin | `athlete` = atlet | `lend` = meminjamkan | `win` = menang
Tips praktis: Sorot satu fakta keluargamu pakai it was ... that.

#### Bagian 2: What-Cleft Buat Alasan dan Aksi
Penjelasan: What-cleft itu buat nyorot aksi/alasan: "What I need is sleep" (yang kubutuhkan adalah tidur). Rasanya filosofis, cocok buat pembuka paragraf kesimpulan.

Rumusnya: What + klausa + is/was + fokus. "What caused the delay was the flood". Jangan pakai what untuk benda spesifik orang — itu tugasnya it-cleft.

Kesalahan umum: "What I need is sleeping" — setelah is pakai kata benda/infinitive, bukan gerund sendirian. What I need is sleep / to sleep. Pilih yang benda biar aman.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `What bothers me is the noise.` — Yang menggangguku adalah kebisingan. | `What we should do is plant more trees.` — Yang harus kita lakukan adalah menanam lebih banyak pohon. | `What happened was unexpected.` — Yang terjadi tak terduga.
Kosakata baru (4): `bother` = mengganggu | `noise` = kebisingan | `plant` = menanam | `unexpected` = tak terduga
Tips praktis: Tulis satu keluhan kosanmu diawali What bothers me is ....

#### Bagian 3: Kapan Cleft Bikin Skor Naik?
Penjelasan: Cleft itu bumbu, bukan nasi. Satu-dua per esai di thesis atau topic sentence sudah cukup buat nunjukin range. "It is the government that must act" di pembuka langsung kerasa tegas.

Di speaking Part 3, cleft bikin jawabanmu terstruktur: "What matters most is education". Satu kalimat, penguji langsung catat kamu C1. Nggak perlu banyak, perlu tepat.

Hindari cleft berlapis: "It is what I think that..." — ruwet dan rawan salah. Satu sorotan per kalimat, itu aturan mainnya.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `It is education that changes villages.` — Pendidikanlah yang mengubah desa. | `What Indonesia needs is better trains.` — Yang Indonesia butuhkan adalah kereta lebih baik. | `It was not money that made him happy.` — Bukan uang yang membuatnya bahagia.
Kosakata baru (4): `education` = pendidikan | `village` = desa | `need` = membutuhkan | `happy` = bahagia
Tips praktis: Tulis ulang thesis esaimu versi it-cleft.
Kuis:
1. ___ was Andi who fixed my laptop. | A) This B) It C) What D) That | Jawaban: B | Alasan: It-cleft untuk menyorot orang memakai It was ... who.
2. What I need ___ more practice. | A) are B) is C) am D) be | Jawaban: B | Alasan: What-cleft memakai is untuk fokus benda.
3. It was yesterday ___ we signed the contract. | A) who B) what C) that D) whose | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Penanda waktu dalam it-cleft memakai that.

### en31 · C1 3/8 · Dari Kata Kerja ke Kata Benda: Nominalisation & Hedging
- Deskripsi: Ubah decide→decision dan jinakkan always dengan tends to biar nada akademismu pas.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: Nominalisation Biar Formal
Penjelasan: Akademik suka kata benda: decide→decision, develop→development, fail→failure. "The decision to delay was wise" kedengeran jauh lebih formal dari "They decided to delay and it was wise". Padat dan dewasa.

Orang Indonesia sering kalimatnya kepanjangan beranak: "Because the government decided to..." — nominalkan: "Due to the government's decision to...". Satu frasa benda gantikan satu klausa.

Hafalkan akhiran: -tion, -ment, -ness, -ity, -ance. Lima itu saja sudah buka puluhan kata formal. "Aware→awareness" langsung naik kelas.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The construction of the dam took five years.` — Pembangunan bendungan memakan lima tahun. | `There has been a reduction in poverty.` — Telah terjadi penurunan kemiskinan. | `Her argument was well-structured.` — Argumennya tersusun rapi.
Kosakata baru (4): `construction` = pembangunan | `reduction` = penurunan | `argument` = argumen | `wise` = bijak
Tips praktis: Nominalkan 3 kata kerja di esaimu kemarin.

#### Bagian 2: Hedging Biar Nggak Sok Pasti
Penjelasan: Akademik benci klaim mutlak. Ganti "Smoking causes cancer, all smokers die" dengan "Smoking tends to increase risk, many smokers may develop issues". Ada tends to, may, many — aman dan ilmiah.

Senjata hedging: tend to, seem to, appear to, may/might/could, often, in many cases, suggests that. Tabur ini, tulisanmu langsung kerasa S2.

Orang Indonesia suka always, never, all, 100% — musuh skor C1. Coret kata mutlak, ganti probabilitas. Penguji IELTS langsung senyum.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Social media tends to affect teenagers more strongly.` — Medsos cenderung memengaruhi remaja lebih kuat. | `This policy may reduce congestion in some areas.` — Kebijakan ini mungkin mengurangi macet di beberapa area. | `Evidence suggests that sleep improves memory.` — Bukti menunjukkan tidur meningkatkan memori.
Kosakata baru (4): `tend to` = cenderung | `affect` = memengaruhi | `policy` = kebijakan | `evidence` = bukti
Tips praktis: Coret always/never di esaimu, ganti tends to/often.

#### Bagian 3: Paragraf Akademik Rasa Jurnal
Penjelasan: Gabungkan keduanya: "The implementation of free buses led to a reduction in traffic. Evidence suggests commuters tend to switch when service is reliable." Dua kalimat, tiga nominalisasi, dua hedging. Ini rasa C1.

Struktur: klaim hedged → bukti nominal → contoh → implikasi. Nggak perlu panjang, perlu padat. 5 kalimat padat kalahkan 10 kalimat ngalor-ngidul.

Tutup dengan saran hedged: "It would therefore seem advisable to pilot the scheme". Sopan, formal, nggak ngotot. Itulah nada akademik.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The expansion of rail links seems advisable.` — Perluasan jalur kereta tampaknya bijak. | `Investment in clean water often yields high returns.` — Investasi air bersih sering memberi hasil tinggi. | `Failure to act may worsen flooding.` — Gagal bertindak mungkin memperparah banjir.
Kosakata baru (4): `expansion` = perluasan | `investment` = investasi | `yield` = menghasilkan | `worsen` = memperparah
Tips praktis: Tulis ulang satu paragraf B2-mu versi nominal + hedging.
Kuis:
1. Bentuk nominal dari decide adalah ___. | A) deciding B) decision C) decided D) decisive | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Nominalisasi decide adalah decision.
2. Kalimat paling hedged: ___. | A) All teens are addicted B) Teens tend to be vulnerable C) Teens never study D) All phones destroy brains | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Tend to menunjukkan kehati-hatian akademik.
3. Ganti kata kerja menjadi benda: They developed the village → ___ of the village. | A) The development B) They developing C) Develop D) Developing | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Nominalisasi develop adalah development.

### en32 · C1 4/8 · Kolokasi dan Reading N1-nya IELTS
- Deskripsi: Bedakan make/do, say/tell versi C1 dan taklukkan bacaan panjang dengan skimming presisi.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Reading | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: Kolokasi: Pasangan Kata yang Nggak Bisa Selingkuh
Penjelasan: Bahasa Inggris itu setia: make a mistake (bukan do), do homework (bukan make), heavy rain (bukan big rain), strong coffee (bukan powerful coffee). Salah pasangan langsung ketahuan non-native.

Orang Indonesia nerjemahkan "hujan besar" jadi big rain — harusnya heavy rain. "Minum obat" jadi drink medicine — harusnya take medicine. Kolokasi memang harus hafal, nggak bisa logika.

Fokus ke keluarga make/do/take/have/get + kata benda. 30 kolokasi inti ini muncul di mana-mana dari TOEIC sampai IELTS.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I made a serious mistake on the form.` — Aku membuat kesalahan serius di formulir. | `Take your medicine after meals.` — Minum obatmu setelah makan. | `Heavy rain delayed the match.` — Hujan deras menunda pertandingan.
Kosakata baru (4): `mistake` = kesalahan | `medicine` = obat | `heavy` = lebat/deras | `delay` = menunda
Tips praktis: Catat 5 kolokasi baru tiap baca artikel Inggris.

#### Bagian 2: Idiom Netral vs Gaul, Pilih Arena
Penjelasan: Di C1 bedakan idiom netral ujian (play a key role, bring into question) vs gaul (piece of cake, raining cats and dogs — jangan pakai di esai formal!). Salah arena bikin skor turun.

Orang Indonesia suka tabur idiom gaul di esai biar kerasa pinter — justru nurunin formalitas. Simpan piece of cake buat speaking santai, pakai play a vital role buat writing.

Hafalkan 10 idiom akademik: come into play, give rise to, in the long run, on the brink of, take into account. Lima ini saja sudah dongkrak lexical resource.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Cheap fuel gives rise to more traffic.` — BBM murah memicu lebih banyak lalu lintas. | `Take age into account before judging.` — Pertimbangkan usia sebelum menilai. | `In the long run, education pays off.` — Jangka panjang, pendidikan membuahkan hasil.
Kosakata baru (4): `role` = peran | `judge` = menilai | `pay off` = membuahkan hasil | `account` = pertimbangan
Tips praktis: Ganti satu kata gaul di esaimu dengan idiom formal.

#### Bagian 3: Skimming + Scanning Bacaan 800 Kata
Penjelasan: Bacaan C1 itu maraton 800 kata. Strateginya: baca judul + kalimat pertama tiap paragraf (skimming 1 menit), baca soal, baru buru detail (scanning). Jangan baca linear dari kata 1 sampai 800.

Sinyal soal: however (kontras), therefore (akibat), for example (contoh), in contrast (perbandingan). Garisbawahi ini, jawaban biasanya di sekitarnya.

Jebakan True/False/Not Given: False = teks bilang sebaliknya, Not Given = teks nggak bahas. Kalau nggak ketemu buktinya setelah scanning 2 menit, jawab Not Given dan move on.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Skim the topic sentences first.` — Pindai kalimat topik dulu. | `Underline contrast words like however.` — Garisbawahi kata kontras seperti however. | `If no evidence is found, answer Not Given.` — Jika tak ada bukti, jawab Not Given.
Kosakata baru (4): `skim` = memindai cepat | `contrast` = kontras | `evidence` = bukti | `answer` = jawaban
Tips praktis: Latih satu artikel 800 kata dengan timer 15 menit.
Kuis:
1. Pasangan kolokasi benar: ___. | A) big rain B) heavy rain C) strong rain D) large rain | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Hujan deras berkolokasi dengan heavy.
2. Idiom formal untuk esai: ___. | A) raining cats and dogs B) play a key role C) piece of cake D) cool as cucumber | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Play a key role netral-formal cocok untuk esai.
3. Jika teks tidak membahas info soal, jawab ___. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Tanpa bukti di teks jawabannya Not Given.

### en65 · C1 5/8 · Padat Karya: Participle Clauses
- Deskripsi: Pangkas which was boring jadi boring dengan klausa partisipel biar tulisanmu padat jurnal.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: V-ing Gantikan Which/When Aktif
Penjelasan: Daripada The man who was wearing batik helped me, pangkas jadi The man wearing batik helped me. Who was dibuang, wearing maju. Sama arti, 2 kata hemat. Akademik suka hemat.

Aturannya: subjek sama, aktif → V-ing. Opening the door, she smiled (= When she opened...). Folding clothes, I listen to podcast. Dua aksi satu pelaku, gabung V-ing.

Orang Indonesia sering Having opened... untuk semua — having + V3 hanya untuk duluan selesai: Having finished homework, I slept. Kalau bareng, V-ing biasa cukup. Having itu penanda senioritas.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Walking home, I saw a stunning sunset.` — Berjalan pulang, aku lihat senja menawan. | `Having saved for a year, we bought a laptop.` — Setelah menabung setahun, kami beli laptop. | `The students wearing uniforms are from Aceh.` — Murid beruniform itu dari Aceh.
Kosakata baru (4): `stunning` = menawan | `fold` = melipat | `uniform` = seragam | `podcast` = siniar
Tips praktis: Pangkas 3 who/which-mu jadi V-ing hari ini.

#### Bagian 2: V3 Gantikan Which Pasif
Penjelasan: Untuk pasif, pakai V3: The phone made in China is cheap (= which was made...). Built in 1920, the mosque still stands. Made/built pangkas which was. Padat dan elegan.

Jangan gantung (dangling): Walking home, the rain started — siapa jalan? Hujan? Subjek dua klausa harus sama. Yang benar: Walking home, I got wet. Aku yang jalan, aku yang basah. Logis.

Dangling favorit Indonesia: Being cheap, I bought it (yang cheap siapa? Aku? Barangnya?). Harusnya Being cheap, the shirt was bought. Cek subjek sebelum pangkas!

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Written in 2020, this book predicts AI well.` — Ditulis 2020, buku ini prediksi AI tepat. | `Exhausted after work, she slept early.` — Kelelahan usai kerja, dia tidur cepat. | `Located near campus, the kos is always full.` — Terletak dekat kampus, kos selalu penuh.
Kosakata baru (4): `located` = terletak | `predict` = memprediksi | `exhausted` = kelelahan | `still` = masih
Tips praktis: Cek 2 kalimat -ing-mu: subjeknya sama?

#### Bagian 3: Perfect Participle Buat Sebab-Akibat
Penjelasan: Having + V3 buat sebab duluan: Having missed the bus, I ran. Having eaten, we left. Sebabnya kelar dulu, akibatnya nyusul. Rapi dan formal.

Di esai, ini variasi cohesive device selain because: Having limited funds, the city piloted one route. Funds terbatas duluan, pilot nyusul. Formal tanpa because melulu.

Tutup dengan review: Having read this module, you can compress sentences. Kamu sudah baca, kini bisa memadatkan. Having + V3 + klaim. Meta tapi benar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Having finished the report, I sent it.` — Setelah selesaikan laporan, kukirim. | `Not having cash, we paid by QRIS.` — Karena tak bawa tunai, kami bayar QRIS. | `Having been delayed twice, the flight was cancelled.` — Setelah delay dua kali, penerbangan batal.
Kosakata baru (4): `fund` = dana | `compress` = memadatkan | `delay` = tunda | `cancel` = batal
Tips praktis: Gabung 2 kalimat sebab-akibatmu pakai Having ....
Kuis:
1. ___ home, I saw sunset. (aku yang jalan) | A) Walking B) Walked C) Walks D) To walk | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Subjek sama aktif memakai V-ing.
2. The phone ___ in China is cheap. | A) making B) made C) makes D) to make | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Pasif terpangkas memakai V3.
3. ___ finished, I slept. | A) Have B) Having C) Has D) Had been always | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Sebab duluan memakai Having + V3.

### en66 · C1 6/8 · Masa Depan di Masa Lalu: Future in the Past
- Deskripsi: Ceritakan rencana gagal dan janji lampau dengan was going to dan would yang bernas.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: Was Going To Buat Rencana Gagal
Penjelasan: I was going to call, but my credit ran out. Rencananya ada, realisasinya gagal. Was/were + going to + verb = niat lampau yang kandas. Drama + grammar.

Bedakan dengan used to: used to = kebiasaan lama (I used to swim), was going to = rencana sekali yang gagal (I was going to swim yesterday, but rain). Satu rutin, satu insiden. Jangan tukar.

Orang Indonesia sering I will go yesterday — will untuk lampau itu mustahil. Lampau pakai was going to / would. Will steril dari masa lalu.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I was going to join the trip, but I got sick.` — Aku mau ikut trip, tapi sakit. | `They were going to marry in June, but postponed.` — Mereka mau nikah Juni, tapi tunda. | `We were just about to eat when guests came.` — Kami baru mau makan saat tamu datang.
Kosakata baru (4): `postpone` = menunda | `guest` = tamu | `credit` = pulsa | `just about to` = baru mau
Tips praktis: Tulis 1 rencana gagalmu pakai was going to.

#### Bagian 2: Would Buat Janji & Prediksi Lampau
Penjelasan: Would dua job di lampau: janji (He said he would help = katanya mau bantu) dan prediksi (I thought it would rain). Said/thought lampau + would = future in the past. Reported + future digabung.

Bedakan would rutin lampau (Every night he'd read = dulu tiap malam baca, = used to) vs would janji (He would come tomorrow (diucapkan kemarin) = akan datang). Konteks kemarin vs dulu rutin beda.

Was about to = mau banget detik itu: I was about to leave when... About to lebih mepet dari going to. Going to = rencana jam/hari, about to = detik. Presisi detik.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `She promised she would pay Friday.` — Dia janji akan bayar Jumat (diucapkan lampau). | `I knew the price would drop soon.` — Aku tahu harga akan turun segera. | `He would always bring sate when visiting.` — Dulu dia selalu bawa sate saat berkunjung (rutin).
Kosakata baru (4): `promise` = berjanji | `drop` = turun | `visit` = berkunjung | `soon` = segera
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 janji temanmu jadi would versi reported.

#### Bagian 3: Narasi Kegagalan Elegan
Penjelasan: Gabungkan: We were going to launch in March. We thought sales would soar. But supply failed, so we postponed. Rencana-prediksi-gagal-tunda. Empat langkah, narasi startup gagal yang elegan.

Di IELTS Task 1 (grafik lampau), future in the past jarang dipakai — simpan buat Task 2 / Speaking Part 3 refleksi. In hindsight, we were going to... Hindsight = melihat ke belakang. Frasa C1.

Hindari would have di sini — would have itu conditional 3 (khayal lampau), was going to itu rencana real yang gagal. Real vs khayal beda. I was going to go (real, gagal) vs If I'd gone, I'd have... (khayal).

Contoh (3 kalimat): `In hindsight, we were going to expand too fast.` — Melihat ke belakang, kami mau ekspansi terlalu cepat. | `I expected the rain would stop by noon.` — Kukira hujan akan berhenti siang. | `The plan was about to fail when help arrived.` — Rencana hampir gagal saat bantuan tiba.
Kosakata baru (4): `hindsight` = melihat ke belakang | `expand` = memperluas | `expect` = mengira | `supply` = pasokan
Tips praktis: Ceritakan 1 proyek gagal 3 kalimat was going to/would.
Kuis:
1. I ___ call you, but credit ran out. | A) will B) was going to C) am going to D) go to | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Rencana lampau gagal memakai was going to.
2. She said she ___ help. | A) will B) would C) is D) has | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Future in the past reported memakai would.
3. Every night he ___ read to us. (rutin lampau) | A) would B) will C) is going D) has | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Kebiasaan lampau bisa memakai would + kata kerja.

### en67 · C1 7/8 · Hemat Kata: Ellipsis & Substitution
- Deskripsi: Jawab singkat ala native dengan so do I, neither can I, dan potongan kalimat yang rapi.
- Durasi: 45 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: So Do I, Neither Do I Biar Kompak
Penjelasan: Setuju positif: So do I (= I also...), setuju negatif: Neither/Nor do I (= I don't either). I love rendang → So do I. I don't like durian → Neither do I. Aux ngikut tense: So did I (lampau), So can I (modal). Cermin aux-nya!

Orang Indonesia sering So I do / Neither I do — urutan kebalik. So/neither + aux + subjek, bukan subjek + aux. Aux di depan subjek kayak inversion mini. So do I, bukan so I do.

Either vs neither: I don't either (di belakang negatif biasa) = Neither do I (di depan inversion). Dua-duanya benar, neither lebih formal. Pilih sesuai panggung.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I have finished. So has she.` — Aku sudah selesai. Dia juga. | `I can't swim. Neither can my brother.` — Aku tak bisa renang. Kakakku juga tidak. | `I went to Bromo. So did they!` — Aku ke Bromo. Mereka juga!
Kosakata baru (4): `either` = juga (negatif belakang) | `neither` = juga tidak (depan) | `aux` = kata bantu | `agree` = setuju
Tips praktis: Setujui 3 status temanmu pakai So/Neither.

#### Bagian 2: Potong yang Sudah Jelas (Ellipsis)
Penjelasan: Ellipsis itu buang yang sudah jelas: I ordered coffee and Dina (ordered) tea. Want some? (Do you want some?). Kurungnya dibuang karena lawan sudah tahu. Hemat + natural.

Aturannya: boleh buang kalau paralel dan jelas. She can swim and I can't (swim). Can't + (swim) dibuang oke karena paralel swim. Kalau tidak paralel, jangan buang — ambigu.

Di jawaban pendek: Have you eaten? I have (eaten). Will you come? I will (come). Aux sendirian = sudah cukup. Jangan I have eaten full kalau ditanya cepat — kelamaan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I like tea, she likes coffee.` — (tanpa pengulangan kata like yang boros, tapi masih jelas; versi elips: I like tea and she coffee — formal). | `Are you coming? I am.` — Apakah datang? Ya. | `Who will pay? You will.` — Siapa bayar? Kamu.
Kosakata baru (4): `ellipsis` = pelesapan | `parallel` = sejajar | `short answer` = jawaban pendek | `clear` = jelas
Tips praktis: Jawab 5 tanya Yes/No cukup aux saja hari ini.

#### Bagian 3: One/Ones & So/Not Ganti Klausa
Penjelasan: One/ones ganti benda: red shirt or blue one? I want the small ones. Biar nggak ulang shirt-shirt. So/not ganti klausa: Is it raining? I think so (= it is raining). Is he coming? I hope not (= he isn't). So/not hemat satu klausa!

Bedakan I think so (yakin ya) vs I don't think so (ragu/tidak). I hope so vs I hope not. So/not di belakang think/hope/believe. Kecil tapi native banget.

Hindari pengulangan nama: Rina said Rina will... → She... Pronoun + ellipsis + substitution = tulisanmu 30% lebih ramping. Ramping = C1.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `I need a new charger, the old one is broken.` — Butuh charger baru, yang lama rusak. | `Will it flood? I hope not!` — Apakah banjir? Semoga tidak! | `Is the bus late? I think so.` — Apakah bus telat? Kurasa ya.
Kosakata baru (4): `one` = yang (pengganti) | `so` = demikian (pengganti klausa) | `hope` = harap | `believe` = percaya
Tips praktis: Ganti 3 pengulangan di esaimu pakai one/so/not.
Kuis:
1. I love soto. ___ | A) So I do B) So do I C) I do so D) So do me | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Setuju positif memakai So + aux + subjek.
2. Have you eaten? ___ | A) I have eaten fully yes B) I have C) I has D) Me have | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Jawaban pendek cukup aux.
3. Is it broken? I think ___. | A) so B) yes that C) it D) that so | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Pengganti klausa memakai so.

### en68 · C1 8/8 · Ngalah Elegan: Concession (Although/Despite/Whereas)
- Deskripsi: Akui lawan lalu tikung dengan although, despite, whereas biar argumenmu dewasa.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: C1

#### Bagian 1: Although + Klausa vs Despite + Benda
Penjelasan: Although/though/even though + subjek + verb (Although it rained, we came). Despite/in spite of + benda/-ing (Despite the rain / Despite raining, we came). Klausa vs benda, jangan tukar. Despite it rained itu salah, despite the rain yang benar.

Though di belakang juga bisa: It was far, though we enjoyed it. Though belakang = tapi (informal). Although depan = walaupun (formal). Posisi menentukan rasa.

Even though lebih kuat dari although: Even though he is rich, he is stingy (padahal kaya, pelit!). Even = penegas. Pakai saat kontrasnya keterlaluan.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Although buses are slow, they are cheap.` — Walau bus lambat, mereka murah. | `Despite being tired, she finished the report.` — Meski lelah, dia selesaikan laporan. | `Even though prices rose, sales grew.` — Walau harga naik, penjualan tumbuh.
Kosakata baru (4): `although` = walaupun (+ klausa) | `despite` = meski (+ benda) | `stingy` = pelit | `report` = laporan
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 although-mu jadi despite + benda/-ing.

#### Bagian 2: Whereas/While Buat Kontras Dua Fakta
Penjelasan: Whereas/while + kontras seimbang: Java is crowded, whereas Papua is vast. Motorbikes are fast, while buses are safe. Dua fakta, dua sisi, satu kalimat. Banding elegan.

Bedakan while waktu (While I ate, he came) vs while kontras (While I love tea, she loves coffee). Konteks yang menentukan. Kalau dua fakta sejajar, itu kontras. Kalau satu latar satu potong, itu waktu.

However (awal kalimat baru + koma) vs although (tengah satu kalimat). However, buses are late. Although buses are late, ... However butuh titik/koma sebelumnya, although tidak. Jangan However + klausa setengah.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Online shops are cheap, whereas malls are comfy.` — Olshop murah, sedangkan mal nyaman. | `While ojek is fast, it is risky in rain.` — Sementara ojek cepat, berisiko saat hujan. | `Bali is famous, whereas Lombok is quieter.` — Bali terkenal, sedangkan Lombok lebih sepi.
Kosakata baru (4): `whereas` = sedangkan | `vast` = luas | `risky` = berisiko | `comfy` = nyaman
Tips praktis: Bandingkan 2 kota versimu pakai whereas.

#### Bagian 3: Paragraf Concession-Refutation
Penjelasan: Struktur band 7+: concession (While critics say..., Admittedly...), refutation (nevertheless/however... + bukti + contoh). While buses pollute, they cut cars overall. Akui, tikung, bukti. Dewasa!

Contoh: Admittedly, free buses cost billions. Nevertheless, Tallinn shows ridership pays back via taxes. Akui biaya, tikung data Tallinn. Satu data konkret kalahkan tiga opini.

Hindari although + but double: Although rain, but we came — although sudah but, jangan tambah but. Satu kontras cukup. Although..., ... (tanpa but) ATAU ..., but ... (tanpa although).

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Admittedly, cars are comfy. Nevertheless, buses serve more people.` — Diakui, mobil nyaman. Namun, bus layani lebih banyak. | `Despite the cost, pilots are worth trying.` — Meski biaya, uji coba layak. | `While villas profit, locals lose water.` — Sementara vila untung, warga kehilangan air.
Kosakata baru (4): `admittedly` = diakui | `nevertheless` = namun | `pilot` = uji coba | `profit` = untung
Tips praktis: Tulis 1 paragraf akui-tikung soal HP di sekolah.
Kuis:
1. ___ the rain, we left. | A) Although B) Despite C) Because D) So | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Despite + kata benda (rain).
2. ___ it rained, we left. | A) Despite B) In spite C) Although D) Despite of | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Klausa memakai although.
3. Java is dense, ___ Papua is vast. | A) whereas B) although too C) despite D) because | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Kontras seimbang memakai whereas.

### Level C2

### en33 · C2 1/8 · Campur Tapi Rapi: Mixed Conditional
- Deskripsi: Silangkan syarat lampau dengan hasil kini (dan sebaliknya) buat argumen kelas debat.
- Durasi: 55 menit | Tag: Grammar | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Past Cause, Present Result
Penjelasan: Mixed paling sering: If + past perfect (lampau), would + kata kerja (sekarang). "If I had slept early, I wouldn't be sleepy now". Sebabnya kemarin, deritanya sekarang. Relate banget kan?

Orang Indonesia sering samakan dengan conditional 3 semua would have — padahal hasilnya kini, bukan lampau. Cek kata now/today/at the moment: kalau ada, hasilnya would tanpa have.

Rumus cepat: Had done → would do now. Tempel di otak kayak rumus fisika. Sekali hafal, nggak akan ketukar lagi.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If I had saved money, I would own a camera now.` — Jika dulu menabung, kini aku punya kamera. | `She wouldn't be lost if she had brought a map.` — Dia tidak akan tersesat jika tadi bawa peta. | `We would be rich now if we had bought that land.` — Kita akan kaya kini jika dulu membeli tanah itu.
Kosakata baru (4): `save` = menabung | `own` = memiliki | `lost` = tersesat | `land` = tanah
Tips praktis: Tulis satu sesal lampau yang efeknya masih kerasa kini.

#### Bagian 2: Present Cause, Past Result (Kebalikannya)
Penjelasan: Kebalikannya jarang tapi muncul di C2: If + past simple (fakta umum kini), would have + V3 (lampau). "If I were diligent, I would have finished yesterday". Sifatku (malas) menjelaskan kegagalan kemarin.

Pola ini favorit esai kritik kebijakan: "If the city were serious, the flood wouldn't have happened". Kritik kini, sesali lampau. Tajam dan elegan.

Jangan overuse — satu per esai cukup. Kebanyakan mixed bikin pembaca pusing kayak nonton Interstellar tanpa subtitle.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If he were smarter, he wouldn't have signed it.` — Jika dia cerdas, dia tidak akan menandatanganinya. | `If I trusted you, I would have lent the money.` — Jika aku percaya padamu, aku akan meminjamkan uang itu. | `Life would have been easier if she were organized.` — Hidup akan lebih mudah jika dia teratur.
Kosakata baru (4): `diligent` = rajin | `sign` = menandatangani | `trust` = percaya | `organized` = teratur
Tips praktis: Kritik satu kebijakan kotamu pakai pola ini.

#### Bagian 3: Debat: Seandainya Dulu Beda
Penjelasan: Mixed itu bahan debat premium: "If colonial schools had taught local wisdom, we would value jamu now". Sejarah + masa kini + pendirian. Penguji C2 suka yang berlapis gini.

Struktur debat 4 kalimat: klaim mixed, bukti, contoh lokal, solusi kini. "If we had invested in trains, Jakarta wouldn't choke now. Data shows... For example... So we must...". Komplet.

Waspada would di dua sisi: "If I would have..." tetap salah walau C2. If-nya had/were, would-nya di hasil. Aturan abadi semua conditional.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If we had protected forests, floods wouldn't threaten us now.` — Jika dulu melindungi hutan, banjir tak mengancam kini. | `Had we acted sooner, we would be safer today.` — Seandainya bertindak lebih cepat, kini kita lebih aman. | `If I hadn't skipped English, I would be working abroad now.` — Jika dulu tidak bolos Inggris, kini aku kerja di luar negeri.
Kosakata baru (4): `protect` = melindungi | `threaten` = mengancam | `skip` = membolos | `abroad` = luar negeri
Tips praktis: Tulis argumen seandainya tentang satu isu nasional.
Kuis:
1. If I had studied medicine, I ___ a doctor now. | A) would be B) would have been C) will be D) am | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Hasil kini memakai would tanpa have.
2. If she ___ careful, she wouldn't have crashed. | A) had been B) were C) is D) would be | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Sebab umum kini memakai past simple were.
3. Had we left sooner, we ___ home now. | A) will be B) would be C) would have been D) are | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Inversi had lampau dengan hasil kini memakai would be.

### en34 · C2 2/8 · Bahasa Bos: Idiom Bisnis dan Kolokasi Lanjutan
- Deskripsi: Kuasai take over, cut back, bottom line biar rapat dan email-mu kedengeran profesional.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Vocab | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Phrasal Verb Kantor yang Sering Nongol
Penjelasan: Kantor itu sarang phrasal verb: take over (ambil alih), lay off (PHK), cut back (pangkas), roll out (luncurkan), catch up (kejar). Nggak hafal ini, meeting-mu kayak nonton tanpa subtitle.

Orang Indonesia suka "PHK karyawan" diterjemahkan fire employees saja — lay off lebih halus dan formal. Pilih kata yang sesuai etika korporat, bukan sekadar benar.

Bedakan bring forward (majukan jadwal) vs put back (mundurkan). Ketukar ini bisa bikin proyek maju-mundur cantik. Catat pasangan antonimnya sekalian.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `We need to cut back on travel costs.` — Kita perlu memangkas biaya perjalanan. | `The new app will roll out next month.` — Aplikasi baru akan diluncurkan bulan depan. | `He took over the project in March.` — Dia mengambil alih proyek itu bulan Maret.
Kosakata baru (4): `take over` = mengambil alih | `cut back` = memangkas | `roll out` = meluncurkan | `lay off` = PHK
Tips praktis: Ubah 3 kabar kantormu jadi phrasal verb Inggris.

#### Bagian 2: Idiom Rapat Biar Nggak Kelihatan Baru
Penjelasan: Rapat punya bahasanya sendiri: bottom line (intinya), ballpark figure (angka kasar), on the same page (sepaham), think outside the box (kreatif), touch base (kontak singkat). Pakai ini, kamu langsung dianggap insider.

Tapi ingat arena: idiom bisnis oke buat email internal dan speaking, jangan taruh di esai akademik IELTS yang butuh formal netral. Beda panggung beda kostum.

Orang Indonesia suka terjemahkan "garis bawah" jadi underline untuk bottom line — salah konteks. Bottom line itu kesimpulan finansial/inti, bukan garis fisik.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The bottom line is we need more sales.` — Intinya kita butuh lebih banyak penjualan. | `Are we on the same page about the deadline?` — Apakah kita sepaham soal tenggat? | `Let's touch base next Monday.` — Mari kontak singkat Senin depan.
Kosakata baru (4): `bottom line` = intinya | `deadline` = tenggat | `sale` = penjualan | `figure` = angka
Tips praktis: Sisipkan satu idiom bisnis di email Bahasa Inggrismu minggu ini.

#### Bagian 3: Email Formal 3 Paragraf
Penjelasan: Email C2 itu: pembuka sopan ("I hope you are well"), isi padat dengan nominalisasi ("Due to a delay in shipment..."), penutup aksi ("Could you please confirm by Friday?"). Tiga paragraf, kelar. Nggak perlu curhat.

Hindari gonna, wanna, ASAP yang kasar. Ganti: going to, want to, as soon as possible / at your earliest convenience. Formal itu soal pilihan kata receh yang konsisten.

Tutup dengan sign-off sesuai hubungan: Best regards (aman umum), Kind regards (lebih hangat), Yours sincerely (sangat formal). Jangan pakai Cheers ke klien Jepang.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Please find attached the revised proposal.` — Terlampir proposal revisi. | `We would appreciate confirmation by Thursday.` — Kami menghargai konfirmasi sebelum Kamis. | `Apologies for the inconvenience caused.` — Mohon maaf atas ketidaknyamanan yang terjadi.
Kosakata baru (4): `attached` = terlampir | `revised` = revisi | `appreciate` = menghargai | `inconvenience` = ketidaknyamanan
Tips praktis: Tulis ulang satu WA kerja-mu jadi email formal 3 baris.
Kuis:
1. Roll out artinya ___. | A) menggelinding keluar B) meluncurkan C) memecat D) menunda | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Roll out berarti meluncurkan produk/layanan.
2. Bottom line dalam rapat berarti ___. | A) garis bawah halaman B) inti/poin utama C) kerugian total D) tanda tangan | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Bottom line berarti kesimpulan inti.
3. Email formal pengganti ASAP: ___. | A) ASAP!!! B) at your earliest convenience C) gonna soon D) cepetan | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Frasa sopan untuk segera adalah at your earliest convenience.

### en35 · C2 3/8 · Esai Band 8: Argumentasi Super Rapat
- Deskripsi: Rancang esai IELTS Writing Task 2 band 7-9 dengan koherensi dan lexical resource jumbo.
- Durasi: 60 menit | Tag: Exam Prep | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Anatomi Band 8: Apa yang Dinilai?
Penjelasan: IELTS menilai 4: Task Response (jawab tuntas), Coherence (mengalir), Lexical (kosakata), Grammar (struktur). Band 8 butuh keempatnya tinggi, nggak bisa jago satu doang. Banyak yang grammar jago tapi off-topic, ya tetap 6.

Orang Indonesia sering curhat kepanjangan di intro dan kehabisan waktu body. Aturan 40 menit: 5 menit rencana, 30 menit tulis, 5 menit cek. Disiplin waktu = setengah kemenangan.

Hindari menghafal template mentah ("This essay will discuss..."). Penguji hafal semua template itu. Parafrase prompt dengan katamu sendiri, itu yang dinilai.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Some argue cities should ban cars; others defend freedom.` — Sebagian bilang kota harus larang mobil; lainnya bela kebebasan. | `This essay supports limited bans with better transit.` — Esai ini dukung pelarangan terbatas dengan transit lebih baik. | `It will first outline benefits, then address objections.` — Esai akan uraikan manfaat, lalu jawab keberatan.
Kosakata baru (4): `ban` = melarang | `defend` = membela | `outline` = menguraikan | `objection` = keberatan
Tips praktis: Parafrase satu soal IELTS tanpa lihat contoh jawaban.

#### Bagian 2: Counter-argument yang Bikin Kamu Dewasa
Penjelasan: Bedakan band 6 dan 8: band 6 cuma dukung satu sisi, band 8 jawab keberatan lawan. "Critics argue bans hurt workers. Admittedly, delivery drivers suffer. However, exemptions and subsidies can offset this." Ada akui, ada jawab. Dewasa.

Frasa penakluk: Admittedly, Granted, While it is true that..., Nevertheless, Nonetheless. Lima ini bikin kamu bisa nikung argumen lawan dengan elegan.

Contoh lokal itu senjata rahasia: "...as seen in Bogor's car-free Sundays". Penguji baca ratusan esai London-New York, satu Bogor bikin esaimu diingat.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Admittedly, bans burden small traders.` — Diakui, pelarangan membebani pedagang kecil. | `Nevertheless, phased rules plus subsidies ease the shift.` — Namun, aturan bertahap plus subsidi permudah transisi. | `For example, Yogyakarta piloted night deliveries successfully.` — Contohnya, Jogja uji coba antar malam dengan sukses.
Kosakata baru (4): `burden` = membebani | `nevertheless` = namun | `subsidy` = subsidi | `shift` = peralihan
Tips praktis: Tulis satu paragraf counter-argument untuk topik favoritmu.

#### Bagian 3: Parafrase dan Kalimat Kompleks Variatif
Penjelasan: Parafrase itu ganti kata + ganti struktur: "Children spend too much time on phones" → "Adolescents devote excessive hours to smartphones". Ganti children→adolescents, spend→devote, too much→excessive, phones→smartphones. Plus ubah aktif→pasif sesekali.

Variasi kalimat wajib: simple, compound (but/so), complex (although/because), compound-complex, inversion, cleft, passive. Kalau 10 kalimatmu semua S-V-O, lexical dan grammar-mu mentok 6.

Terakhir, cek 5 menit: artikel, -s, tense, koma, ejaan. Kesalahan receh di band 8 itu fatal karena ekspektasinya near-native. Proofread bukan opsional.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Not only do gadgets distract, but they also isolate.` — Gawai tidak hanya mengganggu, tapi juga mengasingkan. | `It is regulation that must balance freedom and safety.` — Regulasilah yang harus imbangi kebebasan dan keamanan. | `Excessive use, often ignored, harms sleep.` — Pemakaian berlebih, yang sering diabaikan, merusak tidur.
Kosakata baru (4): `distract` = mengganggu | `isolate` = mengasingkan | `excessive` = berlebih | `balance` = menyeimbangkan
Tips praktis: Parafrase 3 kalimat prompt tanpa ubah makna.
Kuis:
1. Kriteria IELTS Writing TIDAK termasuk ___. | A) Task Response B) Coherence C) Handwriting art D) Lexical Resource | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Empat kriteria resmi adalah TR, CC, LR, GRA.
2. Frasa terbaik untuk mengakui lawan: ___. | A) You are stupid B) Admittedly C) Whatever D) No way | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Admittedly mengakui keberatan secara akademis.
3. Parafrase terbaik untuk many people think: ___. | A) many people think (copas) B) it is widely believed C) I copy paste D) people people think | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Parafrase mengubah kata dan struktur.

### en36 · C2 4/8 · Simulasi Penuh: Reading N1-nya Inggris + Opini Tajam
- Deskripsi: Bedah teks editorial 12-15 kalimat dan tulis respons opini 200 kata gaya ujian asli.
- Durasi: 60 menit | Tag: Exam Prep | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Editorial: Siapa Ngomong, Apa Maunya?
Penjelasan: Editorial itu opini berbaju fakta. Tugasmu: temukan thesis penulis di 2 paragraf pertama, lalu petakan argumen vs bukti. "Penulis dukung pajak gula karena obesitas, buktinya data Riset Kesehatan." Kalau thesis-nya ketemu, soalnya gampang.

Orang Indonesia sering baca editorial kayak baca WA — linear, tenggelam di detail. Baca vertikal: judul, subjudul, kalimat pertama/terakhir tiap paragraf. Pola argumen langsung kelihatan.

Waspadai nada: cautiously optimistic, highly critical, neutral. Kata sifat nada (admittedly, surprisingly, regrettably) adalah bocoran sikap penulis. Garisbawahi semua adverb sikap itu.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The author contends sugar tax works.` — Penulis berpendapat pajak gula berhasil. | `Regrettably, enforcement remains weak.` — Sayangnya, penegakan masih lemah. | `Surprisingly, youth support the policy.` — Mengejutkannya, anak muda dukung kebijakan itu.
Kosakata baru (4): `contend` = berpendapat | `enforcement` = penegakan | `remain` = tetap | `policy` = kebijakan
Tips praktis: Baca satu tajuk Kompas English dan tulis thesis-nya 1 kalimat.

#### Bagian 2: Kosakata Konteks Tanpa Kamus
Penjelasan: Di ujian nggak boleh buka kamus, jadi tebak dari konteks: definisi ("...refers to..."), contoh ("...such as..."), kontras ("...however..."), sinonim di kalimat sebelah. Empat sinyal ini cukup buat 80% kata sulit.

Jangan terpaku satu kata asing lalu panik. Lingkari, terus jalan. Sering artinya kebuka dua paragraf kemudian. Pembaca C2 itu tahan nggak tahu sementara.

Latihan: ambil editorial, tutup 5 kata sulit, tebak dari tetangganya, baru cek kamus. Skor tebakanmu = skor kesiapan ujianmu.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Draconian, or excessively harsh, rules backfire.` — Aturan drakonian, atau terlalu keras, jadi bumerang. | `Subsidies, such as cash for farmers, help.` — Subsidi, seperti tunai untuk petani, membantu. | `The plan is viable; however, funding is short.` — Rencana layak; namun, dana kurang.
Kosakata baru (4): `harsh` = keras | `backfire` = bumerang | `viable` = layak | `funding` = pendanaan
Tips praktis: Tebak 5 kata sulit hari ini tanpa kamus dulu.

#### Bagian 3: Respons 200 Kata: Setuju Tapi Kritis
Penjelasan: Puncak C2: tulis respons 200 kata: ringkas argumen penulis (50 kata, reported + nominalisasi), beri pendirianmu + 2 alasan + contoh Indonesia (120 kata), tutup rekomendasi hedged (30 kata). Ini format HSK 6写作 dan IELTS Task 2 digabung.

Pakai semua senjata: inversion sekali, cleft sekali, hedging tiga kali, kolokasi bisnis/akademik lima. Bukan pamer, tapi nunjukin range. Penguji C2 memang cari itu.

Proofread 5 menit: cek that/which, artikel, paralelisme, ejaan -tion/-ment. Satu paragraf rapi kalahkan tiga paragraf ngalor-ngidul. Kualitas di atas kuantitas di level ini.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The editorial argues that remote work boosts output.` — Tajuk berpendapat kerja remote dongkrak output. | `While productivity may rise, isolation tends to grow.` — Meski produktivitas mungkin naik, isolasi cenderung tumbuh. | `It is hybrid schemes that seem most viable.` — Skema hibridalah yang tampaknya paling layak.
Kosakata baru (4): `boost` = mendongkrak | `output` = keluaran | `isolation` = isolasi | `hybrid` = hibrid
Tips praktis: Tulis respons 200 kata untuk berita hari ini timer 40 menit.
Kuis:
1. Thesis editorial biasanya ada di ___. | A) tengah lampiran iklan B) awal teks C) daftar pustaka D) kolom komentar | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Thesis umumnya di awal untuk memandu argumen.
2. Kata sinyal definisi konteks: ___. | A) refers to B) however C) yesterday D) loudly | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Refers to menandakan definisi kata sulit.
