3. Respons C2 ideal berisi ___. | A) curhat tanpa struktur B) ringkasan + pendirian + rekomendasi C) copas teks D) emoji banyak | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Struktur ringkas-argumen-rekomendasi menunjukkan kematangan.

### en69 · C2 5/8 · Formal Abis: Subjunctive & It Is Essential That
- Deskripsi: Tulis rekomendasi formal dengan subjunctive (be/have) yang bikin email dan esaimu berwibawa.
- Durasi: 55 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: It Is Essential That He Be... (Tanpa -s!)
Penjelasan: Setelah essential/important/vital/necessary + that, kata kerja tanpa -s/to: It is essential that he be on time (bukan is!), It is vital that she submit now (bukan submits!). Be/submit polos walau subjek he/she. Inilah subjunctive present.

Orang Indonesia sering It is essential that he is... — is-nya harus be. Harus buang -s/to/be modifikasi. Anggap that menghapus semua baju kata kerja, sisakan polosnya.

Daftar pemicu: demand, request, suggest, insist, recommend + that + polos. I suggest he go (bukan goes). Suggest + that polos, tapi suggest + -ing (suggest going) tanpa that. Dua pola suggest jangan ketukar.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `It is crucial that every student bring ID.` — Penting setiap murid bawa KTP. | `We insist that the report be sent today.` — Kami mendesak laporan dikirim hari ini. | `I recommend that she rest more.` — Kusarankan dia istirahat lebih.
Kosakata baru (4): `essential` = penting/mendasar | `crucial` = krusial | `insist` = mendesak | `submit` = mengumpulkan
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 rekomendasi formal pakai It is vital that....

#### Bagian 2: Should Tersembunyi & Were untuk Semua
Penjelasan: British sering selipkan should: It is essential that he should be... Artinya sama dengan versi polos, cuma lebih sopan. American langsung polos. Kenali dua-duanya biar reading-mu aman.

Were untuk semua di khayal formal: If he were here, I were you (bukan was!). Di C2 tulis were walau subjek I/he. Was hanya lisan santai. Ujian tulis = were.

Long live! God save! Heaven forbid! Itu subjunctive fosil tanpa s: Long live the king! (bukan lives!). Hafalkan tiga fosil ini buat variasi.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `If I were the mayor, I would ban plastic.` — Jika aku wali kota, akan larang plastik. | `God save our village from floods!` — Semoga Tuhan selamatkan desa dari banjir! | `It is best that they should wait outside.` — Sebaiknya mereka menunggu di luar.
Kosakata baru (4): `mayor` = wali kota | `ban` = melarang | `forbid` = melarang (formal) | `should` = sebaiknya (tersembunyi)
Tips praktis: Ubah 2 was khayalmu jadi were formal.

#### Bagian 3: Email Rekomendasi 3 Baris
Penjelasan: Template: I suggest that the meeting be moved (pasif polos: be moved!), It is important that all confirm (tanpa -s), Please ensure all be on time? Ensure + polos juga. Tiga subjunctive, email-mu langsung partner-level.

Hindari mandarin-style "I suggest you to go" — suggest tidak pakai to + orang. I suggest (that) you go ATAU I suggest going. To-nya buang. Suggest benci to.

Tutup: Kindly ensure that payment be made by Friday. Be made polos pasif. Formal maksimal, sopan maksimal.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `We request that payment be made in full.` — Kami minta pembayaran dilunasi penuh. | `It is necessary that he attend the briefing.` — Perlu dia hadir briefing. | `I propose that the fee be waived.` — Kuusulkan biaya dibebaskan.
Kosakata baru (4): `request` = meminta | `propose` = mengusulkan | `waive` = membebaskan | `briefing` = pengarahan
Tips praktis: Tulis ulang 1 WA tagih utang jadi subjunctive formal.
Kuis:
1. It is essential that he ___ on time. | A) is B) be C) was D) been | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Subjunctive memakai kata kerja polos be.
2. I suggest that she ___ early. | A) goes B) go C) to go D) going that | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Suggest + that memakai polos tanpa -s.
3. If I ___ you, I'd accept. (formal tulis) | A) am B) was C) were D) be | Jawaban: C | Alasan: Khayal formal memakai were semua subjek.

### en70 · C2 6/8 · Sorot Depan-Belakang: Fronting & Extraposition
- Deskripsi: Atur fokus dengan mengedepankan objek dan memecah subjek panjang biar tulisanmu variatif.
- Durasi: 55 menit | Tag: Writing | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Fronting: Objek/Negatif di Depan
Penjelasan: Fronting itu taruh yang penting di depan: This offer I cannot refuse (normal: I cannot refuse this offer). Such was his anger (amarahnya sebesar itu). Depan = sorotan + variasi. Sekali per paragraf cukup, kebanyakan puitis norak.

Pola: objek/adjunct + S + V (tanpa aux tambahan, kecuali negatif yang butuh inversion). Never/Rarely depan + aux (sudah dibahas inversion) itu fronting + inversion. Kombo panggung.

Orang Indonesia sering depan-belakang dobel subjek: That book, I I like it — I dobel + it dobel. Yang benar: That book, I like. Satu subjek, tanpa it karena that book sudah objeknya.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `This rule we must obey strictly.` — Aturan ini wajib kita patuhi ketat. | `Down the road came the carnival parade.` — Dari ujung jalan datang pawai. | `Such a mess they made after the party!` — Berantakan sekali yang mereka buat usai pesta!
Kosakata baru (4): `fronting` = pengedepanan | `strictly` = ketat | `carnival` = karnaval | `mess` = berantakan
Tips praktis: Depankan 1 objek di esaimu minggu ini.

#### Bagian 2: It Extraposition: Pecah Subjek Panjang
Penjelasan: Subjek panjang (To drive here daily is tiring) pecah jadi It is tiring to drive here daily. It dummy di depan, to-V di belakang. Ringan dibaca, formal didengar. Akademik wajib ini.

Pola: It is + sifat + to-V / that-clause. It is unclear whether buses help. It is likely that prices rise. Whether/that di belakang, it di depan. Seimbang.

Tanpa extraposition, tulisanmu berat kepala: That the policy failed without pilots surprises no one. Pecah: It surprises no one that the policy failed... Langsung enteng.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `It is difficult to find parking here.` — Sulit cari parkir di sini. | `It is doubtful whether the subsidy works.` — Diragukan apakah subsidi berhasil. | `It annoys me that riders ignore helmets.` — Menggangguku bahwa pemotor abaikan helm.
Kosakata baru (4): `extraposition` = pemecahan subjek | `doubtful` = meragukan | `annoy` = mengganggu | `ignore` = mengabaikan
Tips praktis: Pecah 2 subjek panjangmu pakai It is ....

#### Bagian 3: There Advanced & Existential Elegan
Penjelasan: There bukan cuma ada: There seems to be a mistake (tampaknya ada salah), There tends to be traffic (cenderung ada macet). There + seem/tend + to be = hedging eksistensial. C1-C2 banget.

Bedakan There is being... (salah, is + being double be) — pakai There is + being repaired? Being hanya passive continuous: There is being built? Hindari, pakai There is construction. Nominalisasi lebih aman.

Tutup variasi: Here comes the bus! There goes my diet! Here/there + verb + subjek (tanpa aux) buat drama lisan. Here comes trouble! Sastra dikit boleh.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `There appears to be a delay in shipment.` — Tampaknya ada keterlambatan kiriman. | `Here comes the rain again!` — Itu hujan datang lagi! | `It was money, not praise, that motivated him.` — Uang, bukan pujian, yang memotivasinya (cleft + fronting rasa).
Kosakata baru (4): `appear` = tampak | `shipment` = kiriman | `praise` = pujian | `motivate` = memotivasi
Tips praktis: Tulis 2 It is ... that/to versimu.
Kuis:
1. ___ is tiring to commute daily. | A) That B) It C) This D) There being | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Extraposition memakai dummy It.
2. There ___ to be an error here. | A) seems B) is seems C) seeming D) seem to is | Jawaban: A | Alasan: There + seem + to be untuk hedging eksistensial.
3. This offer I cannot ___. (fronting) | A) refuses B) refuse C) refusing D) to refuse | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Fronting tidak ubah kata kerja (refuse polos).

### en71 · C2 7/8 · Metafora Cerdas: Idiom Akademik & Bisnis Lanjutan
- Deskripsi: Bedakan break new ground vs cut corners biar metaforamu tajam bukan klise.
- Durasi: 50 menit | Tag: Vocab | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Idiom Inovasi vs Jalan Pintas
Penjelasan: Inovasi positif: break new ground (rintis baru), cutting-edge (terdepan), game-changer (pengubah permainan), think outside the box. Jalan pintas negatif: cut corners (curang hemat), back to square one (balik nol), uphill battle (perjuangan berat). Pilih sesuai moral ceritamu.

Orang Indonesia suka groundbreaking semua — groundbreaking = memecah tanah (inovatif). Ground + breaking. Jangan groundbraking typo. Satu u beda nasib.

Di esai, satu idiom per 150 kata cukup. Dua lebih sudah klise. Idiom itu sambal, bukan nasi. Taruh di topic/conclusion biar nendang.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The QRIS system broke new ground for street vendors.` — QRIS merintis baru buat kaki lima. | `Cutting corners on safety backfires badly.` — Sunat keamanan jadi bumerang parah. | `We are back to square one after the leak.` — Kami balik ke nol usai bocor.
Kosakata baru (4): `break new ground` = merintis baru | `cut corners` = memangkas curang | `backfire` = bumerang | `vendor` = pedagang
Tips praktis: Ganti 1 kata biasa di esaimu jadi idiom inovasi.

#### Bagian 2: Metafora Air & Api Buat Ekonomi
Penjelasan: Ekonomi suka air: cash flow (aliran kas), dive/plunge (anjlok), float (mengambang), inject funds (suntik dana). Api: overheat (kepanasan), cool down (mendingin), spark growth (picu tumbuh). Air + api = ekonomi hidup.

Flood the market (banjiri pasar = suplai berlebih), dry up (mengering = habis), bubble burst (gelembung pecah). Tiga ini langganan berita ekonomi C2. Hafalkan paketnya.

Jangan campur metafora: cash flooded and burned? Pilih satu elemen per kalimat. Mixed metaphor bikin pembaca ketawa, bukan kagum.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Cheap imports flooded the market last year.` — Impor murah banjiri pasar tahun lalu. | `Funds dried up after the scandal.` — Dana mengering usai skandal. | `Rate hikes cooled the overheated property.` — Kenaikan suku dinginkan properti memanas.
Kosakata baru (4): `flood` = membanjiri | `dry up` = mengering | `overheat` = terlalu panas (ekonomi) | `scandal` = skandal
Tips praktis: Baca 1 berita ekonomi, lingkari 3 metaforanya.

#### Bagian 3: Hedged Idiom: Seem to, Tend to + Metafora
Penjelasan: Gabungkan hedging + idiom biar C2 penuh: The policy seems to have sparked growth, Funds tend to dry up quickly. Seems/tend melembutkan, idiom menguatkan. Gas-rem seimbang.

Hindari idiom gaul di formal: gonna blow up (gaul) vs surge (formal). Blow up simpan buat tongkrongan, surge buat laporan. Beda panggung beda kostum, selalu.

Tutup: In the long run, cutting-edge pilots pay off, while corner-cutting backfires. Long run + pay off + backfires satu kalimat, tiga idiom terkontrol. Ini rasa C2.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `In the long run, quality pays off.` — Jangka panjang, kualitas membuahkan hasil. | `Subsidies seem to spark local trade.` — Subsidi tampaknya picu dagang lokal. | `Without oversight, projects tend to overheat.` — Tanpa awasi, proyek cenderung memanas.
Kosakata baru (4): `pay off` = membuahkan hasil | `oversight` = pengawasan | `surge` = melonjak | `trade` = perdagangan
Tips praktis: Tulis 1 kalimat hedging + idiom versimu.
Kuis:
1. Break new ground artinya ___. | A) menghancurkan tanah B) merintis baru C) menggali kubur D) merusak jalan | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Idiom inovasi berarti merintis baru.
2. Cut corners artinya ___. | A) memotong sudut kertas B) memangkas curang C) belok tajam D) potong rambut | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Cut corners berarti hemat curang berisiko.
3. Funds ___ up (habis). | A) dried B) dry C) drying to D) dryed | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Frasa habis adalah dried up (past).

### en72 · C2 8/8 · Ringkas Tajam: Summarising & Paraphrase C2
- Deskripsi: Ringkas editorial 500 kata jadi 80 kata tanpa copas dengan reported + nominalisasi penuh.
- Durasi: 60 menit | Tag: Exam Prep | Level: C2

#### Bagian 1: Aturan Main Ringkasan: Tanpa Opini, Tanpa Copas
Penjelasan: Ringkasan itu cermin, bukan cat: laporkan isi (The author argues...), tanpa setuju/tidak (I think... buang!), tanpa contoh baru, tanpa copas 4+ kata beruntun. 500→80 kata, 84% dibuang. Kejam tapi adil.

Orang Indonesia sering ringkasan = pendekan tiap paragraf satu kalimat — salah. Ringkasan = thesis + 2-3 argumen kunci + kesimpulan penulis. Detail, angka kecil, anekdot buang semua. Hanya tulang, bukan daging.

Sinyal tulang: thesis awal, topic sentence tiap paragraf, kata ulang 3+ kali, kesimpulan akhir. Daging (contoh, angka rinci, kutipan) buang. Bedakan tulang vs daging dalam 2 menit.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `The editorial contends free buses cut poverty.` — Tajuk berpendapat bus gratis pangkas kemiskinan. | `It cites Tallinn's ridership surge as evidence.` — Mengutip lonjakan Tallinn sebagai bukti. | `It concludes pilots are worth trying.` — Menyimpulkan uji coba layak dicoba.
Kosakata baru (4): `contend` = berpendapat | `cite` = mengutip | `surge` = lonjakan | `worth` = layak
Tips praktis: Ringkas 1 berita hari ini 3 kalimat tanpa copas.

#### Bagian 2: Parafrase Total: Ganti Kata + Struktur
Penjelasan: Parafrase 3 jurus: sinonim (many→numerous, buy→purchase), ubah jenis (decide→decision, quickly→with speed), ubah struktur (aktif→pasif, although→despite). Satu jurus saja = masih copas. Tiga jurus = aman.

Contoh: Many students buy phones → Numerous learners purchase devices (sinonim) → Purchase of devices by numerous learners... (nominal + pasif). Dua langkah, tidak dikenali anti-plagiarisme.

Hindari thesaurus buta: cheap→stingy (pelit, bukan murah!). Sinonim harus se-konteks. Cheap price (murah) vs cheap person (pelit). Cek konteks sebelum ganti!

Contoh (3 kalimat): `Numerous commuters prefer rail links.` — Banyak komuter pilih jalur kereta. (many→numerous, train→rail). | `The decision to delay was deemed wise.` — Keputusan menunda dinilai bijak. (decide→decision). | `Despite tight funds, pilots succeeded.` — Meski dana ketat, uji coba berhasil. (although→despite).
Kosakata baru (4): `numerous` = banyak (formal) | `purchase` = membeli (formal) | `deem` = menilai | `device` = perangkat
Tips praktis: Parafrase 3 kalimat prompt tanpa ubah makna.

#### Bagian 3: Respons 80 + 120: Ringkas + Kritis
Penjelasan: Format ujian C2: 80 kata ringkas netral + 120 kata respons kritis (setuju/tidak + 2 alasan + contoh Indonesia + rekomendasi hedged). Total 200 kata, 40 menit. Ringkas cermin, respons cat (warnamu).

Respons kritis: While the author is right about..., ...tends to be overlooked. Puji parsial + tikung halus + data. Admittedly... Nevertheless... + contoh Bogor/Jogja. Template dewasa.

Proofread: cek that/which, artikel, -tion ejaan, 3rd -s. Kesalahan receh di C2 fatal karena ekspektasi near-native. 5 menit cek = 0.5 band.

Contoh (3 kalimat): `While efficiency may rise, equity tends to suffer.` — Meski efisiensi mungkin naik, keadilan cenderung terdampak. | `It is small traders that bear the cost.` — Pedagang kecillah yang tanggung biaya. | `Phased pilots with subsidies seem wisest.` — Uji bertahap dengan subsidi tampaknya paling bijak.
Kosakata baru (4): `equity` = keadilan | `bear` = menanggung | `phased` = bertahap | `wisest` = paling bijak
Tips praktis: Tulis 80+120 untuk tajuk hari ini timer 40 menit.
Kuis:
1. Ringkasan yang benar ___. | A) tanpa opini pribadi B) curhat setuju C) copas 10 kata D) tambah contoh baru | Jawaban: A | Alasan: Ringkasan netral tanpa opini.
2. Parafrase terbaik: many buy → ___. | A) many buy (copas) B) numerous purchase C) many buys D) buy many | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Ganti kata + struktur tanpa ubah makna.
3. Respons kritis berisi ___. | A) ringkasan saja B) pendirian + alasan + contoh C) emoji D) copas teks | Jawaban: B | Alasan: Respons = posisi + bukti + contoh.

## 2. Flashcard
| Kata/Frasa | Romanisasi/Pelafalan | Arti | Contoh kalimat + terjemahan | Tingkat |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| hello | /həˈloʊ/ he-LOU | halo | Hello, I am Sinta. — Halo, aku Sinta. | Pemula |
| friend | /frɛnd/ frend | teman | She is my friend. — Dia temanku. | Pemula |
| tired | /ˈtaɪərd/ TAI-erd | lelah | I am tired today. — Aku lelah hari ini. | Pemula |
| apple | /ˈæpəl/ E-pel | apel | I ate an apple. — Aku makan sebuah apel. | Pemula |
| cat | /kæt/ ket | kucing | The cat is sleeping. — Kucing itu sedang tidur. | Pemula |
| there is/are | /ðɛr ɪz/ðɛr ɑr/ | ada | There is a lamp. — Ada sebuah lampu. | Pemula |
| some / any | /sʌm/ /ˈɛni/ | beberapa / ada (tanya) | Do you have any questions? — Punya pertanyaan? | Pemula |
| on the table | /ɒn ðə ˈteɪbəl/ | di atas meja | Keys are on the table. — Kunci di atas meja. | Pemula |
| agree | /əˈɡriː/ e-GRII | setuju | I agree with you. — Aku setuju denganmu. | Pemula |
| going to | /ˈɡoʊɪŋ tuː/ | akan (rencana) | We are going to move. — Kami akan pindah. | Pemula |
| will | /wɪl/ wil | akan (spontan) | I'll help you. — Aku akan membantumu. | Pemula |
| think | /θɪŋk/ thingk | berpikir | I think this is thin. — Menurutku ini tipis. | Pemula |
| cheap - cheaper | /tʃiːp/ - /ˈtʃiːpər/ | murah - lebih murah | This is cheaper. — Ini lebih murah. | Pemula |
| can | /kæn/ ken | bisa/boleh | Can I borrow this? — Boleh pinjam ini? | Pemula |
| must | /mʌst/ mast | harus (wajib) | You must wear a helmet. — Wajib pakai helm. | Pemula |
| should | /ʃʊd/ syud | sebaiknya | You should rest. — Sebaiknya istirahat. | Pemula |
| visited | /ˈvɪzɪtɪd/ VI-zit-id | mengunjungi (lampau) | We visited Borobudur. — Kami mengunjungi Borobudur. | Pemula |
| while | /waɪl/ wail | selagi/saat | While I was queuing, I met her. — Saat antre, aku bertemu dia. | Pemula |
| delicious | /dɪˈlɪʃəs/ di-LI-syes | enak | The food was delicious. — Makanannya enak. | Pemula |
| straight | /streɪt/ streit | lurus | Go straight. — Jalan lurus. | Pemula |
| present perfect | /ˈprɛzənt ˈpɜːrfɪkt/ | telah (pengalaman) | I have tried seblak. — Aku pernah coba seblak. | Menengah |
| since / for | /sɪns/ /fɔːr/ | sejak / selama | Since 2020 / for 5 years. — Sejak 2020 / selama 5 th. | Menengah |
| unless | /ənˈlɛs/ en-LES | kecuali jika | Unless you hurry, you'll be late. — Kecuali buru-buru, telat. | Menengah |
| passive (was built) | /ˈpæsɪv/ | dibangun (pasif) | Temple was built in 9th century. — Candi dibangun abad 9. | Menengah |
| reported (he said) | /rɪˈpɔːrtɪd/ | berkata (tak langsung) | He said he was busy. — Dia bilang dia sibuk. | Menengah |
| who / which | /huː/ /wɪtʃ/ | yang | Girl who sits here is smart. — Gadis yang duduk sini pintar. | Menengah |
| put off | /pʊt ɒf/ put off | menunda | Don't put off homework. — Jangan tunda PR. | Menengah |
| however | /haʊˈɛvər/ | namun | It is cheap. However, risky. — Murah. Namun, berisiko. | Menengah |
| therefore | /ˈðɛrfɔːr/ | oleh karena itu | Therefore, be careful. — Oleh karena itu, hati-hati. | Menengah |
| would (khayalan) | /wʊd/ wud | akan (khayalan) | If I were rich, I would travel. — Jika kaya, aku akan jalan. | Menengah |
| had studied | /hæd ˈstʌdid/ | telah belajar (sesal) | If I had studied, I'd have passed. — Jika belajar, aku lulus. | Menengah |
| must be | /mʌst biː/ | pasti (dugaan) | She must be home. — Dia pasti di rumah. | Menengah |
| might have | /maɪt hæv/ | mungkin telah | He might have forgotten. — Dia mungkin lupa. | Menengah |
| have cut (causative) | /hæv kʌt/ | minta potongkan | I had my hair cut. — Aku potong rambut (di barber). | Menengah |
| thesis statement | /ˈθiːsɪs/ | pernyataan pendirian | This essay agrees that... — Esai ini setuju bahwa... | Menengah |
| for instance | /fɔːr ˈɪnstəns/ | contohnya | For instance, in Surabaya... — Contohnya, di Surabaya... | Menengah |
| partly agree | /ˈpɑːrtli əˈɡriː/ | sebagian setuju | I partly agree. — Aku sebagian setuju. | Menengah |
| aid | /eɪd/ eid | bantuan | Aid is on the way. — Bantuan dalam perjalanan. | Menengah |
| save money | /seɪv ˈmʌni/ | menabung | Save money to buy it. — Tabung uang untuk membelinya. | Menengah |
| burden | /ˈbɜːrdən/ BER-den | beban | Ease their burden. — Ringankan beban mereka. | Menengah |
| inversion (never have I) | /ɪnˈvɜːrʒən/ | inversi | Never have I seen this. — Belum pernah kulihat ini. | Mahir |
| not only...but also | /nɒt ˈoʊnli/ | tidak hanya...tapi juga | Not only cheap but fast. — Tak hanya murah tapi cepat. | Mahir |
| cleft (it is...that) | /klɛft/ | sorotan | It is discipline that wins. — Disiplinlah yang menang. | Mahir |
| nominalisation | /ˌnɒmɪnəlaɪˈzeɪʃən/ | pembendaan | Decision, development. — Keputusan, pembangunan. | Mahir |
| tend to | /tɛnd tuː/ | cenderung | Teens tend to scroll. — Remaja cenderung scroll. | Mahir |
| collocation | /ˌkɒləˈkeɪʃən/ | pasangan kata | Heavy rain, make mistake. — Hujan deras, buat salah. | Mahir |
| bottom line | /ˈbɒtəm laɪn/ | intinya | Bottom line is sales. — Intinya penjualan. | Mahir |
| take over | /teɪk ˈoʊvər/ | ambil alih | He took over project. — Dia ambil alih proyek. | Mahir |
| mixed conditional | /mɪkst/ | kondisional campur | If I'd saved, I'd own now. — Jika dulu nabung, kini punya. | Mahir |
| counter-argument | /ˈkaʊntər ˈɑːrɡjʊmənt/ | sanggahan | Admittedly..., however... — Diakui..., namun... | Mahir |
| lexical resource | /ˈlɛksɪkəl rɪˈsɔːrs/ | kekayaan kata | Varied vocabulary. — Kosakata variatif. | Mahir |
| coherence | /koʊˈhɪərəns/ | keruntutan | Clear flow. — Alur jelas. | Mahir |
| hedged claim | /hɛdʒd kleɪm/ | klaim hati-hati | May reduce risk. — Mungkin kurangi risiko. | Mahir |
| touch base | /tʌtʃ beɪs/ | kontak singkat | Let's touch base Monday. — Kontak singkat Senin. | Mahir |
| enforcement | /ɛnˈfɔːrsmənt/ | penegakan | Enforcement is weak. — Penegakan lemah. | Mahir |
| viable | /ˈvaɪəbəl/ VAI-e-bel | layak | Plan is viable. — Rencana layak. | Mahir |
| hybrid | /ˈhaɪbrɪd/ HAI-brid | hibrid | Hybrid scheme works. — Skema hibrid berhasil. | Mahir |
| output | /ˈaʊtpʊt/ AUT-put | keluaran | Boost output. — Dongkrak output. | Mahir |
| regret (should have) | /rɪˈɡrɛt/ | sesal (seharusnya) | Should have told me. — Seharusnya beri tahu aku. | Mahir |
| on the same page | /ɒn ðə seɪm peɪdʒ/ | sepaham | Are we same page? — Apakah kita sepaham? | Mahir |

## 4. Dialog Situasional

### DI KAFE · Pesan Kopi Susu Gula Aren · Pemula · A1 · santai
Kamu pesan minum dengan sopan dan tanya harga.
A: `Good morning! Can I have an iced brown sugar latte?` — Selamat pagi! Boleh minta latte brown sugar dingin?
B: `Sure! Small or large?` — Tentu! Kecil atau besar?
A: `Small, please. How much is it?` — Kecil saja. Berapa harganya?
B: `Twenty-five thousand. Would you like some cake too?` — Dua puluh lima ribu. Mau kue juga?
A: `No, thanks. Just the coffee.` — Tidak, makasih. Kopinya saja.
B: `Your coffee will be ready in five minutes.` — Kopimu siap lima menit lagi.
A: `Great! Can I wait over there near the window?` — Bagus! Bolehkah menunggu di sana dekat jendela?
B: `Of course. I will call your name when it is ready.` — Tentu. Akan kupanggil namamu saat siap.
A: `Do you have WiFi here? What is the password?` — Ada WiFi di sini? Apa kata sandinya?
B: `Yes, the password is on your receipt. It is kopi123.` — Ada, kata sandi ada di strukmu. Kopi123.
A: `Thank you so much!` — Makasih banyak!
B: `You're welcome. Enjoy!` — Sama-sama. Selamat menikmati!

### BELANJA · Nawar Batik di Pasar · Pemula · A1 · santai
Kamu nawar batik dengan ramah tanpa menyinggung penjual.
A: `Excuse me, how much is this batik shirt?` — Permisi, berapa harga kemeja batik ini?
B: `One hundred fifty thousand, miss.` — Seratus lima puluh ribu, mbak.
A: `That's a bit expensive. Can you give me a discount?` — Agak mahal. Bisa diskon?
B: `Okay, one hundred twenty for you.` — Oke, seratus dua puluh buat kamu.
A: `Do you have this in blue?` — Ada yang ini warna biru?
B: `Yes, here it is. Do you want to try it on?` — Ada, ini dia. Mau coba pakai?
A: `Yes, where is the fitting room?` — Ya, di mana ruang ganti?
B: `Over there, on your left.` — Di sana, sebelah kirimu.
A: `It fits well! But do you have a smaller size?` — Pas! Tapi ada ukuran lebih kecil?
B: `Let me check. Yes, here is the small one.` — Kuceklah. Ya, ini yang kecil.
A: `How much is the small one? Same price?` — Berapa yang kecil? Sama harga?
B: `Same price, miss. One hundred twenty only.` — Sama, mbak. Seratus dua puluh saja.
A: `Okay, I'll take it. Can I pay by QRIS?` — Oke, kuambil. Bisa bayar QRIS?
B: `Yes, please scan here. Thank you for shopping!` — Bisa, silakan pindai di sini. Makasih belanjanya!

### ARAH JALAN · Nyasar di Malioboro · Pemula · A2 · santai
Kamu tanya arah ke stasiun karena Google Maps mati.
A: `Excuse me, where is Tugu Station?` — Permisi, di mana Stasiun Tugu?
B: `Go straight, then turn right at the traffic light.` — Jalan lurus, lalu belok kanan di lampu merah.
A: `Is it far from here?` — Apakah jauh dari sini?
B: `No, it's a five-minute walk.` — Tidak, lima menit jalan kaki.
A: `Could you speak slowly, please? I don't understand.` — Bisa bicara pelan? Aku tidak paham.
B: `Sure. Walk. Straight. Then. Right.` — Tentu. Jalan. Lurus. Lalu. Kanan.
A: `Thank you! Is there a minimarket near the station?` — Makasih! Ada minimarket dekat stasiun?
B: `Yes, there is one across the street.` — Ya, ada satu di seberang jalan.
A: `Is the road safe to cross at night?` — Apakah aman menyeberang malam hari?
B: `Yes, there is a zebra cross and a traffic light.` — Ya, ada zebra cross dan lampu merah.
A: `How much is a ticket to Solo?` — Berapa tiket ke Solo?
B: `Thirty thousand. You can buy it on the app.` — Tiga puluh ribu. Bisa beli di aplikasi.
A: `Perfect! Thank you for your help!` — Sempurna! Makasih bantuanmu!
B: `You're welcome. Have a safe trip!` — Sama-sama. Semoga selamat sampai tujuan!

### RESTORAN · Alergi Udang Darurat · Pemula · A2 · formal
Kamu pesan makan sambil pastikan aman dari alergi.
A: `Good evening. I'd like a bowl of fried rice, please.` — Selamat malam. Saya mau semangkuk nasi goreng.
B: `Sure. Is it spicy or medium?` — Baik. Pedas atau sedang?
A: `Medium, please. I'm allergic to shrimp. Is there shrimp in it?` — Sedang saja. Aku alergi udang. Ada udang di dalamnya?
B: `No, but we can make it without shrimp paste to be safe.` — Tidak, tapi bisa dibuat tanpa terasi biar aman.
A: `Thank you. Can we have the bill later?` — Makasih. Nanti minta bonnya ya?
B: `Of course. Anything to drink?` — Tentu. Minumnya apa?
A: `Two iced teas, please.` — Dua es teh, tolong.
B: `No problem. Less ice or normal?` — Siap. Es sedikit atau biasa?
A: `Less ice, please. My throat hurts a bit.` — Es sedikit. Tenggorokanku agak sakit.
B: `Okay, noted. Please wait ten minutes.` — Oke, dicatat. Tunggu sepuluh menit ya.
A: `Sure. By the way, is the toilet over there?` — Siap. Ngomong-ngomong, toilet di sana?
B: `Yes, turn left behind the cashier. Enjoy your meal!` — Ya, belok kiri belakang kasir. Selamat makan!

### KANTOR · Izin Sakit ke Atasan · Menengah · B1 · formal
Kamu telepon atasan untuk izin sakit dengan bahasa profesional.
A: `Good morning, Mr. Andi. This is Rina from marketing.` — Selamat pagi, Pak Andi. Ini Rina dari marketing.
B: `Morning, Rina. What's up?` — Pagi, Rina. Ada apa?
A: `I have a fever and I have visited the doctor. I need one day off.` — Aku demam dan sudah ke dokter. Butuh izin sehari.
B: `Have you sent the report that was due today?` — Apakah laporan yang jatuh tempo hari ini sudah dikirim?
A: `Yes, I have sent it by email. I told Dimas to present it if needed.` — Sudah kukirim via email. Aku sudah bilang Dimas untuk presentasi jika perlu.
B: `Okay, get well soon. If you feel better, join online tomorrow.` — Oke, cepat sembuh. Jika membaik, gabung online besok.
A: `I will. Thank you for understanding.` — Pasti. Terima kasih pengertiannya.
B: `No problem. Please send the doctor's note later.` — Santai. Tolong kirim surat dokter nanti.
A: `Sure, I'll send it this afternoon by WA.` — Siap, kukirim sore ini via WA.
B: `Good. And rest well, don't check email today.` — Bagus. Dan istirahatlah, jangan cek email hari ini.
A: `Okay, I will turn off my laptop now.` — Oke, kumatikan laptop kini.
B: `Perfect. See you online tomorrow morning!` — Sempurna. Sampai jumpa online besok pagi!

### TRAVEL · Komplain Hotel AC Mati · Menengah · B1 · formal
Kamu komplain AC mati dengan tegas tapi sopan.
A: `Excuse me, our AC has not worked since last night.` — Permisi, AC kami tidak berfungsi sejak semalam.
B: `I'm sorry to hear that. What is your room number?` — Maaf mendengarnya. Nomor kamar berapa?
A: `Room 305. We have called twice but nobody came.` — Kamar 305. Kami sudah telepon dua kali tapi tak ada yang datang.
B: `I apologize. I will send a technician in ten minutes.` — Saya minta maaf. Saya akan kirim teknisi sepuluh menit lagi.
A: `Could we move rooms if it can't be fixed?` — Bisakah pindah kamar jika tidak bisa diperbaiki?
B: `Of course. Unless it is fixed, you can use room 310.` — Tentu. Kecuali diperbaiki, bisa pakai kamar 310.
A: `Thank you. Please hurry, my baby can't sleep.` — Makasih. Tolong cepat, bayiku tidak bisa tidur.
B: `I understand. I will call you back in five minutes.` — Aku paham. Akan kutelepon balik lima menit lagi.
A: `Good. Should we wait in the room or lobby?` — Bagus. Haruskah menunggu di kamar atau lobi?
B: `Please wait in the room. The technician will knock twice.` — Tunggu di kamar. Teknisi akan ketuk dua kali.
A: `Okay. And is breakfast still included tomorrow?` — Oke. Dan apakah sarapan masih termasuk besok?
B: `Yes, from six to ten near the pool. Sorry again!` — Ya, jam 6 sampai 10 dekat kolam. Maaf lagi ya!

### DISKUSI · Motor vs Bus ke Kampus · Menengah · B2 · santai
Kamu dan teman debat transport dengan argumen rapi.
A: `In my view, buses are better for students.` — Menurutku, bus lebih baik buat mahasiswa.
B: `I see your point, but motorbikes are faster.` — Paham maksudmu, tapi motor lebih cepat.
A: `True, however buses are cheaper and safer. I spend only 100k monthly.` — Betul, namun bus lebih murah dan aman. Aku cuma habis 100rb sebulan.
B: `I partly agree, yet buses are often late. I missed class twice.` — Sebagian setuju, tapi bus sering telat. Aku telat kelas dua kali.
A: `That's true. If the schedule improved, more students would switch.` — Itu benar. Jika jadwal diperbaiki, lebih banyak mahasiswa akan beralih.
B: `Agreed. So we both want better buses, just different reasons?` — Sepakat. Jadi kita sama-sama mau bus lebih baik, cuma beda alasan?
A: `Exactly! Let's write a letter to campus.` — Tepat! Yuk tulis surat ke kampus.
B: `Good idea. Who should we send it to?` — Ide bagus. Ke siapa kita kirim?
A: `To the student affairs office. I have the email.` — Ke kemahasiswaan. Aku punya emailnya.
B: `What should we write first? The price or safety?` — Apa ditulis dulu? Harga atau keamanan?
A: `Both. First safety, then price with our data.` — Keduanya. Pertama keamanan, lalu harga dengan data.
B: `Okay, I'll draft tonight and you check tomorrow?` — Oke, kudraf malam ini dan kamu cek besok?
A: `Deal! Let's meet at the library at three.` — Sepakat! Ketemu di perpus jam 3.
B: `Perfect. Bring your bus receipts as proof!` — Sempurna. Bawa struk bus sebagai bukti!

### WAWANCARA KERJA · Fresh Graduate Grogi · Menengah · B2 · formal
Kamu jawab pertanyaan HR dengan STAR mini dan conditional pas.
A: `Tell me about yourself.` — Ceritakan dirimu.
B: `I graduated in 2024 and I have worked as a barista for a year. I serve 100 cups daily.` — Aku lulus 2024 dan sudah kerja barista setahun. Menyajikan 100 cangkir tiap hari.
A: `Why should we hire you?` — Kenapa harus terima kamu?
B: `Because I learn fast. If I don't know something, I will ask and practice until I can.` — Karena aku cepat belajar. Jika tidak tahu, aku akan tanya dan latihan sampai bisa.
A: `Where do you see yourself in three years?` — Di mana dirimu tiga tahun lagi?
B: `I will have finished my digital marketing course and I hope to lead a small team.` — Aku akan sudah selesaikan kursus digital marketing dan berharap pimpin tim kecil.
A: `What would you do if a customer complained?` — Apa yang kamu lakukan jika pelanggan komplain?
B: `I would listen first, apologize, and offer a solution quickly.` — Aku akan dengarkan dulu, minta maaf, dan tawarkan solusi cepat.
A: `Good. Are you willing to work weekends?` — Bagus. Bersediakah kerja akhir pekan?
B: `Yes, I am used to weekend shifts from my cafe job.` — Ya, terbiasa sif akhir pekan dari kerja kafe.
A: `Nice. When can you start?` — Bagus. Kapan bisa mulai?
B: `I can start next Monday. I have finished my contract.` — Bisa mulai Senin depan. Kontrakku sudah selesai.
A: `Perfect. We will call you Friday for the result.` — Sempurna. Kami telepon Jumat untuk hasilnya.
B: `Thank you so much for this opportunity!` — Makasih banyak untuk kesempatan ini!

### NEGOSIASI · Minta Naik Gaji · Mahir · C1 · formal
Kamu negosiasi gaji dengan data dan hedging elegan.
A: `Thank you for meeting me. I'd like to discuss my compensation.` — Makasih waktunya. Aku ingin diskusikan kompensasiku.
B: `Sure. What have you achieved this year?` — Tentu. Apa pencapaianmu tahun ini?
A: `I led the rollout that increased sales 20%. Not only did we hit target, but we also cut costs.` — Aku pimpin peluncuran yang naikkan penjualan 20%. Tak hanya capai target, juga pangkas biaya.
B: `Impressive. However, budgets are tight.` — Mengesankan. Namun, anggaran ketat.
A: `I understand. Evidence suggests my output exceeds peers. Could we consider 10%?` — Aku paham. Bukti menunjukkan output-ku di atas rata-rata. Bisakah pertimbangkan 10%?
B: `We can offer 7% now and review in six months.` — Kami bisa tawarkan 7% kini dan tinjau enam bulan lagi.
A: `That seems reasonable. Let's touch base in June with clear KPIs.` — Itu masuk akal. Mari kontak lagi Juni dengan KPI jelas.
B: `Agreed. I will send the updated KPIs by Friday.` — Sepakat. Kukirim KPI terbaru Jumat.
A: `Thank you. Should I share them with my team first?` — Makasih. Haruskah kubagi ke tim dulu?
B: `Yes, please. And keep driving those sales numbers.` — Ya, tolong. Dan terus genjot angka penjualan.
A: `I will. I have been tracking them weekly.` — Pasti. Sudah kulacak mingguan.
B: `Excellent. See you in the review meeting!` — Luar biasa. Sampai jumpa di rapat tinjauan!

### RAPAT · Presentasi Data Turun · Mahir · C1 · formal
Kamu jelaskan penurunan dengan passive dan nominalisasi rapi.
A: `Our traffic dropped 15% last quarter.` — Traffic kami turun 15% kuartal lalu.
B: `What caused the reduction?` — Apa sebab penurunannya?
A: `The delay was caused by late shipment. Two campaigns were postponed.` — Keterlambatan disebabkan kiriman telat. Dua kampanye ditunda.
B: `What would you suggest?` — Apa saranmu?
A: `It is investment in local warehouses that we need. Failure to act may worsen churn.` — Investasi gudang lokallah yang kita butuhkan. Gagal bertindak mungkin perparah churn.
B: `Admittedly, logistics is weak. Nevertheless, is budget available?` — Diakui, logistik lemah. Namun, apakah anggaran ada?
A: `If we had acted sooner, we would be safer now. Let's pilot one city first.` — Jika dulu bertindak cepat, kini kita aman. Mari uji satu kota dulu.
B: `Which city do you suggest for the pilot?` — Kota mana yang kamu sarankan?
A: `Surabaya. It has good warehouses and tracking.` — Surabaya. Gudangnya bagus dan tracking oke.
B: `How long will the pilot take?` — Berapa lama uji cobanya?
A: `Two months. We will have data by September.` — Dua bulan. Data ada September.
B: `Okay, please send the budget plan tonight.` — Oke, kirim rencana anggaran malam ini.
A: `I will. Thank you for your support!` — Pasti. Makasih dukunganmu!

### DEBAT PUBLIK · Pajak Gula · Mahir · C2 · formal
Kamu debat kebijakan dengan mixed conditional dan counter-argument.
A: `The government should tax sugary drinks.` — Pemerintah harus pajaki minuman manis.
B: `Admittedly, obesity is high. Nevertheless, taxes burden the poor.` — Diakui, obesitas tinggi. Namun, pajak bebani orang miskin.
A: `While it is true they pay more, health costs drop in the long run. In Mexico, consumption fell 7%.` — Walau benar mereka bayar lebih, biaya kesehatan turun jangka panjang. Di Meksiko, konsumsi turun 7%.
B: `If the city were serious, education would have worked already. Why punish sellers?` — Jika kota serius, edukasi sudah berhasil. Kenapa hukum penjual?
A: `Had we relied only on education, we would still wait now. Taxes plus subsidies work faster.` — Seandainya hanya andalkan edukasi, kini kita masih menunggu. Pajak plus subsidi lebih cepat.
B: `Fair point. So pilot in one province with clear review?` — Poin adil. Jadi uji satu provinsi dengan tinjauan jelas?
A: `Exactly. It is careful piloting that builds trust.` — Tepat. Uji coba hati-hatilah yang bangun kepercayaan.
B: `I agree. Who should monitor the results?` — Setuju. Siapa harus pantau hasilnya?
A: `An independent campus team plus the health office.` — Tim kampus independen plus dinas kesehatan.
B: `Good. And how do we protect small sellers?` — Bagus. Dan bagaimana lindungi penjual kecil?
A: `With subsidies for water stations and clear labels.` — Dengan subsidi depot air dan label jelas.
B: `That sounds fair. Let's draft the proposal together.` — Itu adil. Yuk susun proposal bersama.
A: `Perfect. I'll bring the Mexico data tomorrow.` — Sempurna. Kubawa data Meksiko besok.

### EMAIL KRISIS · Komplain Klien Besar · Mahir · C2 · formal
Kamu tangani klien marah via telepon dengan idiom bisnis halus.
A: `We are upset. Shipment is two weeks late.` — Kami kesal. Kiriman telat dua minggu.
B: `Apologies for the inconvenience caused. I take full responsibility.` — Mohon maaf atas ketidaknyamanan. Aku tanggung jawab penuh.
A: `What's the bottom line? When will it arrive?` — Intinya apa? Kapan tiba?
B: `It was cleared yesterday and will roll out Monday. I have attached tracking.` — Sudah cleared kemarin dan akan dikirim Senin. Sudah kulampirkan tracking.
A: `Are we on the same page that compensation is needed?` — Sepaham ya kompensasi perlu?
B: `Absolutely. We will cut 15% and assign a dedicated PIC. Never have we missed twice.` — Pasti. Kami potong 15% dan tugaskan PIC khusus. Tak pernah kami gagal dua kali.
A: `Okay. Let's touch base Friday. Don't disappoint us again.` — Oke. Kontak lagi Jumat. Jangan kecewakan lagi.
B: `We won't. I will personally check the truck tomorrow.` — Tidak akan. Aku pribadi cek truk besok.
A: `Good. Please send photos of the goods too.` — Bagus. Kirim foto barang juga ya.
B: `Sure. I have already asked the warehouse team.` — Siap. Sudah kuminta ke tim gudang.
A: `Thank you. We hope this is the last delay.` — Makasih. Semoga ini delay terakhir.
B: `It will be. Thank you for your patience!` — Pasti. Makasih kesabaranmu!

### KOS · Lapor Keran Bocor ke Ibu Kos · Pemula · A1 · santai
Kamu lapor kerusakan kos dengan sopan dan jelas.
A: `Good afternoon, Bu. Sorry to bother you.` — Selamat siang, Bu. Maaf mengganggu.
B: `No problem. What's wrong?` — Santai. Ada apa?
A: `The tap in my bathroom is leaking.` — Keran kamar mandiku bocor.
B: `Oh no! Since when?` — Ya ampun! Sejak kapan?
A: `Since yesterday morning. There is water everywhere.` — Sejak kemarin pagi. Air di mana-mana.
B: `Okay, I'll call the repairman today.` — Oke, kutelpon tukang hari ini.
A: `Thank you, Bu. Should I turn off the water first?` — Makasih, Bu. Haruskah kumatikan air dulu?
B: `Yes, please. Turn the blue valve to the right.` — Ya, tolong. Putar keran biru ke kanan.
A: `Okay, I will. How much is the fee?` — Oke. Berapa biayanya?
B: `Free, it's my responsibility. Don't worry!` — Gratis, itu tanggung jawabku. Santai!
A: `Great! Can I wait in my room?` — Bagus! Bolehkah menunggu di kamar?
B: `Yes, he will knock three times at four.` — Ya, dia akan ketuk tiga kali jam 4.

### KELUARGA · Telepon Ibu Kangen Rumah · Pemula · A1 · santai
Kamu telepon ibumu karena homesick dan cerita kabar.
A: `Hello, Mom? This is me, Rina.` — Halo, Ibu? Ini aku, Rina.
B: `Hi, dear! How are you? Are you healthy?` — Hai, sayang! Apa kabar? Sehat?
A: `I'm okay, but I'm a bit homesick.` — Aku baik, tapi agak kangen rumah.
B: `Oh, what do you miss most?` — Oh, apa yang paling dikangenin?
A: `Your fried chicken and our small house.` — Ayam goreng Ibu dan rumah kecil kita.
B: `We miss you too. Is your room comfortable?` — Kami juga kangen. Kamarmu nyaman?
A: `Yes, but it is small and a bit noisy.` — Ya, tapi kecil dan agak berisik.
B: `Be patient. Do you eat well there?` — Sabar ya. Makan baik di sana?
A: `Sometimes I skip breakfast. I wake up late.` — Kadang lewati sarapan. Bangun kesiangan.
B: `Don't skip meals! You should eat vegetables.` — Jangan lewati makan! Harus makan sayur.
A: `Okay, Mom. I will cook tonight.` — Oke, Bu. Akan kumasak malam ini.
B: `Good girl. Call me every Sunday, okay?` — Anak pintar. Telepon tiap Minggu ya?

### KAMPUS · Tanya Tugas ke Teman · Pemula · A2 · santai
Kamu tanya PR yang terlewat karena kemarin izin sakit.
A: `Hi, Dimas! Did I miss anything yesterday?` — Hai, Dimas! Apa aku ketinggalan sesuatu kemarin?
B: `Yes, the lecturer gave new homework.` — Ya, dosen kasih PR baru.
A: `Oh no! What should I do?` — Ya ampun! Harus apa aku?
B: `Don't worry. I have the notes. Do you want photos?` — Santai. Aku punya catatan. Mau fotonya?
A: `Yes, please! Send them by WA.` — Ya, tolong! Kirim via WA.
B: `Okay. The task is on page twelve, numbers one to five.` — Oke. Tugas halaman 12, nomor 1 sampai 5.
A: `When is the deadline?` — Kapan tenggatnya?
B: `Friday morning. We must submit it on paper.` — Jumat pagi. Harus kumpul kertas.
A: `Is it difficult? I don't understand chapter three.` — Sulit? Aku tidak paham bab 3.
B: `A bit. But I can explain after class.` — Agak. Tapi bisa kujelaskan usai kelas.
A: `Really? Are you free at three?` — Serius? Luang jam 3?
B: `Yes! Let's meet at the library.` — Ya! Ketemu di perpus.

### OJEK · Pesan Ojek ke Stasiun · Pemula · A2 · santai
Kamu pesan ojek dan pastikan harga serta rute jelas.
A: `Good morning, Pak! Are you free?` — Pagi, Pak! Luang?
B: `Yes, where are you going?` — Ya, mau ke mana?
A: `To Tugu Station. How much is it?` — Ke Stasiun Tugu. Berapa?
B: `Twenty thousand. With a bag it's twenty-five.` — Dua puluh ribu. Dengan tas 25.
A: `Okay. Can you go fast? My train is at seven.` — Oke. Bisa cepat? Keretaku jam 7.
B: `Sure. Get on, please wear the helmet.` — Siap. Naik, pakai helm ya.
A: `Is the road crowded now?` — Apakah jalan macet kini?
B: `A bit near the market, but I know a shortcut.` — Agak dekat pasar, tapi aku tahu jalan pintas.
A: `Great! How long does it take?` — Bagus! Berapa lama?
B: `About fifteen minutes if no rain.` — Sekitar 15 menit jika tidak hujan.
A: `Perfect. Please drop me at the east gate.` — Sempurna. Turunkan di gerbang timur ya.
B: `Okay. Hold tight, let's go!` — Oke. Pegangan, berangkat!

### KLINIK · Periksa Batuk Pilek · Menengah · B1 · formal
Kamu periksa ke klinik kampus dan jelaskan gejala runtut.
A: `Good morning, Doctor. I have a bad cough.` — Pagi, Dok. Aku batuk parah.
B: `Since when? Do you have a fever?` — Sejak kapan? Apakah demam?
A: `Since three days ago. I have a slight fever at night.` — Sejak 3 hari lalu. Demam ringan malam hari.
B: `Have you taken any medicine yet?` — Sudah minum obat?
A: `I have just drunk herbal tea. It hasn't worked.` — Baru minum teh herbal. Belum mempan.
B: `Do you smoke? Are you allergic to anything?` — Merokok? Alergi sesuatu?
A: `I never smoke. I'm allergic to dust.` — Tidak pernah merokok. Alergi debu.
B: `Okay, open your mouth. Your throat is red.` — Oke, buka mulut. Tenggorokan merah.
A: `Is it serious? Should I rest at home?` — Apakah serius? Haruskah istirahat di rumah?
B: `Not serious, but you should rest two days.` — Tidak serius, tapi harus istirahat 2 hari.
A: `What medicine should I take?` — Obat apa harus kuminum?
B: `Take this twice a day after meals and drink warm water.` — Minum ini 2x sehari usai makan dan air hangat.
A: `Thank you, Doctor. When should I come back?` — Makasih, Dok. Kapan harus kembali?
B: `If it hasn't improved by Monday, come again.` — Jika belum membaik Senin, datang lagi.

### BANK · Buka Rekening Baru · Menengah · B1 · formal
Kamu buka rekening dengan tanya syarat dan biaya jelas.
A: `Good afternoon. I want to open a savings account.` — Siang. Aku mau buka tabungan.
B: `Sure. Do you have your ID card?` — Tentu. Ada KTP?
A: `Yes, here it is. What else do I need?` — Ya, ini dia. Apa lagi yang perlu?
B: `Your student card and fifty thousand deposit.` — Kartu mahasiswa dan deposit 50 ribu.
A: `Is there a monthly admin fee?` — Apakah ada biaya admin bulanan?
B: `Yes, five thousand, unless you are a student.` — Ya, 5 ribu, kecuali mahasiswa.
A: `I'm a student at UGM. Is it free for me?` — Aku mahasiswa UGM. Gratis buatku?
B: `Yes! Students who show KTM pay no fee.` — Ya! Mahasiswa yang tunjukkan KTM gratis.
A: `Great! How long does the card take?` — Bagus! Berapa lama kartunya jadi?
B: `The card that you requested will be ready Friday.` — Kartu yang kamu minta siap Jumat.
A: `Can I use mobile banking today?` — Bisakah pakai mobile banking hari ini?
B: `Yes, I'll help you activate it now.` — Ya, kubantu aktifkan kini.
A: `Thank you! Where should I sign?` — Makasih! Di mana harus tanda tangan?
B: `Here and here. Welcome to our bank!` — Di sini dan sini. Selamat datang!

### SEMINAR · Tanya Jawab Setelah Presentasi · Menengah · B2 · formal
Kamu tanya ke pembicara dengan bahasa akademik sopan.
A: `Thank you for your talk. May I ask a question?` — Makasih presentasinya. Bolehkah bertanya?
B: `Of course. Please go ahead.` — Tentu. Silakan.
A: `You said traffic fell in Tallinn. How was it measured?` — Katamu lalu lintas turun di Tallinn. Bagaimana diukur?
B: `Good question. We used bus GPS and ticket data.` — Pertanyaan bagus. Kami pakai GPS bus dan data tiket.
A: `I see. If the subsidy stopped, would ridership fall?` — Paham. Jika subsidi berhenti, akankah penumpang turun?
B: `Probably. Unless taxes replaced it, numbers would drop.` — Mungkin. Kecuali pajak gantikan, angka akan turun.
A: `That makes sense. Have you compared it with Jakarta?` — Masuk akal. Sudah bandingkan dengan Jakarta?
B: `Not yet. But data that we have suggests similar trends.` — Belum. Tapi data yang ada tunjukkan tren mirip.
A: `I partly agree, however costs here are higher.` — Sebagian setuju, namun biaya di sini lebih tinggi.
B: `Fair point. What would you suggest for funding?` — Poin adil. Apa saran pendanaanmu?
A: `Maybe a small fuel tax plus private ads on buses.` — Mungkin pajak BBM kecil plus iklan swasta di bus.
B: `Interesting! Let's discuss after the session.` — Menarik! Mari diskusi usai sesi.
A: `Sure! Can I send you my paper by email?` — Siap! Bolehkah kirim paper via email?
B: `Absolutely. I look forward to reading it.` — Pasti. Kutunggu bacanya.

### KANTOR · Lembur dan Minta Izin Pulang · Menengah · B2 · formal
Kamu negosiasi lembur dengan atasan tanpa terlihat malas.
A: `Excuse me, Sir. Can we discuss tonight's overtime?` — Permisi, Pak. Bisakah diskusikan lembur malam ini?
B: `Sure. The client needs the report by morning.` — Tentu. Klien butuh laporan pagi.
A: `I understand. I have been working since seven.` — Paham. Aku sudah kerja sejak jam 7.
B: `I know. The team that handles this is only us.` — Tahu. Tim yang pegang ini hanya kita.
A: `Could I finish the charts home and send by ten?` — Bolehkah selesaikan grafik di rumah kirim jam 10?
B: `Hmm. Would the data be safe on your laptop?` — Hmm. Apakah data aman di laptopmu?
A: `Yes, I have encrypted it and use VPN.` — Ya, sudah kuenkripsi dan pakai VPN.
B: `Okay. But if the client calls, will you answer?` — Oke. Tapi jika klien telepon, akankah dijawab?
A: `Of course. I'll be online until midnight.` — Tentu. Aku online sampai tengah malam.
B: `Good. Don't overwork, you look exhausted.` — Bagus. Jangan forsir, kamu kelihatan lelah.
A: `Thank you, Sir. I have already finished half.` — Makasih, Pak. Sudah selesai setengah.
B: `Great! Take a taxi home, the office will pay.` — Hebat! Naik taksi pulang, kantor yang bayar.
A: `Really? Thank you so much!` — Serius? Makasih banyak!

### BEASISWA · Wawancara LPDP Mock · Mahir · C1 · formal
Kamu jawab panel beasiswa dengan thesis dan bukti rapi.
A: `Why do you deserve this scholarship?` — Kenapa layak dapat beasiswa ini?
B: `Because I have taught in Papua for two years.` — Karena sudah mengajar di Papua 2 tahun.
A: `Impressive. What will you study?` — Mengesankan. Apa yang dipelajari?
B: `Public policy. It is evaluation that I focus on.` — Kebijakan publik. Evaluasilah fokusku.
A: `Why not study locally?` — Kenapa tidak kuliah lokal?
B: `Admittedly local is cheaper. Nevertheless, UK labs excel in data.` — Diakui lokal murah. Namun, lab UK unggul data.
A: `Never have we sent someone from your district. Are you ready?` — Belum pernah kami kirim orang daerahmu. Siapkah?
B: `Yes. Not only am I ready, but my school supports me.` — Ya. Tidak hanya siap, sekolahku mendukung.
A: `What if you fail a subject?` — Bagaimana jika gagal satu mapel?
B: `Had I failed, I would retake and report honestly.` — Seandainya gagal, akan mengulang dan lapor jujur.
A: `Good. How will you contribute after?` — Bagus. Bagaimana kontribusi usai lulus?
B: `I will have built a data lab by 2028 in Wamena.` — 2028 aku sudah bangun lab data di Wamena.
A: `That seems ambitious. Evidence?` — Itu ambisius. Buktinya?
B: `I have already trained ten teachers online.` — Sudah kulatih 10 guru online.

### STARTUP · Pitch ke Investor · Mahir · C2 · formal
Kamu pitch aplikasi dengan angka dan idiom bisnis pas.
A: `You have three minutes. What's the problem?` — Kamu punya 3 menit. Apa masalahnya?
B: `Small stalls lose cash because bookkeeping is messy.` — Warung kecil rugi kas karena buku berantakan.
A: `How big is the market?` — Seberapa besar pasar?
B: `Sixty million stalls. The bottom line is huge.` — 60 juta warung. Intinya besar.
A: `What is your solution?` — Apa solusimu?
B: `An app that broke new ground: voice bookkeeping in Javanese.` — Aplikasi perintis: buku suara bahasa Jawa.
A: `Are we on the same page on revenue?` — Sepaham soal pendapatan?
B: `Yes. Freemium plus two percent on QRIS top-ups.` — Ya. Freemium plus 2% top-up QRIS.
A: `Had you launched earlier, you'd be bigger now. Why late?` — Seandainya luncur lebih awal, kini lebih besar. Kenapa telat?
B: `We waited to get the dialect right. It was trust that mattered.` — Kami tunggu dialek pas. Kepercayaanlah yang penting.
A: `Fair. When will you break even?` — Adil. Kapan impas?
B: `We will have broken even by Q4 with 50k users.` — Kami sudah impas Q4 dengan 50rb pengguna.
A: `Okay. Let's touch base next Monday with metrics.` — Oke. Kontak lagi Senin dengan metrik.
B: `Perfect. I'll send the deck tonight. Thank you!` — Sempurna. Kukirim deck malam ini. Makasih!

### SIDANG · Presentasi Skripsi Bab 4 · Mahir · C2 · formal
Kamu pertahankan data dengan hedging dan nominalisasi rapi.
A: `Please summarize your findings in two minutes.` — Ringkas temuanmu 2 menit.
B: `The implementation of QRIS tends to raise turnover.` — Penerapan QRIS cenderung naikkan omzet.
A: `How did you measure turnover?` — Bagaimana ukur omzet?
B: `Through daily logs, which vendors filled for a month.` — Lewat log harian, yang pedagang isi sebulan.
A: `Isn't the sample too small? Only thirty?` — Bukankah sampel terlalu kecil? Hanya 30?
B: `Admittedly it is limited. Nevertheless, trends are consistent.` — Diakui terbatas. Namun, tren konsisten.
A: `What would you have done differently?` — Apa yang dilakukan beda (seandainya)?
B: `Had I had more time, I would have tracked a full quarter.` — Seandainya punya lebih waktu, akan lacak triwulan penuh.
A: `Good reflection. What is your recommendation?` — Refleksi bagus. Apa rekomendasimu?
B: `It is essential that training be paired with subsidies.` — Perlu pelatihan dipasangkan subsidi.
A: `Why subsidies, not just training?` — Kenapa subsidi, bukan latih saja?
B: `Because training alone tends to fade in a month.` — Karena latih saja cenderung pudar sebulan.
A: `Fair enough. Any closing statement?` — Cukup adil. Ada penutup?
B: `In conclusion, small tech plus trust pays off.` — Kesimpulannya, teknologi kecil plus kepercayaan membuahkan hasil.

### KONFERENSI · Networking Coffee Break · Mahir · C1 · santai
Kamu kenalan dengan peneliti asing secara natural.
A: `Hi! Is this seat taken?` — Hai! Kursi ini ada yang duduki?
B: `No, please join! Are you presenting today?` — Tidak, gabung! Apakah presentasi hari ini?
A: `Yes, on bus subsidies. What about you?` — Ya, soal subsidi bus. Kamu?
B: `On air quality. I have been measuring it for a year.` — Soal udara. Sudah kuukur setahun.
A: `Nice! Never have I met someone in that field here.` — Keren! Belum pernah ketemu bidang itu di sini.
B: `Haha, rare species! Where are you from?` — Haha, spesies langka! Dari mana?
A: `Yogyakarta. Have you ever been there?` — Jogja. Pernah ke sana?
B: `Once. What I loved was the street food!` — Sekali. Yang kusuka jajanan jalannya!
A: `Same! So do most of my friends.` — Sama! Kebanyakan temanku juga.
B: `Let's exchange contacts. Are you on LinkedIn?` — Yuk tukar kontak. Ada LinkedIn?
A: `Yes! I'll send my QR. Let's touch base next week.` — Ya! Kukirim QR. Kontak lagi minggu depan.
B: `Perfect. Enjoy the conference!` — Sempurna. Nikmati konferensinya!

## 5. Bacaan

### PENGUMUMAN · Libur Kos Mendadak · Pemula · A1 · 2 menit
Teks: Hi everyone! The water pump is broken. (1) There is no water on the second floor. (2) Please take water from the first floor. (3) The technician will come tomorrow morning. (4) He will fix it before noon. (5) Please save water today and don't shower too long. (6) There are two big buckets near the stairs. (7) You can use them for washing and wudu. (8) Sorry for the trouble. (9) Thank you for understanding! (10)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Di mana tidak ada air? 2. Kapan teknisi datang? 3. Di mana ember besar?
Kunci jawaban: Lantai 2 tidak ada air; teknisi datang besok pagi sebelum siang; ember di dekat tangga.

### DESKRIPSI · Kucingku Si Mochi · Pemula · A1 · 2 menit
Teks: I have a cat named Mochi. (1) She is white and fat. (2) She likes sleeping on my bed. (3) She doesn't like spicy food. (4) Every morning she drinks milk. (5) She is very funny when she chases flies. (6) My sister likes playing with her in the afternoon. (7) We give her fish twice a day. (8) I love her very much! (9)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa warna Mochi? 2. Apa yang diminum tiap pagi? 3. Apa yang dilakukan bersama adik?
Kunci jawaban: Putih dan gemuk; minum susu tiap pagi; main bersama sore hari.

### JADWAL · Kereta Jogja-Solo · Pemula · A2 · 3 menit
Teks: The Prameks train leaves Jogja at 6.30 a.m. every day. (1) It arrives in Solo at 7.45 a.m. (2) Tickets cost thirty thousand rupiah. (3) You can buy them on the app or at the station. (4) The gate closes ten minutes before departure. (5) There are food sellers on the platform every morning. (6) They sell warm tea and fried snacks. (7) I usually sit near the window because the view is nice. (8) Don't be late or you will miss it! (9)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Jam berapa berangkat dan tiba? 2. Berapa harga tiket dan di mana beli? 3. Di mana penulis duduk?
Kunci jawaban: 06.30 berangkat, 07.45 tiba; Rp30 ribu via app/stasiun; dekat jendela.

### CERITA · Banjir Sepatu di Pasar · Pemula · A2 · 3 menit
Teks: Last Sunday I went to the market with my mother. (1) While we were bargaining, heavy rain started. (2) The road was flooded in ten minutes. (3) My new shoes were wet and dirty. (4) We waited at a stall and ate fried bananas. (5) The seller was kind and gave us warm tea. (6) My mother bought cheap vegetables because of the rain. (7) We went home by becak at five. (8) It was annoying but funny too! (9)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa yang terjadi saat menawar? 2. Apa yang dimakan dan diminum saat menunggu? 3. Naik apa pulangnya?
Kunci jawaban: Hujan deras mulai; pisang goreng + teh hangat; becak jam 5.

### TIPS · Hemat Ala Anak Kos · Menengah · B1 · 4 menit
Teks: Living in a boarding house is fun but money runs out fast. (1) First, cook twice a week to save money. (2) For instance, instant noodles plus eggs cost only 10k. (3) Moreover, use public transport instead of ojek daily. (4) A monthly bus pass is much cheaper than daily rides. (5) Unless you track spending, you will panic at month-end. (6) If you save 200k monthly, you will have emergency cash. (7) You should also share WiFi costs with roommates. (8) Don't buy coffee daily if you can brew it. (9) Invite friends to cook together so it is cheaper and fun. (10) Record every expense in a small notebook. (11) Therefore, small habits matter more than big salary. (12) Start this week and feel the difference! (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Sebutkan tiga tips hemat di teks! 2. Apa akibat jika tidak catat pengeluaran? 3. Berapa tabungan darurat disarankan?
Kunci jawaban: Masak 2x seminggu + bus bulanan + patungan WiFi/masak bareng; panik akhir bulan; 200rb/bulan.

### OPINI · HP di Kelas: Larang atau Atur? · Menengah · B1 · 4 menit
Teks: Schools debate whether phones should be banned. (1) Supporters say phones distract students badly. (2) For example, many students scroll TikTok during lessons. (3) However, phones can help learning if used wisely. (4) Students who use dictionaries learn faster. (5) Unless rules are clear, chaos will continue. (6) If teachers guide usage, phones become tools not toys. (7) In my school, phones are collected then returned after class. (8) Teachers who support this say scores have improved. (9) Parents have been asked to monitor screen time at home. (10) Students are taught to take notes digitally twice a week. (11) If this system continues, discipline will improve further. (12) This system seems fair for everyone. (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa argumen pendukung larangan? 2. Apa solusi penulis?
Kunci jawaban: Ganggu belajar (scroll saat pelajaran); kumpulkan lalu kembalikan + panduan guru.

### BERITA · Banjir Semarang Surut · Menengah · B2 · 5 menit
Teks: Floods hit Semarang last night after heavy rain. (1) Officials say 200 houses were damaged. (2) Residents were evacuated to two shelters. (3) While officers were distributing aid, more rain started. (4) If the rain continues, more areas will be flooded. (5) The mayor said he would visit shelters today. (6) He told reporters that aid had arrived from Jakarta. (7) Volunteers who bring boats are badly needed. (8) Donations can be sent to the command post. (9) The water has been rising since midnight and hasn't receded. (10) Schools that are near the river have been closed for two days. (11) Unless the pumps work tonight, the market will stay closed. (12) Stay calm and follow official instructions. (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Berapa rumah rusak dan ke mana warga dievakuasi? 2. Apa yang dikatakan wali kota via reported speech?
Kunci jawaban: 200 rumah, 2 pengungsian; akan kunjungi pengungsian hari ini & bantuan sudah tiba.

### PROFIL · Nadiem Anak Kopi Keliling · Menengah · B2 · 5 menit
Teks: Nadiem sells coffee from his motorbike every morning. (1) He leaves home at 5 a.m. and parks near the station. (2) If it rains, he will move under the bridge. (3) He would expand if he had more capital, but he saves slowly. (4) Customers say his kopi tubruk is as strong as café coffee. (5) He must have served thousands of cups by now. (6) He told me he had started with only 500k. (7) The bike that carries his stall was his father's gift. (8) Unless prices rise sharply, he will keep prices low. (9) He has been waking at 4 a.m. for three years without complaint. (10) While he was brewing yesterday, a tourist offered partnership. (11) If he accepts, he will have opened two stalls by December. (12) His story shows small capital plus consistency wins. (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa yang dilakukan jika hujan? Apa mimpinya? 2. Berapa modal awalnya menurut reported speech?
Kunci jawaban: Pindah bawah jembatan; ingin ekspansi jika ada modal; mulai 500rb.

### EDITORIAL · Bus Gratis: Solusi atau Beban? · Mahir · C1 · 7 menit
Teks: Traffic jams cost Indonesian cities billions yearly. (1) Many argue free buses are the answer. (2) This essay agrees that limited free routes would help greatly. (3) Firstly, free transport eases poverty because students spend 30% on ojek. (4) For instance, in Tallinn free transit increased ridership sharply. (5) Moreover, buses reduce emissions if fleets are electric. (6) Admittedly, budgets are tight and critics fear misuse. (7) Nevertheless, phased pilots plus clear KPIs can offset risks. (8) Not only is it cheap for users, but it is also efficient for cities. (9) Evidence suggests commuters tend to switch when service is reliable. (10) In Tallinn's case, car trips fell while jobs grew. (11) Consequently, cities should trial one corridor first. (12) It is careful design, not hype, that determines success. (13) Having reviewed Tallinn's data, planners tend to favor phased funding. (14) While critics warn about misuse, audits can limit leakage. (15) In the long run, cleaner air plus savings pay off. (16) There appears to be public support when pilots are transparent. (17) It is essential that fares be reviewed yearly. (18) In conclusion, benefits outweigh costs if done carefully. (19)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa thesis penulis? 2. Bagaimana penulis jawab keberatan lawan?
Kunci jawaban: Dukung rute gratis terbatas; akui anggaran ketat lalu jawab dengan pilot bertahap + KPI.

### ESAI · Remote Work: Bebas Tapi Sepi · Mahir · C1 · 7 menit
Teks: Remote work is praised as freedom from commutes. (1) It is flexibility that attracts youth most. (2) What employees gain is time, yet what they lose is connection. (3) The expansion of online tools seems unstoppable. (4) Investment in home offices often yields comfort but rarely teamwork. (5) Never have we been so connected yet so lonely. (6) Social cues tend to fade on screen, and trust may erode slowly. (7) Admittedly, some thrive alone and output rises. (8) However, juniors suffer because mentoring needs presence. (9) A hybrid scheme appears most viable for many firms. (10) For example, three office days plus two remote balances both needs. (11) Failure to design hybrid well may worsen inequality. (12) It is deliberate design, not location, that determines success. (13) Having worked remotely for a year, many report blurred boundaries. (14) There tends to be overwork when home becomes office. (15) While seniors save commuting hours, juniors lose mentoring. (16) Unless firms set core hours, meetings spread chaotically. (17) It is essential that managers be trained for hybrid. (18) In conclusion, flexibility with guardrails wins. (19)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa paradoks utama menurut penulis? 2. Apa solusi yang disarankan?
Kunci jawaban: Terhubung tapi sepi (flexibility vs connection); skema hibrid 3+2 yang dirancang sadar.

### BERITA EKONOMI · Rupiah dan Suku Bunga · Mahir · C2 · 8 menit
Teks: The rupiah wobbled this week as markets eyed rate decisions. (1) Analysts contend pressure comes from global yields, not local politics. (2) Bank Indonesia is believed to hold rates steady for now. (3) Had it hiked sooner, volatility would be lower today, some argue. (4) Exporters who price in dollars benefit, while importers face thinner margins. (5) The bottom line is costs will be passed to consumers. (6) Small traders, whose cash flow is tight, feel the squeeze first. (7) Evidence suggests SMEs tend to cut stock when rates swing. (8) Not only do they delay orders, but they also lay off part-timers. (9) The government says it had secured food supply before prices spiked. (10) Officials told reporters subsidies would roll out next month. (11) Unless global calm returns, hedging seems advisable. (12) What matters most is clear communication, not surprise moves. (13) Firms on the same page with banks will survive. (14) Having faced swings before, veterans tend to lock rates early. (15) While exporters celebrate, street vendors fear price hikes. (16) It is the poor that bear the sharpest squeeze. (17) Not only do costs rise, but jobs also freeze. (18) There seems to be hope if US yields cool next quarter. (19) It is vital that policy be communicated clearly. (20)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa penyebab tekanan rupiah menurut analis? 2. Siapa paling terdampak dan kenapa?
Kunci jawaban: Yield global bukan politik lokal; importir & UMKM cash ketat karena margin tipis & potong stok/PHK.

### OPINI IELTS · Pajak Gula: Setuju dengan Catatan · Mahir · C2 · 8 menit
Teks: Should cities tax sugary drinks to fight obesity? (1) This essay supports limited taxes paired with subsidies. (2) Sugar consumption gives rise to diabetes that burdens hospitals. (3) In Mexico, a 10% tax cut purchases 7% in two years. (4) It is low-income kids who benefit most because they drink most soda. (5) Admittedly, taxes burden small stalls that rely on drink sales. (6) While it is true costs rise, earmarked revenue can fund water stations. (7) If taxes had been paired with education earlier, trust would be higher now. (8) Evidence suggests combined policies tend to work better than solo bans. (9) Moreover, phased rollout lets sellers adapt without shock. (10) For instance, Bogor's car-free Sundays show pilots build acceptance. (11) Never have public health gains come without pushback. (12) Nevertheless, careful design plus review can balance health and jobs. (13) Having taxed for two years, Mexico tends to sustain lower intake. (14) While stalls fear losses, water stations create new income. (15) Unless revenue is earmarked, trust will erode quickly. (16) It is transparent funds that convince skeptics. (17) There appears to be youth support when schools join. (18) It is crucial that reviews be published yearly. (19) In conclusion, tax smartly, subsidize water, and review yearly. (20)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa posisi penulis? 2. Bagaimana penulis tangkal keberatan soal pedagang kecil?
Kunci jawaban: Dukung pajak terbatas + subsidi; akui beban lalu jawab dengan dana earmark + rollout bertahap + contoh.

### UNDANGAN · Arisan RT Sabtu Sore · Pemula · A1 · 2 menit
Teks: Hello neighbours! Our monthly arisan is this Saturday. (1) It starts at four in the afternoon. (2) It is at Mrs. Ani's house near the mosque. (3) Please bring your own plate and spoon. (4) There are door prizes for early birds. (5) We will eat soto together after the draw. (6) Children can play in the yard with my son. (7) Don't be late because the draw is at five sharp. (8) See you there! (9)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Jam berapa mulai dan di mana? 2. Apa yang dibawa dan dimakan?
Kunci jawaban: Jam 4 sore di rumah Bu Ani dekat masjid; bawa piring-sendok, makan soto.

### CHAT · Janjian Futsal Malam Ini · Pemula · A1 · 2 menit
Teks: Hi guys! Are you free tonight? (1) Let's play futsal at eight. (2) The field is behind the campus. (3) It costs twenty thousand per person. (4) I have one ball but we need more water. (5) Doni is bringing his brother too. (6) Don't wear sandals, wear sports shoes! (7) We will go home at ten by ojek together. (8) Reply fast, okay? (9)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Main jam berapa di mana? 2. Berapa biaya dan apa yang dilarang?
Kunci jawaban: Jam 8 lapangan belakang kampus; 20rb/orang, dilarang pakai sandal.

### MENU · Warteg Barokah Buka Sampai Malam · Pemula · A2 · 3 menit
Teks: Welcome to Warteg Barokah! We open every day. (1) We open at seven in the morning. (2) We close at nine at night. (3) A plate of rice costs five thousand. (4) Fried chicken is eight thousand per piece. (5) There is free soup for dine-in customers. (6) We have iced tea for three thousand a glass. (7) Our best seller is fried fish with chili. (8) It is spicy but very tasty. (9) Come with your friends! (10)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Jam buka-tutup? 2. Apa best seller dan harganya?
Kunci jawaban: 07.00-21.00 tiap hari; ikan goreng sambal, nasi 5rb ayam 8rb.

### SURAT · Izin Tidak Masuk Sekolah · Pemula · A2 · 3 menit
Teks: Dear Mrs. Wulan, I am Dita from class 8B. (1) I was sick yesterday with a fever. (2) I went to the clinic with my father. (3) The doctor said I should rest for two days. (4) I didn't do the math homework on page ten. (5) Could I submit it on Monday? (6) My mother has signed this letter below. (7) I will ask Sinta for the notes tonight. (8) Thank you for your kind help! (9)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Kenapa izin dan berapa lama istirahat? 2. Minta apa ke guru?
Kunci jawaban: Demam, istirahat 2 hari; minta kumpul PR matematika Senin.

### TIPS · Packing Mudik 7 Kilo · Menengah · B1 · 4 menit
Teks: Mudik with only 7 kilos is possible if you pack smart. (1) First, roll your clothes instead of folding them. (2) For example, three shirts rolled fit in half a backpack. (3) Moreover, wear your jacket instead of packing it. (4) Jackets that are worn save huge space. (5) Unless you bring souvenirs, your bag stays light. (6) If you need oleh-oleh, buy them at your destination. (7) Put chargers in a small pouch so they don't tangle. (8) Bring a refill bottle because stations have free water. (9) Travelers who pack light move faster through crowds. (10) You should also screenshot tickets in case signal dies. (11) If you follow these steps, you will board relaxed. (12) Therefore, pack less and enjoy more! (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Sebutkan 3 tips packing! 2. Kenapa bawa botol isi ulang?
Kunci jawaban: Gulung baju, pakai jaket, pouch charger + screenshot tiket; karena ada air gratis di stasiun.

### RESEP · Seblak Level 3 Pedas Nampol · Menengah · B1 · 4 menit
Teks: Seblak is loved because it is cheap, spicy, and fast. (1) First, soak 50 grams of crackers for ten minutes. (2) Crackers that are pink usually taste more savory. (3) Then, grind garlic, kencur, and five chilies. (4) If you hate super spicy, use only two chilies. (5) Heat oil and fry the paste until it smells good. (6) Add one egg and stir quickly so it doesn't clump. (7) Pour 300 ml water and add the crackers. (8) Unless you stir often, the crackers will stick. (9) Add salt, sugar, and a little cheese if you like. (10) Cooks who sell seblak often add sausage too. (11) If it is too spicy, add an extra egg. (12) Serve hot and enjoy with friends! (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa 3 bahan bumbu diulek? 2. Apa solusi jika terlalu pedas/ lengket?
Kunci jawaban: Bawang, kencur, cabai; tambah telur, sering aduk.

### LAPORAN · Gotong Royong Minggu Pagi · Menengah · B2 · 5 menit
Teks: Our RT held gotong royong last Sunday morning. (1) Twenty residents gathered at seven with brooms and sacks. (2) While mothers were sweeping, fathers fixed the broken ditch. (3) If it hadn't rained, we would have finished by nine. (4) But rain that fell at eight slowed us down. (5) Teenagers who brought speakers played dangdut to lift spirits. (6) The head of RT said he had prepared fried snacks for all. (7) He told us that the city would donate paint next month. (8) Unless more youth join, the alley will stay dirty. (9) We have been cleaning this alley for three months regularly. (10) By December, we will have repainted all walls. (11) At 10, we'll be planting chilies along the fence. (12) Everyone agreed the alley looks brighter now. (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa yang dikerjakan ibu vs bapak? 2. Apa janji ketua RT via reported?
Kunci jawaban: Ibu menyapu, bapak perbaiki selokan; siapkan gorengan + kota sumbang cat bulan depan.

### WAWANCARA · Tukang Parkir Teladan · Menengah · B2 · 5 menit
Teks: Pak Slamet has guarded the market parking for ten years. (1) He arrives at 5 a.m. and leaves after maghrib. (2) If it rains, he will wear a plastic coat and keep working. (3) He would have quit if his kids hadn't supported him. (4) Drivers say his whistle is as loud as a referee's. (5) He must have arranged a million motorbikes by now. (6) He told me he had once returned a wallet full of cash. (7) The wallet that he found had three million inside. (8) Unless people respect him, chaos will return quickly. (9) He has been standing in heat for a decade without umbrella. (10) While he was guiding yesterday, a reporter interviewed him. (11) If donations continue, he will have built a shelter by June. (12) His honesty shows small jobs carry big dignity. (13)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa bukti kejujurannya? 2. Apa rencananya jika donasi lanjut?
Kunci jawaban: Kembalikan dompet 3 juta; bangun shelter Juni.

### ANALISIS · Sampah Plastik Pasar Tradisional · Mahir · C1 · 7 menit
Teks: Traditional markets produce mountains of plastic daily. (1) It is convenience that drives sellers to wrap everything. (2) What buyers want is speed, yet what the planet needs is restraint. (3) The consumption of single-use bags has doubled in five years. (4) Regulation tends to fail when enforcement is weak. (5) Never have bans worked without affordable alternatives. (6) Admittedly, banana leaves cost more and tear easily. (7) Nevertheless, pilots in Denpasar cut plastic 30% in six months. (8) Not only did vendors switch, but shoppers also brought baskets. (9) Evidence suggests habits tend to stick after three weeks. (10) While elders adapt quickly, youth often forget bags. (11) Unless levies fund free totes, backsliding seems likely. (12) Having studied four cities, researchers favor gradual fees. (13) There seems to be support for a 500-rupiah charge. (14) It is essential that revenue be used for alternatives. (15) Despite tight budgets, markets can pilot refills. (16) Whereas malls charge for bags, wet markets lag. (17) In the long run, reuse pays off for all. (18) In conclusion, price plastic and fund green wraps. (19)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa thesis dan bukti Denpasar? 2. Apa rekomendasi penulis?
Kunci jawaban: Dukung biaya plastik bertahap; Denpasar turun 30% 6 bulan; tarif 500 + dana tote/refill.

### OPINI · Motor Listrik: Solusi atau Gaya? · Mahir · C1 · 7 menit
Teks: Electric motorbikes crowd Jakarta ads this year. (1) It is running cost that attracts drivers most. (2) What riders save is fuel, yet what they risk is battery debt. (3) The adoption of e-bikes seems unstoppable in big cities. (4) Subsidies tend to pull buyers faster than bans. (5) Rarely does a new tech spread without repair shops. (6) Admittedly, charging takes hours and swaps confuse elders. (7) However, ojek who tested swaps report higher income. (8) Not only is fuel cheaper, but maintenance is simpler. (9) Data suggests users tend to charge overnight safely. (10) While Jakarta pilots swaps, Surabaya waits for stations. (11) Unless batteries standardize, chaos may grow. (12) Having ridden 100 km, testers praise torque highly. (13) There appears to be youth excitement for silent rides. (14) It is vital that safety be taught before sales. (15) Despite high upfront cost, totals favor electric. (16) Whereas petrol bikes roar, electrics glide quietly. (17) In conclusion, subsidize swaps and train mechanics. (18) Failure to standardize may stall the shift. (19)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa daya tarik utama dan risikonya? 2. Apa syarat sukses menurut penulis?
Kunci jawaban: Hemat BBM vs utang baterai/cas lama; standardisasi baterai + subsidi swap + latih bengkel.

### EDITORIAL · Air Bersih: Siapa Bayar? · Mahir · C2 · 8 menit
Teks: Clean water scarcity hits Java's north coast yearly. (1) Analysts contend overuse, not rainfall, causes most shortages. (2) Wells that are drilled deeper now taste salty. (3) PDAM is believed to lose 30% to leaks. (4) Had it fixed pipes sooner, pressure would be stable today. (5) Factories which draw groundwater face looser checks. (6) The bottom line is households pay the price. (7) Poor alleys, whose pipes are oldest, queue nightly. (8) Evidence suggests the poor tend to pay more per liter. (9) Not only do they buy gallons, but they also lose work hours. (10) The mayor says he had secured funds before the dry peak. (11) He told voters subsidies would roll out in August. (12) Unless metering expands, theft seems hard to curb. (13) What matters most is fair tariffs, not flat hikes. (14) Communities on the same page with utilities save more. (15) Having piloted meters in two wards, losses fell 15%. (16) While industry resists caps, residents back quotas. (17) If caps had been set earlier, aquifers would be healthier now. (18) It is essential that extraction be licensed strictly. (19) There seems to be will if data stays public. (20) In sum, meter fairly and fix leaks first. (21)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa penyebab utama menurut analis? 2. Siapa paling dirugikan dan buktinya?
Kunci jawaban: Overuse bukan hujan; warga miskin pipa tua antre + bayar/liter lebih mahal.

### ESAI · AI di Kelas: Asisten atau Pengganti? · Mahir · C2 · 8 menit
Teks: Should AI tutors replace teachers in remote schools? (1) This essay argues AI assists but cannot replace. (2) Personalized drills give rise to faster basics for many. (3) In trials, scores rose 12% where AI guided practice. (4) It is patience that AI offers best: endless repetition. (5) Admittedly, devices plus power burden poor districts. (6) While it is true AI scales cheaply, mentoring needs humans. (7) If tutors had been paired with teachers earlier, trust would be higher now. (8) Data suggests mixed models tend to beat solo tech. (9) Moreover, phased training lets teachers adapt calmly. (10) For instance, Sumba's pilot paired tablets with local mentors. (11) Never have gains lasted without teacher buy-in. (12) Nevertheless, auto-grading plus chat help saves hours. (13) Having graded thousands, AI frees weekends for coaching. (14) While urban schools surge ahead, rural ones risk lag. (15) Unless connectivity funds grow, gaps may widen. (16) It is blended routines that seem most viable. (17) There appears to be parent support when results show. (18) It is crucial that data be kept private. (19) In conclusion, deploy AI as aide, train teachers first. (20)
Pertanyaan pemahaman: 1. Apa posisi penulis? 2. Apa bukti dan syarat suksesnya?
Kunci jawaban: AI asisten bukan pengganti; trial +12% & Sumba; blended + latih guru + konektivitas + privasi data.

## 6. Pola Tata Bahasa

### To Be Present (am/is/are) · Pemula · A1 · Grammar dasar
- Deskripsi singkat: Fondasi perkenalan dan status, wajib sebelum main kata kerja.
- Formula/rumus: S + am/is/are + Adj/Noun | Are you...? | S + is not...
- 2 contoh kalimat: `She is a nurse.` — Dia seorang perawat. | `Are they ready?` — Apakah mereka siap?
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `I am happy today.` — Aku senang hari ini. | Negatif: `She is not busy now.` — Dia tidak sibuk kini. | Tanya: `Are you ready?` — Apakah kamu siap?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Menambah be sebelum kata kerja (I'm agree) dan bilang I have 20 years untuk umur.

### Artikel A/An/The · Pemula · A1 · Grammar dasar
- Deskripsi singkat: Penentu spesifik atau umum di depan kata benda.
- Formula/rumus: a/an + N tunggal umum | the + N spesifik/unik | - (nol) + N umum jamak/abstrak
- 2 contoh kalimat: `An hour is enough.` — Satu jam cukup. | `The moon is bright.` — Bulannya cerah.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `I saw a cat.` — Aku melihat seekor kucing. | Negatif: `It is not the cat I meant.` — Itu bukan kucing yang kumaksud. | Tanya: `Did you see an owl?` — Apakah kamu melihat burung hantu?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Skip artikel total (I bought car) atau pakai the untuk hal umum (The love is blind).

### Present Simple -s · Pemula · A1 · Tense dasar
- Deskripsi singkat: Kebiasaan dan jadwal tetap dengan -s untuk he/she/it.
- Formula/rumus: S + V(s/es) | Do/Does + S + V? | S + do/does not + V
- 2 contoh kalimat: `He watches anime at night.` — Dia nonton anime malam hari. | `Does she like sambal?` — Apakah dia suka sambal?
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `She works in a bank.` — Dia bekerja di bank. | Negatif: `He does not eat pork.` — Dia tidak makan babi. | Tanya: `Do they live here?` — Apakah mereka tinggal di sini?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Lupa -s (he work) atau double -s (does she likes?).

### There Is/Are + Some/Any · Pemula · A1 · Existential
- Deskripsi singkat: Menyatakan keberadaan dan jumlah umum.
- Formula/rumus: There is + N tunggal/uncountable | There are + N jamak | some (+), any (?/-)
