- 2 contoh kalimat: `There are some mangoes.` — Ada beberapa mangga. | `Is there any milk?` — Apakah ada susu?
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `There is a lamp on the table.` — Ada lampu di atas meja. | Negatif: `There isn't any sugar left.` — Tidak ada gula tersisa. | Tanya: `Are there any shops nearby?` — Apakah ada toko di dekat sini?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: There is many people dan I don't have some money.

### Preposisi In/On/At · Pemula · A2 · Preposisi
- Deskripsi singkat: Titik (at), permukaan (on), ruang/waktu besar (in).
- Formula/rumus: at + jam/titik | on + hari/tanggal/permukaan | in + bulan/tahun/kota/ruang
- 2 contoh kalimat: `At 7 on Monday in July.` — Jam 7 hari Senin bulan Juli. | `Keys are on the table in the room.` — Kunci di atas meja di kamar.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `She lives in Makassar.` — Dia tinggal di Makassar. | Negatif: `He isn't at the bus stop.` — Dia tidak di halte. | Tanya: `Are you free on Friday?` — Apakah kamu luang hari Jumat?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Pukul rata in semua (in the bus stop, in Monday).

### Like + V-ing · Pemula · A2 · Gerund dasar
- Deskripsi singkat: Hobi dan kesukaan memakai kata kerja -ing.
- Formula/rumus: like/love/hate/enjoy + V-ing
- 2 contoh kalimat: `I like swimming.` — Aku suka berenang. | `They hate queuing.` — Mereka benci antre.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `I enjoy cooking rendang.` — Aku suka memasak rendang. | Negatif: `She doesn't like waiting long.` — Dia tidak suka menunggu lama. | Tanya: `Do you like jogging?` — Apakah kamu suka jogging?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: I like swim, I like to swimming, I'm like gaming.

### Present Perfect vs Past Simple · Menengah · B1 · Tense menengah
- Deskripsi singkat: Pengalaman tanpa waktu (have + V3) vs kejadian bertanggal (V2).
- Formula/rumus: S + have/has + V3 (no specific time) | S + V2 + yesterday/last.../in...
- 2 contoh kalimat: `I have seen Borobudur.` — Aku pernah lihat Borobudur. | `I saw it in 2022.` — Aku melihatnya 2022.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `She has tried durian.` — Dia pernah coba durian. | Negatif: `I haven't finished my homework yet.` — Aku belum selesaikan PR. | Tanya: `Have you ever been to Bali?` — Pernahkah kamu ke Bali?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: I have visited Bali last year (campur have + waktu spesifik).

### Conditional 1 (If Will) · Menengah · B1 · Conditional
- Deskripsi singkat: Syarat realistis masa depan.
- Formula/rumus: If + present, will + V | Unless + present, will + V
- 2 contoh kalimat: `If you save, you will buy it.` — Jika menabung, kamu akan membelinya. | `Unless you hurry, you'll be late.` — Kecuali buru-buru, kamu telat.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `If it rains, I will stay home.` — Jika hujan, aku di rumah. | Negatif: `If you don't hurry, you won't catch it.` — Jika tidak buru-buru, kamu tak keburu. | Tanya: `What will you do if it rains?` — Apa yang kamu lakukan jika hujan?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: If it will rain (will bocor ke klausa if).

### Passive Be + V3 · Menengah · B1 · Voice
- Deskripsi singkat: Fokus ke objek/aksi saat pelaku tak penting.
- Formula/rumus: be (ikut tense) + V3 (+ by agent jika penting)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Batik is sold here.` — Batik dijual di sini. | `Road was repaired yesterday.` — Jalan diperbaiki kemarin.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `The temple was built in 1800.` — Candi dibangun 1800. | Negatif: `The road isn't repaired yet.` — Jalan belum diperbaiki. | Tanya: `Was the bridge opened today?` — Apakah jembatan dibuka hari ini?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Lupa be (My phone stolen) dan overuse passive padahal aktif lebih pas.

### Reported Speech Mundur Tense · Menengah · B2 · Reported
- Deskripsi singkat: Kutip ulang dengan mundur satu tense dan geser waktu.
- Formula/rumus: said + (that) + past/past perfect/would | told + O + to-V | asked + if/WH + S + V (tanpa ?)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `He said he was busy.` — Dia bilang dia sibuk. | `She asked if I was ready.` — Dia tanya apakah aku siap.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `He said he liked coffee.` — Dia bilang suka kopi. | Negatif: `She said she hadn't finished it.` — Dia bilang belum selesaikannya. | Tanya: `Did he ask if I was ready?` — Apakah dia tanya apakah aku siap?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Tidak mundurkan (He said he is busy) dan told that tanpa objek orang.

### Relative Clause Who/Which · Menengah · B2 · Clause
- Deskripsi singkat: Gabung kalimat dengan pewatas orang/benda.
- Formula/rumus: N + who/that (orang) + clause | N + which/that (benda) + clause | Non-defining pakai koma + who/which (no that)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Girl who sits here is smart.` — Gadis yang duduk sini pintar. | `Phone, which I bought yesterday, is lost.` — HP, yang kubeli kemarin, hilang.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `The man who helped me is kind.` — Pria yang membantuku baik. | Negatif: `The book which isn't sold here is rare.` — Buku yang tidak dijual di sini langka. | Tanya: `Is she the girl who won?` — Apakah dia gadis yang menang?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Double pronoun (book which I bought it) dan that setelah koma.

### Modal Deduction Must/Can't/Might · Menengah · B2 · Modal
- Deskripsi singkat: Dugaan kini/lampau berdasar bukti.
- Formula/rumus: must/can't/might + be + Adj (kini) | must/can't/might + have + V3 (lampau)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `She must be home.` — Dia pasti di rumah. | `It must have rained.` — Pasti telah hujan.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `He must be tired after work.` — Dia pasti lelah usai kerja. | Negatif: `She can't be the thief.` — Dia tidak mungkin pencurinya. | Tanya: `Could they have missed the bus?` — Mungkinkah mereka ketinggalan bus?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Must forgot tanpa have, dan mustn't be untuk dugaan negatif (harusnya can't be).

### Inversion Negatif Depan · Mahir · C1 · Emphasis
- Deskripsi singkat: Penekanan formal dengan membalik aux dan subjek.
- Formula/rumus: Never/Rarely/Not only/No sooner + aux + S + V
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Never have I seen this.` — Belum pernah kulihat ini. | `Not only is it cheap, but it's fast.` — Tak hanya murah, tapi cepat.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `Rarely does the bus arrive on time.` — Jarang bus tepat waktu. | Negatif: `Never have I cheated in exams.` — Tak pernah aku menyontek ujian. | Tanya: `Have you ever seen such a queue?` — Pernahkah kamu lihat antrean seperti itu? (inversion tanya retoris)
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Lupa aux (Never I have seen) dan overuse tiap kalimat.

### Cleft It/What · Mahir · C1 · Emphasis
- Deskripsi singkat: Sorot satu unsur dengan membelah kalimat.
- Formula/rumus: It is/was + fokus + that/who | What + clause + is/was + fokus
- 2 contoh kalimat: `It was Rani who helped.` — Rani-lah yang membantu. | `What I need is sleep.` — Yang kubutuhkan adalah tidur.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `It is discipline that wins.` — Disiplinlah yang menang. | Negatif: `It wasn't money that made him happy.` — Bukan uang yang membuatnya bahagia. | Tanya: `Was it Rani who called?` — Apakah Rani yang menelepon?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Tense tidak konsisten (It is ... who cook untuk lampau) dan What I need is sleeping.

### Nominalisation + Hedging · Mahir · C1 · Academic
- Deskripsi singkat: Formalkan dengan kata benda dan lunakkan klaim mutlak.
- Formula/rumus: V → N (-tion/-ment/-ness): decide→decision | Claim → tend to/may/suggests + V
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Decision to delay was wise.` — Keputusan menunda bijak. | `Teens tend to scroll more.` — Remaja cenderung scroll lebih.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `The construction took five years.` — Pembangunan memakan lima tahun. | Negatif: `Pollution doesn't tend to drop quickly.` — Polusi tidak cenderung turun cepat. | Tanya: `Does this policy tend to help the poor?` — Apakah kebijakan ini cenderung bantu miskin?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Kalimat beranak karena tak nominalkan, dan spam always/never/all.

### Mixed Conditional · Mahir · C2 · Conditional lanjut
- Deskripsi singkat: Silang waktu sebab dan hasil beda era.
- Formula/rumus: If + past perfect, would + V (now) | If + past simple, would have + V3 (past result)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `If I'd saved, I'd own a camera now.` — Jika dulu nabung, kini punya kamera. | `If he were diligent, he'd have finished.` — Jika rajin, dia sudah selesai.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `If I had slept early, I wouldn't be sleepy now.` — Jika tidur cepat, kini tak ngantuk. | Negatif: `If she hadn't skipped class, she wouldn't be confused now.` — Jika tidak bolos, kini tak bingung. | Tanya: `Would you be rich now if you had bought land?` — Apakah kini kaya seandainya dulu beli tanah?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Dua sisi would have semua, dan If I would have known.

### Causative Have Something Done · Mahir · C2 · Causative
- Deskripsi singkat: Minta orang lain kerjakan jasa.
- Formula/rumus: have/get + O + V3
- 2 contoh kalimat: `I had my hair cut.` — Aku potong rambut (di barber). | `Got my phone fixed.` — HP-ku diperbaiki (tukang servis).
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `I had my motorbike serviced.` — Motor diservis (bengkel). | Negatif: `I haven't had my AC repaired yet.` — AC belum diperbaiki (teknisi). | Tanya: `Have you had your hair cut recently?` — Apakah rambutmu dipotong (barber) baru-baru ini?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: I cut my hair untuk ke barber (kesan potong sendiri) dan lupa V3 (had my hair cut? no, had my hair cutting).

### Collocation & Idiom Bisnis · Mahir · C2 · Vocab lanjut
- Deskripsi singkat: Pasangan kata tetap dan idiom arena bisnis.
- Formula/rumus: make + mistake | take + medicine | heavy + rain | roll out = launch | bottom line = inti
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Heavy rain delayed rollout.` — Hujan deras tunda peluncuran. | `Bottom line is sales.` — Intinya penjualan.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `We rolled out the app last month.` — Kami meluncurkan aplikasi bulan lalu. | Negatif: `We haven't cut costs yet.` — Kami belum memangkas biaya. | Tanya: `Are we on the same page about deadlines?` — Apakah kita sepaham soal tenggat?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Big rain, drink medicine, underline untuk bottom line, dan idiom gaul di esai formal.

### Imperative + Let's (Perintah & Ajakan) · Pemula · A1 · Instruction
- Deskripsi singkat: Perintah singkat dan ajakan bareng tanpa subjek.
- Formula/rumus: V1 + O! | Don't + V1! | Let's + V1!
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Open the door, please.` — Tolong buka pintu. | `Let's eat bakso!` — Ayo makan bakso!
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `Stir the noodles well.` — Aduk mie dengan baik. | Negatif: `Don't be late again!` — Jangan telat lagi! | Tanya: `Shall we go now?` — Pergikah kita kini?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Let's to go / Let's going (harus Let's go polos).

### This/That/These/Those + Plural · Pemula · A1 · Demonstrative
- Deskripsi singkat: Tunjuk dekat/jauh tunggal/jamak plus jamak beraturan.
- Formula/rumus: This/That + N tunggal | These/Those + N jamak
- 2 contoh kalimat: `This is my key.` — Ini kunciku. | `Those buses are full.` — Bus-bus itu penuh.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `These are fresh mangoes.` — Ini mangga segar. | Negatif: `That isn't my bag.` — Itu bukan tasku. | Tanya: `Is this yours?` — Apakah ini milikmu?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: This are / that ones tanpa bedakan tunggal-jamak.

### Possessive Adjective + 's · Pemula · A1 · Possessive
- Deskripsi singkat: Milik dengan my/your dan nama + 's.
- Formula/rumus: my/your/his/her + N | Nama + 's + N | Whose...?
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Rina's bag is red.` — Tas Rina merah. | `Whose jacket is this?` — Jaket siapa ini?
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `This is my sister.` — Ini adikku. | Negatif: `It isn't his helmet.` — Itu bukan helmnya. | Tanya: `Is this your book?` — Apakah ini bukumu?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Me name (harus my) dan your's pakai apostrof.

### Going To vs Will · Pemula · A2 · Future dasar
- Deskripsi singkat: Rencana matang vs keputusan spontan.
- Formula/rumus: be + going to + V1 (rencana) | will + V1 (spontan/janji)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `We are going to move.` — Kami akan pindah (rencana). | `I'll help you now.` — Akan kubantu kini (spontan).
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `She is going to cook tonight.` — Dia akan masak malam ini. | Negatif: `I won't forget your birthday.` — Aku tak lupa ultahmu. | Tanya: `Are you going to join us?` — Apakah kamu ikut kami?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: I will going to (pilih satu, jangan gabung).

### Past Simple vs Past Continuous · Menengah · B1 · Tense
- Deskripsi singkat: Aksi potong (simple) vs latar jalan (continuous).
- Formula/rumus: was/were + V-ing (latar) | V2 (potong) | while + continuous, when + simple
- 2 contoh kalimat: `I was eating when you called.` — Aku sedang makan saat kamu telepon. | `While we ate, it rained.` — Saat kami makan, hujan.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `They were playing at midnight.` — Mereka sedang main tengah malam. | Negatif: `I wasn't sleeping at 9.` — Aku tidak sedang tidur jam 9. | Tanya: `Were you queuing when I came?` — Apakah kamu sedang antre saat aku datang?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Dua sisi past simple semua sehingga nuansa latar hilang.

### Comparative -er vs More/Most · Menengah · B1 · Comparison
- Deskripsi singkat: Banding pendek -er dan panjang more/most.
- Formula/rumus: Adj pendek + -er/-est + than | more/most + Adj panjang | as + Adj + as
- 2 contoh kalimat: `This is cheaper than that.` — Ini lebih murah dari itu. | `This is the most beautiful here.` — Ini terindah di sini.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `Today is hotter than yesterday.` — Hari ini lebih panas dari kemarin. | Negatif: `It isn't as cold as Dieng.` — Tidak sedingin Dieng. | Tanya: `Is your room bigger than mine?` — Apakah kamarmu lebih besar dari kamarku?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: More cheaper (double) dan more better.

### Gerund vs Infinitive · Menengah · B2 · Verb pattern
- Deskripsi singkat: -ing wajib vs to wajib vs bisa dua dengan beda arti.
- Formula/rumus: enjoy/avoid/finish + V-ing | want/decide/plan + to-V | stop + V-ing (berhenti) vs to-V (mampir)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `I enjoy swimming.` — Aku suka berenang. | `I decided to move.` — Aku memutuskan pindah.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `She finished washing dishes.` — Dia selesai cuci piring. | Negatif: `Don't stop trying!` — Jangan berhenti mencoba! | Tanya: `Do you want to join us?` — Maukah kamu gabung kami?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Enjoy to swim dan decide going.

### Question Tags · Menengah · B2 · Interaction
- Deskripsi singkat: Ekor tanya untuk konfirmasi dan basa-basi.
- Formula/rumus: Positif + tag negatif | Negatif + tag positif | Let's + shall we?
- 2 contoh kalimat: `You're tired, aren't you?` — Kamu lelah, kan? | `Let's go, shall we?` — Pergi yuk, mau kan?
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `She works here, doesn't she?` — Dia kerja di sini, kan? | Negatif: `You haven't eaten, have you?` — Kamu belum makan, kan? | Tanya: `Isn't it a nice day?` — Bukankah hari yang indah? (tag sebagai tanya)
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Isn't it untuk orang (harus aren't you).

### Subjunctive Formal (be/confirm) · Mahir · C1 · Formal
- Deskripsi singkat: Rekomendasi formal dengan kata kerja polos setelah that.
- Formula/rumus: It is essential + that + S + V polos | suggest/demand + that + polos
- 2 contoh kalimat: `It is essential that he be here.` — Perlu dia di sini. | `I suggest she go now.` — Kusarankan dia pergi kini.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `We insist payment be made today.` — Kami desak bayar hari ini. | Negatif: `It is vital he not be late.` — Penting dia tidak telat. | Tanya: `Should the report be sent today?` — Haruskah laporan dikirim hari ini? (versi should sopan)
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: It is essential that he is (harus be polos).

### Participle Clauses (V-ing/V3) · Mahir · C1 · Conciseness
- Deskripsi singkat: Pangkas who/which dengan V-ing aktif dan V3 pasif.
- Formula/rumus: S sama + V-ing (aktif) | S sama + V3 (pasif) | Having + V3 (duluan)
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Walking home, I saw sunset.` — Berjalan pulang, kulihat senja. | `Made in China, it is cheap.` — Dibuat di China, ia murah.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `Having finished, I slept.` — Setelah selesai, aku tidur. | Negatif: `Not having cash, we paid QRIS.` — Karena tak bawa tunai, bayar QRIS. | Tanya: `Having read this, do you get it?` — Setelah baca ini, pahamkah?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Subjek gantung (dangling) beda pelaku.

### Fronting + Extraposition (It/There) · Mahir · C2 · Emphasis
- Deskripsi singkat: Depankan fokus dan pecah subjek panjang.
- Formula/rumus: Objek + S + V (fronting) | It is + Adj + to-V/that | There + seem + to be
- 2 contoh kalimat: `This rule we must obey.` — Aturan ini wajib patuhi. | `It is hard to park here.` — Sulit parkir di sini.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `There seems to be an error.` — Tampaknya ada galat. | Negatif: `It isn't easy to quit smoking.` — Tidak mudah berhenti merokok. | Tanya: `Is it true that prices rose?` — Benarkah harga naik?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Dobel subjek (That book, I I like it).

### Hedged Idiom + Concession · Mahir · C2 · Academic voice
- Deskripsi singkat: Akui lawan (although/despite) lalu tikung dengan hedging + idiom.
- Formula/rumus: Although + klausa / Despite + N | tend to/seem to + V | Admittedly..., nevertheless...
- 2 contoh kalimat: `Despite rain, we left.` — Meski hujan, kami pergi. | `Teens tend to scroll more.` — Remaja cenderung scroll lebih.
- 3 bentuk: Positif: `Although tired, she finished it.` — Walau lelah, dia selesaikan. | Negatif: `Despite funds, it didn't succeed.` — Meski dana, tak berhasil. | Tanya: `Does this tend to work here?` — Apakah ini cenderung berhasil di sini?
- Kesalahan umum penutur Indonesia: Although + but dobel dan always/never mutlak.

## 7. Persiapan Ujian

### IELTS (Academic & General)
- Format & bagian, estimasi durasi, ambang umum: Listening 4 bagian ±30 menit + 10 menit transfer; Reading 3 teks ±60 menit 40 soal; Writing 2 tugas ±60 menit (Task 1 150 kata, Task 2 250 kata); Speaking 3 bagian ±11-14 menit. Skor 0-9 per seksi lalu rata-rata (band). Gambaran umum: banyak kampus luar meminta sekitar band 6-6.5 untuk S1 dan 6.5-7.5 untuk pascasarjana/beasiswa (bukan angka resmi mutlak, cek tiap kampus). Tidak ada lulus/gagal, yang ada syarat band.
- 5 strategi mengerjakan yang paling penting:
1. Baca soal dulu baru teks (Reading/Listening) agar tahu kata kunci yang diburu.
2. Alokasi Task 2 dua kali Task 1 (20 menit vs 40 menit) karena bobot dua kali lipat.
3. Parafrase, jangan copas soal; variasi struktur (inversion/cleft/passive sekali-sekali).
4. Untuk True/False/Not Given: tanpa bukti jelas = Not Given, jangan over-infer.
5. Speaking Part 2 pakai 1 menit persiapan buat kerangka who-what-why-feeling, bicara 1.5-2 menit penuh.
- 3 contoh soal representatif + kunci + pembahasan:
1. Writing Task 2: Some think free public transport solves traffic. Discuss both views and give opinion. | Kunci: Esai 4 paragraf thesis + 2 body PEEL + counter + kesimpulan. | Pembahasan: Jawab both views (dampak + biaya), beri opini tegas, contoh lokal, hedging.
2. Reading T/F/NG: "Tallinn's free transit increased ridership." Teks menyebut ridership rose sharply. | Kunci: True. | Pembahasan: Sinonim increased = rose, ada bukti langsung.
3. Listening: Announcement "Gate closes 30 min before 10.00 departure." Kapan tutup? | Kunci: 09.30. | Pembahasan: Kurangi 30 menit, jebakan 10.30 (tambah).

### TOEFL iBT
- Format & bagian, estimasi durasi, ambang umum: Reading 2-3 teks ±35 menit; Listening 3-5 audio ±36 menit; Speaking 4 tugas ±16 menit; Writing 2 tugas (integrated + discussion) ±29 menit. Total ±2 jam. Skor 0-120 (tiap seksi 0-30). Gambaran umum: banyak kampus AS meminta total sekitar 70-90 untuk S1 dan 90-100+ untuk S2/beasiswa (rentang umum, bukan syarat resmi semua kampus).
- 5 strategi mengerjakan yang paling penting:
1. Catat who-what-why saat Listening, jangan kejar transkrip kata per kata.
2. Integrated Writing: baca 3 menit → dengar bantahan → tulis hubungan kuliah vs bacaan (contradicts/supports).
3. Speaking template 15 detik siap: opinion + reason 1 + example + reason 2 + close.
4. Reading: jawab vocab-in-context via kalimat tetangga (definisi/contoh/kontras), bukan hafalan.
5. Jaga pace: jangan >2 menit per soal Reading, tandai dan lanjut.
- 3 contoh soal representatif + kunci + pembahasan:
1. Integrated: Reading pro sugar tax, lecture bantah dengan data jobs. Apa hubungan? | Kunci: Lecture casts doubt / contradicts. | Pembahasan: Cari kata bantahan (however, fails to consider) lalu rangkum 2-3 poin lawan.
2. Listening inference: Nada pembicara "Regrettably, enforcement is weak." Sikap? | Kunci: Critical/disappointed. | Pembahasan: Adverb regrettably = bocoran nada negatif.
3. Structure: "Not only ___ cheap but fast." | Kunci: is it. | Pembahasan: Not only depan memicu inversion.

### TOEIC Listening & Reading
- Format & bagian, estimasi durasi, ambang umum: Listening 100 soal ±45 menit (foto, Q-R, percakapan, talk); Reading 100 soal ±75 menit (incomplete sentences, error recognition versi lama / text completion + comprehension). Skor 10-990 total. Gambaran umum: low 120-225 (dasar), menengah 550-700 (komunikasi kerja umum), 800+ (mahir kerja) — rentang umum untuk orientasi, bukan standar kelulusan resmi.
- 5 strategi mengerjakan yang paling penting:
1. Lihat foto 5 detik sebelum audio: tebak aksi (is being + V3) dan posisi (on/under/beside).
2. Part 5 (grammar singkat): baca 4 opsi dulu, tentukan jenis (tense/preposisi/kolokasi) baru baca kalimat.
3. Part 7: baca pertanyaan (bukan opsi) dulu, scanning email/pengumuman cari angka/nama.
4. Jangan kosongkan: tidak ada minus, isi semua walau tebak.
5. Hafalkan 50 kolokasi kantor (attached, appreciate, confirm, delay, shipment) karena keluar terus.
- 3 contoh soal representatif + kunci + pembahasan:
1. Foto: Man ___ documents at desk. Opsi: is reading / reads / read / reading. | Kunci: is reading. | Pembahasan: Aksi berlangsung di foto memakai continuous.
2. Grammar: Please find ___ the report. Opsi: attach/attached/attachment/attaching. | Kunci: attached. | Pembahasan: Kolokasi tetap find attached.
3. Email: Meeting moved to Friday, where? Teks "moved from Thu to Fri, Room B." | Kunci: Room B Friday. | Pembahasan: Scanning kata moved + Room.

## 8. Simulasi Ujian

### IELTS Listening (Full Set)
- Durasi: 30 menit + 10 menit transfer
- Jumlah soal: 20
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Dengar tiap audio sekali saja dan tulis jawaban singkat; perhatikan batas kata.

Soal:
1. Form: Nama pemesan kos — "My name is Rina Sari, R-I-N-A." Tulis nama depan. | A) Rina B) Rini C) Riana D) Rino | Jawaban: A
2. Nomor HP dibaca "oh-eight-one-two-seven." Digit ke-3? | A) 8 B) 1 C) 2 D) 7 | Jawaban: B
3. Check-in hotel jam "half past two." Artinya pukul? | A) 02.30 B) 01.30 C) 02.15 D) 03.30 | Jawaban: A
4. Harga tiket "thirty-five thousand." Tulis angka. | A) 35.000 B) 13.000 C) 30.500 D) 53.000 | Jawaban: A
5. Gerbang tutup 30 menit sebelum 10.00. Jam tutup? | A) 10.30 B) 09.30 C) 09.00 D) 10.00 | Jawaban: B
6. Speaker bilang "I am allergic to shrimp." Alergi apa? | A) Shrimp B) Fish C) Egg D) Milk | Jawaban: A
7. Cuaca besok "cloudy but dry." Pilihan tepat? | A) Sunny B) Cloudy dry C) Heavy rain D) Storm | Jawaban: B
8. Bus jurusan "number fourteen." Nomor berapa? | A) 40 B) 14 C) 4 D) 44 | Jawaban: B
9. Meeting pindah "from Thursday to Friday, Room B." Kapan-di mana? | A) Thu A B) Fri B C) Fri A D) Thu B | Jawaban: B
10. Diskon "twenty percent for students." Berapa persen? | A) 12% B) 20% C) 25% D) 2% | Jawaban: B
11. Opini: "Admittedly pricey, nevertheless worth it." Sikap? | A) Reject B) Mixed-positive C) Angry D) Confused | Jawaban: B
12. Arah: "Past the mosque, on your left." Patokan apa? | A) Mosque B) Market C) School D) Bank | Jawaban: A
13. Kuliah "starts at quarter to nine." Jam berapa? | A) 09.15 B) 08.45 C) 09.45 D) 08.15 | Jawaban: B
14. "Mind your step, floor is wet." Artinya? | A) Hitung langkah B) Hati-hati langkah C) Lari cepat D) Duduk | Jawaban: B
15. "The train is delayed fifteen minutes." Berapa menit? | A) 5 B) 15 C) 50 D) 30 | Jawaban: B
16. Pembicara: "I look forward to meeting you." Nada? | A) Formal sopan B) Marah C) Santai gaul D) Sedih | Jawaban: A
17. "Take your medicine twice a day." Dosis? | A) 1x B) 2x C) 3x D) 4x | Jawaban: B
18. Berat bagasi "twenty-three kilos." Angka? | A) 23 B) 32 C) 13 D) 30 | Jawaban: A
19. "No entry beyond this point." Artinya? | A) Masuk gratis B) Dilarang masuk C) Pintu masuk D) Keluar | Jawaban: B
20. Ejaan "J-O-K-O." Nama? | A) Joko B) Jaka C) Joki D) Juko | Jawaban: A

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. A — Ejaan R-I-N-A = Rina. 2. B — Digit ke-3 dari 08127 adalah 1. 3. A — Half past two = 02.30. 4. A — Thirty-five = 35. 5. B — 10.00 minus 30 = 09.30. 6. A — Allergic to shrimp. 7. B — Cloudy dry sesuai audio. 8. B — Fourteen = 14 (bukan forty). 9. B — Moved to Friday Room B. 10. B — Twenty = 20. 11. B — Admittedly + nevertheless = campuran positif. 12. A — Patokan mosque. 13. B — Quarter to nine = 08.45. 14. B — Mind = hati-hati. 15. B — Fifteen = 15. 16. A — Look forward to = formal. 17. B — Twice = 2x. 18. A — Twenty-three = 23. 19. B — No entry = larangan. 20. A — J-O-K-O = Joko.

### IELTS Reading (Full Set)
- Durasi: 60 menit
- Jumlah soal: 20
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Baca tiap teks lalu jawab True/False/Not Given atau pilihan ganda; tulis bukti barisnya.

Soal:
1. "Tallinn ridership rose sharply." Klaim teks sama. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
2. "All cities banned cars in 2020." Teks hanya bahas 1 kota. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: C
3. "Heavy rain means hujan besar (big rain)." Kolokasi benar? | A) True (big) B) False (heavy) C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: B
4. Thesis editorial di awal teks. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
5. "Take medicine" kolokasi minum obat. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
6. Sinonim increased = rose. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
7. "The moon is unique so uses the." | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
8. "Unless you pay, speed drops." Unless = if not. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
9. "Plants die if overwatered." Zero conditional fakta. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
10. Penulis dukung "limited free routes." | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
11. Ide pokok terbaik untuk teks kos hemat? | A) Cook & bus pass save money B) Buy cars C) Sleep all day D) Ignore budget | Jawaban: A
12. "Not only cheap but fast" butuh inversion. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
13. "What I need is sleep" (bukan sleeping). | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
14. Referensi "they" di kalimat bus = buses. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
15. "Evidence suggests" menandakan hedging. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
16. Judul terbaik teks Nadiem? | A) Small capital wins B) Sleep longer C) Hate coffee D) Close stalls | Jawaban: A
17. "If I were you" formal untuk saran. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
18. "However" menandakan kontras. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
19. "For instance" menandakan contoh. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A
20. "In conclusion" di akhir teks. | A) True B) False C) Not Given D) Maybe | Jawaban: A

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. A — Ada bukti rose sharply. 2. C — Teks tak bahas semua kota. 3. B — Kolokasi heavy rain. 4. A — Thesis di awal. 5. A — Take medicine tetap. 6. A — Sinonim langsung. 7. A — Benda unik pakai the. 8. A — Unless = if not. 9. A — Fakta present. 10. A — Thesis limited routes. 11. A — Hemat = cook + bus. 12. A — Not only picu inversion. 13. A — What-cleft pakai benda. 14. A — They = buses. 15. A — Suggests = hedge. 16. A — Modal kecil + konsisten. 17. A — Were formal. 18. A — However = kontras. 19. A — For instance = contoh. 20. A — Penanda simpulan.

### IELTS Writing Task 2 (Prompt Set)
- Durasi: 40 menit
- Jumlah soal: 3 prompt
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Tulis minimal 250 kata, 4 paragraf, beri opini tegas dan contoh lokal.

Soal:
1. Some think free public transport solves traffic. Discuss both views and give your opinion. | Jawaban: Model thesis dukung terbatas + PEEL + counter + simpulan
2. Phones should be banned in schools. To what extent do you agree? | Jawaban: Setuju sebagian + aturan + contoh ambil-kembali
3. Sugar tax fights obesity but burdens poor. Discuss and give opinion. | Jawaban: Dukung pajak + subsidi + pilot + review

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. Model band 7+: intro parafrase, body1 manfaat, body2 biaya + counter pilot, tutup rekomendasi. 2. Partially agree: ganggu vs alat; solusi sita-kembali + panduan. 3. Limited tax + earmark + contoh Meksiko/Bogor + hedging.

### IELTS Speaking (Prompt Set)
- Durasi: 11-14 menit
- Jumlah soal: 3 set (Part 1+2+3)
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Part 1 jawab 2-3 kalimat, Part 2 bicara 1.5-2 menit, Part 3 beri alasan + contoh.

Soal:
1. Part 1 Hometown + Part 2 Describe a helpful person + Part 3 Community help. | Jawaban: Kerangka who-what-why-feeling + 2 alasan + contoh
2. Part 1 Food + Part 2 Describe a memorable trip + Part 3 Tourism impact. | Jawaban: Past tense runtut + mixed conditional + hedging
3. Part 1 Study/work + Part 2 Describe a goal + Part 3 Success & money. | Jawaban: Future perfect timeline + cleft + inversion sekali

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. Part 2: who (ibu), what (bantu PR), why (sabar), feeling (syukur). 2. Trip: where-when-who + had + would + pelajaran. 3. Goal: by 2027 will have + it is discipline that + never have I.

### TOEFL iBT Reading (Full Set)
- Durasi: 35 menit
- Jumlah soal: 20
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Baca 2 teks akademik lalu jawab pilihan ganda; maksimal 2 menit per soal.

Soal:
1. Kata "viable" (layak) konteks "plan is viable, however funds short." Arti? | A) Layak B) Mahal C) Gelap D) Basah | Jawaban: A
2. "Refers to" menandakan? | A) Definisi B) Hujan C) Kemarin D) Keras | Jawaban: A
3. "Heavy rain delayed rollout." Rollout = ? | A) Launch B) Fire C) Delay D) Cancel | Jawaban: A
4. "Bottom line is sales." Artinya? | A) Inti B) Garis C) Rugi D) Tanda | Jawaban: A
5. "It is discipline that wins." Pola? | A) Cleft B) Passive C) Gerund D) Tag | Jawaban: A
6. "Never have I seen this." Pola? | A) Inversion B) Active C) Imperative D) Plural | Jawaban: A
7. "If I'd saved, I'd own now." Pola? | A) Mixed B) Zero C) Simple D) Future | Jawaban: A
8. "She must have forgotten." Fungsi? | A) Dugaan lampau B) Perintah C) Larangan D) Janji | Jawaban: A
9. "I had my hair cut." Artinya? | A) Ke barber B) Potong sendiri C) Beli rambut D) Cukur kumis | Jawaban: A
10. "Take medicine" bukan "drink." Karena? | A) Kolokasi B) Sopan C) Acak D) Murah | Jawaban: A
11. "Admittedly..., nevertheless..." Fungsi? | A) Counter-argument B) Curhat C) Kabur D) Maki | Jawaban: A
12. "Teens tend to scroll." Nada? | A) Hedged B) Mutlak C) Marah D) Gaul | Jawaban: A
13. "Decision" dari decide adalah? | A) Nominalisasi B) Verb C) Adverb D) Tag | Jawaban: A
14. "Such a day!" Such karena? | A) Paket benda B) Sifat saja C) Verb D) Angka | Jawaban: A
15. "Despite rain, we left." Despite + ? | A) Benda B) Klausa C) Will D) To | Jawaban: A
16. "Although it rained, we left." Although + ? | A) Klausa B) Benda C) Will D) To | Jawaban: A
17. "I look forward to meeting." To + ? | A) V-ing B) V1 C) Will D) To | Jawaban: A
18. "There seems to be an error." Fungsi? | A) Hedging eksistensial B) Perintah C) Tanya D) Marah | Jawaban: A
19. "It is essential he be here." Be karena? | A) Subjunctive B) Typo C) Past D) Plural | Jawaban: A
20. "Walking home, I saw sunset." Subjek? | A) Sama (I) B) Beda C) Hujan D) Jalan | Jawaban: A

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. A — Viable = layak (kontras however dana). 2. A — Refers to = definisi. 3. A — Rollout = launch. 4. A — Bottom line = inti. 5. A — It is...that = cleft. 6. A — Never depan = inversion. 7. A — Had + would kini = mixed. 8. A — Must have = dugaan lampau. 9. A — Causative = jasa orang. 10. A — Kolokasi tetap take. 11. A — Akui-tikung = counter. 12. A — Tend to = hedge. 13. A — Decide→decision nominal. 14. A — Such + benda. 15. A — Despite + benda. 16. A — Although + klausa. 17. A — Forward to + -ing. 18. A — There seem = hedge ada. 19. A — Subjunctive polos. 20. A — V-ing subjek sama I.

### TOEFL iBT Listening (Full Set)
- Durasi: 36 menit
- Jumlah soal: 20
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Dengar percakapan/kuliah lalu jawab ide pokok, detail, dan inferensi nada.

Soal:
1. Ide pokok pengumuman gerbang? | A) Tutup 30 mnt sblm B) Buka telat C) Gratis D) Pindah kota | Jawaban: A
2. Sikap "Regrettably, enforcement weak."? | A) Critical B) Happy C) Neutral D) Excited | Jawaban: A
3. "Would you mind waiting?" Minta? | A) Tunggu B) Pergi C) Bayar D) Makan | Jawaban: A
4. "Are we still on for Friday?" Tanya? | A) Konfirmasi jadi B) Batal C) Lupa D) Marah | Jawaban: A
5. Kuliah: "Evidence suggests..." Nada? | A) Hedged akademik B) Mutlak C) Gaul D) Marah | Jawaban: A
6. "So do I" setelah "I love soto." Artinya? | A) Setuju suka B) Benci C) Bingung D) Lapar | Jawaban: A
7. "Turn it off" (TV). Objek di? | A) Tengah B) Belakang C) Depan D) Hilang | Jawaban: A
8. "Look after mom." Arti? | A) Rawat B) Lihat C) Tinggal D) Jual | Jawaban: A
9. "I wish it would stop raining." Fungsi? | A) Komplain harap B) Fakta C) Janji D) Doa makan | Jawaban: A
10. "By Friday I'll have finished." Kapan kelar? | A) Sblm Jumat B) Jumat lalu C) Tak pasti D) Batal | Jawaban: A
11. "While I was cooking, he came." While + ? | A) Continuous B) Simple C) Will D) Perfect | Jawaban: A
12. "If you heat ice, it melts." Jenis? | A) Zero B) Third C) Mixed D) Wish | Jawaban: A
13. "You're from Solo, right?" Fungsi right? | A) Cek paham B) Marah C) Hitung D) Eja | Jawaban: A
14. "Anyway, back to plan." Fungsi? | A) Balik topik B) Pamitan C) Buka D) Tutup total | Jawaban: A
15. "Well, let me think..." Fungsi? | A) Beli waktu B) Menyerah C) Marah D) Tidur | Jawaban: A
16. Angka "forty vs fourteen." Bedakan via? | A) Tekanan B) Warna C) Rasa D) Bau | Jawaban: A
17. "Half past seven" = ? | A) 07.30 B) 06.30 C) 07.15 D) 07.45 | Jawaban: A
18. "I have been waiting since noon." Sejak? | A) Siang B) Pagi C) Malam D) Kemarin | Jawaban: A
19. "Had we left sooner, we'd be home." Hasil? | A) Kini B) Lampau C) Besok D) Batal | Jawaban: A
20. Tujuan "Let's touch base Monday."? | A) Kontak singkat B) Putus C) Libur D) Pindah | Jawaban: A

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. A — 30 sebelum = inti. 2. A — Regrettably = kritis. 3. A — Mind + -ing = minta tunggu. 4. A — Still on = konfirmasi. 5. A — Suggests = hedge. 6. A — So do I = setuju. 7. A — Pronoun tengah separable. 8. A — Look after = rawat. 9. A — Wish would = komplain. 10. A — By = sebelum batas. 11. A — While + continuous. 12. A — Fakta = zero. 13. A — Right = cek. 14. A — Anyway = balik. 15. A — Well = filler sopan. 16. A — Tekanan -ty/-teen. 17. A — Half past = .30. 18. A — Since noon = siang. 19. A — Would kini = mixed. 20. A — Touch base = kontak.

### TOEFL iBT Writing (Prompt Set)
- Durasi: 29 menit
- Jumlah soal: 3 prompt (1 Integrated + 2 Discussion)
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Integrated 150-225 kata hubungkan baca vs dengar; Discussion 100+ kata beri alasan + contoh.

Soal:
1. Integrated: Baca pro bus gratis vs Dengar bantah biaya. Hubungan? Tulis 180 kata. | Jawaban: Lecture contradicts: biaya, misuse, pilot
2. Discussion: Prof: Should phones be collected in class? Reply + agree/disagree + reason. | Jawaban: Agree + fokus + contoh sita-kembali
3. Discussion: Tax sugary drinks fair? Give stance + 2 reasons + local example. | Jawaban: Fair terbatas + kesehatan + subsidi + Meksiko/Bogor

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. Contradicts: reading pro, lecture bantah 3 poin (however/fails). 2. Agree: distract, tool if guided, system fair. 3. Stance jelas + PEEL + hedge + contoh lokal.

### TOEIC Listening (Full Set)
- Durasi: 45 menit
- Jumlah soal: 20
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Lihat foto/dengar lalu pilih respons tercepat; jangan kosongkan.

Soal:
1. Foto: Man ___ documents. (aksi berlangsung) | A) is reading B) reads C) read D) reading | Jawaban: A
2. "How much is this shirt?" Jawab harga. | A) 120k B) Hello C) Blue D) Cotton | Jawaban: A
3. "Where is the station?" Jawab arah. | A) Past mosque B) Tasty C) Cheap D) Late | Jawaban: A
4. "When does the train leave?" | A) At 6.30 B) Big C) Yes D) Spicy | Jawaban: A
5. "Who looks after the baby?" | A) My sister B) Blue C) 5pm D) Rice | Jawaban: A
6. "Please find ___ the file." | A) attached B) attach C) attaching D) attachment do | Jawaban: A
7. "We will cut ___ 15%." | A) back on B) up with C) into D) off it wrong | Jawaban: A
8. "Let's ___ base Friday." | A) touch B) hit C) kick D) hug | Jawaban: A
9. "The shipment will ___ Monday." | A) roll out B) roll in C) roll up D) roll over | Jawaban: A
10. "Are we on the ___ page?" | A) same B) some C) seem D) sum | Jawaban: A
11. Talk: Meeting moved Thu→Fri Room B. Kapan? | A) Friday B B) Thursday A C) Monday C D) Sunday D | Jawaban: A
12. Talk: Gate closes 30 before 10. Jam? | A) 09.30 B) 10.30 C) 09.00 D) 10.00 | Jawaban: A
13. "Mind the step." Artinya? | A) Hati-hati B) Hitung C) Lari D) Duduk | Jawaban: A
14. "No smoking here." Artinya? | A) Dilarang B) Boleh C) Gratis D) Tutup | Jawaban: A
15. Ejaan "thirty vs thirteen." Tekanan? | A) Depan vs belakang B) Sama C) Hilang D) Acak | Jawaban: A
16. "Would you like some tea?" (tawaran) Some karena? | A) Sopan B) Salah C) Kasar D) Acak | Jawaban: A
17. "I have an appointment ___ 3 ___ Friday." | A) at/on B) in/at C) on/in D) to/to | Jawaban: A
18. "Get ___ at the next stop." (turun bus) | A) off B) in C) on D) into | Jawaban: A
19. "I am used to ___ early." | A) waking B) wake C) wakes D) waked | Jawaban: A
20. "Have you finished ___?" | A) yet B) already now C) just ever D) ever yet now | Jawaban: A

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. A — Foto berlangsung = continuous. 2. A — How much = harga. 3. A — Where = arah. 4. A — When = waktu. 5. A — Who = orang. 6. A — Kolokasi find attached. 7. A — Cut back on = pangkas. 8. A — Touch base = kontak. 9. A — Roll out = luncur. 10. A — Same page = sepaham. 11. A — Moved to Fri B. 12. A — 09.30. 13. A — Mind = hati-hati. 14. A — No + V-ing = larang. 15. A — -ty depan, -teen belakang. 16. A — Tawaran pakai some. 17. A — At jam, on hari. 18. A — Get off bus. 19. A — Used to + -ing. 20. A — Yet di tanya akhir.

### TOEIC Reading (Full Set)
- Durasi: 75 menit
- Jumlah soal: 20
- Instruksi pengerjaan: Kerjakan Part 5 dulu (grammar cepat) lalu Part 7 scanning; isi semua.

Soal:
1. She ___ to work by bus. | A) goes B) go C) going D) gone | Jawaban: A
2. There ___ much traffic today. | A) is B) are C) have D) has | Jawaban: A
3. Do you have ___ questions? | A) any B) some C) a D) an | Jawaban: A
4. Meeting is ___ Monday ___ 9. | A) on/at B) in/on C) at/in D) to/to | Jawaban: A
5. I enjoy ___ coffee. | A) drinking B) drink C) to drink D) drinks | Jawaban: A
6. I have worked here ___ 2019. | A) since B) for C) ago D) at | Jawaban: A
7. If it rains, we ___ home. | A) will stay B) stay C) stayed D) staying | Jawaban: A
8. Batik ___ in Solo. | A) is made B) makes C) made D) making | Jawaban: A
9. He said he ___ busy. | A) was B) is C) will be D) be | Jawaban: A
10. Friend ___ helps me is kind. | A) who B) which C) whose D) when | Jawaban: A
11. Please ___ off the lights. | A) turn B) put C) look D) run | Jawaban: A
12. Cheap. ___, risky. (kontras) | A) However B) Moreover C) Therefore D) Firstly | Jawaban: A
13. If I had time, I ___ learn drums. | A) would B) will C) can D) shall | Jawaban: A
14. She ___ be home, lights on. | A) must B) mustn't C) shouldn't D) won't | Jawaban: A
15. I had my laptop ___. | A) repaired B) repair C) repairing D) to repair | Jawaban: A
16. Email: "Please confirm by Friday." Minta apa? | A) Konfirmasi Jumat B) Batal C) Libur D) Bayar tunai | Jawaban: A
17. Memo: "Attached revised proposal." Lampiran apa? | A) Proposal revisi B) Foto C) Lagu D) Tiket | Jawaban: A
18. Sign: "Mind your step." Artinya? | A) Hati-hati B) Hitung C) Duduk D) Lari | Jawaban: A
19. "Heavy ___ delayed match." | A) rain B) big C) large D) strong | Jawaban: A
20. "___ honest man helped." | A) An B) A C) The D) - | Jawaban: A

Kunci jawaban & pembahasan singkat per nomor:
1. A — She + -s. 2. A — Traffic uncountable is. 3. A — Tanya any. 4. A — On hari, at jam. 5. A — Enjoy + -ing. 6. A — Since titik. 7. A — Cond1 will. 8. A — Passive is + V3. 9. A — Reported was. 10. A — Orang who. 11. A — Turn off lampu. 12. A — Kontras however. 13. A — Khayal would. 14. A — Bukti must. 15. A — Causative V3. 16. A — Confirm = konfirmasi. 17. A — Attached = lampiran. 18. A — Mind = hati-hati. 19. A — Heavy rain kolokasi. 20. A — Honest h bisu an.
